**[00:13:14.670] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000221)  
Thank you very much, Vice Chair Ordonez.

**[00:13:19.210] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000222)  
As now we will be

**[00:13:21.790] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000223)  
starting off our examination

**[00:13:23.550] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000224)  
of the proposed budget

**[00:13:26.150] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000225)  
of the Department of Social

**[00:13:27.750] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000226)  
Welfare and Development,

**[00:13:29.550] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000227)  
we now recognize that

**[00:13:31.870] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000228)  
the honorable Secretary Rex Gatchalian.

**[00:13:34.150] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000229)  
Secretary, you may now proceed

**[00:13:36.830] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000230)  
with your presentation.

**[00:13:39.670] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000231)  
Good morning, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:13:42.630] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000232)  
Chairman Nava, to the honorable members

**[00:13:45.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000233)  
of the subcommittee on populations,

**[00:13:46.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000234)  
thank you for giving us

**[00:13:48.030] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000235)  
the opportunity to present to you

**[00:13:49.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000236)  
next year's DSWD budget.

**[00:13:52.430] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000237)  
Let me move onto the first slide.

**[00:13:55.430] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000238)  
The Department of Social Welfare

**[00:13:56.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000239)  
and Development including

**[00:13:58.230] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000240)  
its attached and supervised agencies,

**[00:14:00.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000241)  
is

**[00:14:01.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000242)  
honored to appear before the House of Representatives as part of our accountability for the budget appropriated by Congress. In the next slides, we will present our annual budget utilization performance with some details on how we made good use of the allotments and then we will present the proposed budget for 2027.

**[00:14:19.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000243)  
The DSWD's budget increased over the years and for this, we are thankful for the support of Congress. In turn, we made sure we put premium on the trust and the channeling our resources towards people-centered investments.

**[00:14:35.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000244)  
First, human capital development. Reducing and ultimately ending poverty begins with investing in people's capabilities, particularly children, families, senior citizens, persons with disabilities, and other vulnerable sectors.

**[00:14:49.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000245)  
We continue to innovate developmental interventions to improve the implementation of the different programs of the DSWD. Second, community-driven development. We continue to engage

**[00:15:00.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000246)  
the people in the farthest communities as partners in identifying needs, designing solutions and

**[00:15:06.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000247)  
building resiliency. And third, rapid, adequate and sustained disaster response. This is needed

**[00:15:11.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000248)  
to ensure that the gains in human capital development, particularly in communities of

**[00:15:16.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000249)  
vulnerable groups, are not reversed by the severity of disasters. The instruction of the

**[00:15:22.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000250)  
president is clear. The government should be there to respond immediately and remain present for as

**[00:15:29.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000251)  
long as families need support to recover. The next slides will show our report to Congress on

**[00:15:34.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000252)  
how we spent our budget. For our financial performance for fiscal year 2025, the DBM

**[00:15:39.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000253)  
released approximately $265 billion to the DSWD, both for our agency budget and releases from other

**[00:15:48.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000254)  
fund sources. We are pleased to report that the DSWD achieved a near 100% utilization rate as of

**[00:15:55.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000255)  
July 2026. For fiscal year

**[00:15:58.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000256)  
2026,

**[00:16:00.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000258)  
The DSWD has so far utilized 70% of the agency-specific budget of $262.9 billion. For the Calamity Fund and

**[00:16:10.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000259)  
Quick Response Fund replenishments, 78% of the $9.6 billion released so far has already been utilized.

**[00:16:19.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000260)  
The marching order to all DSWD programs is simple. Work harder, work faster and work smarter.

**[00:16:26.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000261)  
We should not waste resources, and every peso

**[00:16:28.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000262)  
appropriated to the DSWD

**[00:16:31.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000263)  
is a lifeline for beneficiaries in need. By directing our budget towards people centers investments,

**[00:16:39.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000264)  
we bring our mandate to life, protect people in crises so that it would not become a long-term

**[00:16:44.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000265)  
setback, promote human development to improve people's capabilities and opportunities,

**[00:16:49.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000266)  
Prevent

**[00:16:50.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000267)  
abuse and exploitation of people in

**[00:16:53.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000268)  
vulnerable situations. And

**[00:16:55.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000269)  
transform people's lives by addressing structural barriers

**[00:16:58.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000270)  
that reproduce poverty and exclusion. So in DSWD, these four roles are interconnected

**[00:17:06.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000271)  
and social protection is the bridge, not the destination. The goal is not simply to help

**[00:17:12.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000272)  
people survive today, but government must work to give them a fairer chance to move forward.

**[00:17:19.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000273)  
With this implementation track record as a backdrop, we now present the DSWD's

**[00:17:25.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000274)  
Families Proposed Budget for Fiscal Year 2027. The proposed budget reflects our commitment to

**[00:17:31.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000275)  
building a bridge to self-sufficiency for every vulnerable client being served by the DSWD and

**[00:17:37.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000276)  
its attached and supervised agencies. It is designed to sustain critical social protection

**[00:17:43.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000277)  
and social welfare interventions while strengthening the systems through assistance

**[00:17:48.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000278)  
that is targeted, delivered, monitored, and evaluated. For Fiscal Year 2027,

**[00:17:55.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000279)  
the DSWD has a total budget of $241.2 billion under the National Expenditure Program.

**[00:18:01.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000280)  
Of this amount, $235.7 billion is allocated to the DSWD Office of the Secretary.

**[00:18:08.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000281)  
For attached agencies, the proposed budgets are as follows. $136.3 million for the Council of

**[00:18:14.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000282)  
the Welfare of Children, $91.6 million for the National Council on Disability Affairs,

**[00:18:20.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000283)  
$126.3 million for the Juvenile Justice and Welfare Council,

**[00:18:24.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000284)  
$811.8

**[00:18:25.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000286)  
million for the National Authority for Child Care.

**[00:18:29.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000287)  
For the supervised agencies, the proposed budgets are $3.8 billion for the National Commission on

**[00:18:35.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000288)  
Senior Citizens, $359.9 million for the National Anti-Poverty Commission, and $233.4 million for

**[00:18:43.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000289)  
the Presidential Commission for the Urban Poor. Compared with Fiscal Year 2026,

**[00:18:48.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000290)  
General Appropriations Act, the proposed budget under the Fiscal Year 2027 NEP for the DSWD includes

**[00:18:55.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000291)  
its attached and supervised agencies, reflects lower levels, with the exception of NACC and NCSC,

**[00:19:03.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000292)  
whose budget increases are on their personnel services, because of the new positions created

**[00:19:08.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000293)  
and or filled up. It is also important to note that pursuant to Executive Order 123 issued on

**[00:19:14.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000294)  
August 18, 2026, the NAPC and the PCUP have been placed under the administrative supervision of

**[00:19:22.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000295)  
the Office of the President. Accordingly, while

**[00:19:25.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000296)  
their Fiscal Year 2027 budget figures are reflected in this

**[00:19:29.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000297)  
presentation, these two agencies are no longer under the administrative supervision of DSWD.

**[00:19:35.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000298)  
Looking at the budget by expenditure classification, the bulk of the budget remains

**[00:19:40.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000299)  
primarily directed towards delivery of programs and services to our beneficiaries. Generally,

**[00:19:45.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000300)  
the operating budgets of all the offices for 2027 are lower than 2026. As mentioned,

**[00:19:51.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000301)  
the increases pertain to the filling up of plantille position, which is very important for the

**[00:19:55.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000302)  
positions authorized by the DBM. Except for the DSWD, which was granted capital outlay for five

**[00:20:01.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000303)  
centers and residential care facilities in year three implementation of a learning institute in

**[00:20:06.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000304)  
Davao and a warehouse in Iliilo, the DSWD has no capital outlay for 2027. We now proceed to the

**[00:20:14.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000305)  
major PAPs. We will provide you with their performance and accomplishments and report to

**[00:20:20.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000306)  
the members of this August subcommittee what we're doing to further improve service delivery.

**[00:20:25.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000307)  
For the protective services for individuals and families in different circumstances,

**[00:20:29.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000308)  
the bulk of the budget pertains to the assistance to individuals and families in crisis situation

**[00:20:34.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000309)  
or AICs. Although there are also community-based protective social welfare programs that we

**[00:20:43.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000310)  
implement to different vulnerable and marginalized sectors. For fiscal year 2025, we have already

**[00:20:49.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000311)  
utilized 100 percent of the budget which enabled the program to assist more than 9.4 million

**[00:20:54.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000312)  
beneficiaries for the year. For 2026, budget utilization is on track at almost 70 percent

**[00:21:01.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000313)  
of the allotment utilizing all its cash programming in real time, serving 4.24 million beneficiaries so

**[00:21:10.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000314)  
far. For fiscal year 2027, the proposed allocation is 33.3 billion consisting of 29.4 billion in

**[00:21:18.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000315)  
subsidies. Given the demand-driven nature of the program, the NEP amount may not be

**[00:21:24.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000316)  
enough to achieve the same level of performance for the past two years. We have informed our

**[00:21:30.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000317)  
budget sponsor about this matter and we have included also in our letter to the honorable

**[00:21:34.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000318)  
chair of the appropriations committee. Mr. Chair, in a bit, when we get into the discussions,

**[00:21:39.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000319)  
we will show you a presentation that will show the adverse effect of this budget decline for the

**[00:21:46.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000320)  
assistance to individuals in crisis situation and the potential displacements of beneficiaries for

**[00:21:52.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000321)  
medical and other social welfare programs.

**[00:21:54.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000322)  
Your honors, it is given that AICS continues to fulfill its core mandate by providing a

**[00:22:00.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000323)  
comprehensive menu of assistance to individuals and families in crisis situations. But beyond

**[00:22:06.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000324)  
individual emergencies, AICS now serves as our primary tool for adaptive and shock responsive

**[00:22:13.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000325)  
social protection, allowing us to quickly act in cases of shocks which impact groups,

**[00:22:18.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000326)  
families or communities at risk. In response to the national energy emergency declared

**[00:22:23.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000327)  
by the president,

**[00:22:24.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000328)  
AICS is at the forefront and is in the front and center of the whole of government intervention under the Uplift program.

**[00:22:32.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000329)  
Through AICS, the government was able to put into motion interventions using three mechanisms under the adaptive and shock-responsive social protection.

**[00:22:40.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000330)  
When the government needed to question impact of the Middle East crisis to our transport sector,

**[00:22:46.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000331)  
the DSWD rolled out the cash relief assistance to drivers affected by the sudden increase in fuel prices as an emergency scheme.

**[00:22:54.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000332)  
Under this mechanism, we adopted a group-based assessment supported through identification, validation, and endorsement by relevant authorities or partner agencies.

**[00:23:05.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000333)  
This assistance helped 1.839 million transport workers nationwide.

**[00:23:11.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000334)  
A rapid assessment by the SWS Commission by the Australia Spring Programme

**[00:23:17.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000335)  
confirms that our cash relief assistance delivered immediate, decisive, impactful, and overwhelming

**[00:23:22.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000336)  
with

**[00:23:24.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000337)  
an overwhelming 96% of transport workers reporting that the 5,000 grant was operationally crucial in keeping them on the road,

**[00:23:33.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000338)  
with 80% using the aid to cover essential household food expenses during the emergency.

**[00:23:39.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000339)  
Then the Uplift Committee chaired by the President decided to have a strategic pivot from individual-based to household-based assistance.

**[00:23:46.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000340)  
Through the Uplift under AICS, vertical expansion is implemented by top-up grants to help compensate for the diminished value of the

**[00:23:54.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000341)  
grants or subsidy being received by

**[00:23:56.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000342)  
existing beneficiaries of social protection programs. Through horizontal expansion, we extended financial assistance to households that were not previously covered by

**[00:24:04.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000343)  
existing programs but became vulnerable because of the shock. To accomplish this,

**[00:24:10.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000344)  
we used the CBMS data to locate the poor, near-poor,

**[00:24:15.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000345)  
and low-income and provide monthly assistance to them.

**[00:24:19.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000346)  
As of today, we have provided

**[00:24:21.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000347)  
Uplift assistance to

**[00:24:22.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000348)  
5.75

**[00:24:23.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000349)  
million households.

**[00:24:25.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000350)  
We now come to the Pantawid Pamilyang Pilipino Program, or 4Ps, which remains

**[00:24:29.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000351)  
the country's flagship investment in human capital development. For fiscal year 2025, the program had

**[00:24:35.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000352)  
an allotment of 63.7 billion, of which 63.4 billion, or 99.53 percent, was utilized. We were able to pay

**[00:24:44.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000353)  
the conditional cash grants of 3.9 million beneficiaries, which are due for payments that

**[00:24:49.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000354)  
year. For fiscal year 2026, the program has an allotment of 112.9 billion, including 98.3 billion

**[00:24:57.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000355)  
for grants, since the conditional grants are paid according to scheduled pay periods. We are on track

**[00:25:03.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000356)  
in terms of obligation and disbursement. For fiscal year 2027, the 4Ps budget is at 99.1 billion,

**[00:25:10.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000357)  
of which 81.3 billion will be for conditional cash grants and 3.5 million for 3.5 million

**[00:25:17.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000358)  
beneficiaries. The 3.5 million

**[00:25:19.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000359)  
total

**[00:25:19.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000360)  
target reflects our continuing effort to ensure that 4Ps resources are focused on poor and near

**[00:25:25.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000361)  
poor households identified through the latest available poverty data, while maintaining the

**[00:30:24.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000417)

We met with PSA yesterday to bring to their attention as well that the DSWD has exhausted

**[00:30:38.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000420)  
the list of poor and near-poor, which is at 10% above the poverty threshold.

It is high time to revisit the statistical definition of the near-poor so that it is

**[00:30:49.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000422)  
not only limited to 10% above the poverty threshold, but up to 25% above the poverty

**[00:30:55.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000423)  
threshold.

**[00:30:56.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000424)  
Because these are households with the same characteristics.

**[00:30:59.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000425)  
The government's hope for the future of all four-piece beneficiaries is clear.

**[00:31:07.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000426)  
Help them while they are in the program and provide them with crutches when they achieve

**[00:31:11.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000427)  
self-sufficiency through education, either tertiary or technical vocation that will give

**[00:31:17.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000428)

**[00:31:22.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000429)  
With this recent enactment of Republic Act No. 12325, tertiary education subsidies now

**[00:31:28.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000430)  
prioritizes four-piece beneficiaries, guaranteeing our youth top-tier access to college and vocational

**[00:31:34.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000431)  
degrees.

**[00:31:37.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000432)  
On disaster response, QRF provides the DSWD the flexibility to immediately respond and

**[00:31:42.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000433)  
provide timely assistance before, during, and after emergencies.

**[00:31:46.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000434)  
Our accomplishments on QRF use has been encouraging.

**[00:31:52.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000435)  
We would like the families affected by disaster to feel that government is present during

**[00:31:56.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000436)  
the times that they need us.

**[00:31:57.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000437)  
We have a range of assistance.

**[00:31:58.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000438)  
We have already utilized 100% of the 2025 allotment and replenishments.

**[00:32:02.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000439)  
For 2026, we have utilized 10.4 billion of the 12.6 billion released from QRF.

**[00:32:09.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000440)  
Our strategy on disaster response is simple.

**[00:32:12.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000441)  
Try to be omnipresent and always armed with adequate relief on food and non-food items.

**[00:32:18.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000442)  
We now have a nationwide network of 1,031 warehouses with pre-positioned goods with

**[00:32:24.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000443)  
a storage capacity of more than 5.1 million family-focused.

**[00:32:28.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000444)  
We have a total of 3.4 million food boxes and a current stockpile of 3.4 million FFPs

**[00:32:32.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000445)  
as of September 4, 2023.

**[00:32:35.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000446)  
We have also modernized production in our disaster resource center, allowing us to produce

**[00:32:39.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000447)  
up to 63,000 FFPs per 8-hour shift and replenish stocks immediately when needed.

**[00:32:45.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000448)  
In 2025 alone, we produce 7.2 family food boxes nationwide.

**[00:32:50.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000449)  
These are complemented by emergency cash transfers and cash for work providing immediate financial

**[00:32:55.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000450)  
support while helping affected families recover and rebuild

**[00:32:58.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000452)  
their livelihoods. In 2025, 1.3 million families received financial relief through emergency cash transfers

**[00:33:05.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000453)  
and cash for work.

**[00:33:07.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000454)  
Another major investment is social pension for indigent senior citizens.

**[00:33:13.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000455)  
For elderly beneficiaries, social pension provides an important layer of income security.

**[00:33:18.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000456)  
For 2025, we have served 4.11 million beneficiaries for 2026.

**[00:33:25.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000457)  
Our utilization stands at 77.74.

**[00:33:27.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000458)  
Because we are in the progress process of enrolling new beneficiaries, the goal is to

**[00:33:32.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000459)  
serve as many older persons using our allotment of 51 billion pesos.

**[00:33:37.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000460)  
For 2027, our NEP is at 51.6 billion, of which 50.8 billion is allocated for subsidies.

**[00:33:45.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000461)  
Based on available CBMS data, we estimate that at least 550,000 older persons remain

**[00:33:52.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000462)  
unserved.

**[00:33:54.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000463)  
We therefore appeal to this honorable

**[00:33:55.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000465)  
August body to cover these older persons under our program next year.

**[00:34:00.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000466)  
For the social pension program, we made a strategic shift to make implementation simpler,

**[00:34:05.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000467)  
fairer and more responsive to older persons.

**[00:34:09.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000468)  
This supports our goal of achieving the progressive realization of universal social pension.

**[00:34:13.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000469)  
First, we remove administrative barriers.

**[00:34:16.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000470)  
We recognize that older persons already face multiple vulnerabilities because of age.

**[00:34:21.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000471)  
So we no longer require them to provide that they are frail or sickly to qualify.

**[00:34:26.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000472)  
issued a memorandum circular where eligibility centers on a simple proof of age wherever they

**[00:34:32.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000473)  
may reside with enrollment prioritized strictly by age patanda requirement your mr chair second

**[00:34:40.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000474)  
we shifted to psa data driven targeting there is no need to apply as a rule when your name is in

**[00:34:45.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000475)  
the cbms data upon confirmation from sss gsis p vow that you are not a pensioner you will be

**[00:34:53.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000476)  
located by our field personnel to validate eligibility under our program guidelines

**[00:34:58.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000477)  
this is our default process but we found many eligible older persons were still not

**[00:35:04.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000478)  
we're still being missed so under a new policy we introduce an on-demand application including

**[00:35:10.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000479)  
referrals from lgus for those 70 years old and above if you belong to that age category and you

**[00:35:16.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000480)  
are not in the cbms data we will process your application by checking with psa if you are in

**[00:35:21.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000481)  
their civil registry

**[00:35:23.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000482)  
meaning the birth registry and at the same time not in the death registry making the process

**[00:35:28.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000483)  
easier for older persons this presupposes as well cross-checking that you are not an active

**[00:35:34.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000484)  
pensioner and finally looking beyond the traditional welfare the swd's leading regional

**[00:35:40.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000485)  
efforts across asean to unlocking the potential of the silver economy recognizing older persons

**[00:35:46.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000486)  
not only as beneficiaries but also contributors to the community and the economy social pen

**[00:35:52.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000487)  
protection must not stop

**[00:35:53.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000488)  
providing assistance through the sustainable livelihood program we help families and

**[00:35:57.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000489)  
communities develop opportunities to generate income and become economically self-reliant

**[00:36:01.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000490)  
the program represents an important bridge between social protection and economic empowerment

**[00:36:06.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000491)  
for fiscal year 2025 slp utilize essentially the full allocation with 100 percent of the 6.2

**[00:36:14.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000492)  
billion obligated in 98.71 percent disburse assisting 275 thousand 478 poor family households

**[00:36:23.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000493)  
or 100.71 percent of its target for fiscal year 2026 4.5 billion or 84.29 has been obligated as of

**[00:36:33.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000494)  
july with 186 736 poor household assisted for fiscal year 2027 the nep is at 5.1 billion including 3.1

**[00:36:43.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000495)  
billion in subsidies to assist 251 449 poor household as will be shown in the next slide

**[00:36:53.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000496)  
its financial performance in client reach more import more than two years ago we revamped the

**[00:37:00.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000497)  
implementation of the slp to ensure that livelihood assistance does not remain one of in a one-off

**[00:37:05.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000498)  
engagement but rather a sustained pathway toward livelihood sustainability one of the key shifts

**[00:37:11.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000499)  
through the pagsibol program where we are looking at livelihood development over a longer horizon

**[00:37:16.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000500)  
with a sustainability plan instead of providing assistance and ending the engagement after the

**[00:37:21.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000501)  
initial intervention this is an important step in achieving a sustainable livelihood program because

**[00:37:23.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000502)  
This allows us to ensure that government resources are invested in enterprises that have potential

**[00:37:29.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000503)  
to grow and become viable sources of income.

**[00:37:33.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000504)  
We are also broadening the way that we support beneficiaries.

**[00:37:36.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000505)  
We are not limited to traditional approach of producing and promoting SLP products.

**[00:37:41.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000506)  
We are strengthening marketing linkages and helping beneficiaries become more competitive

**[00:37:46.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000507)  
through new channels, including digital and online selling platforms such as TikTok.

**[00:37:51.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000508)  
We have seen this approach work particularly for viable enterprises, including larger and

**[00:37:56.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000509)  
more innovative livelihood opportunities.

**[00:37:59.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000510)  
The objective is to move SLP from one-time livelihood assistance to sustained enterprise

**[00:38:05.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000511)  
development with government investment linked to viability, market access, and the capacity

**[00:38:10.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000512)  
of beneficiaries to compete.

**[00:38:13.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000513)  
The department also continues to provide residential and center-based services for individuals

**[00:38:17.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000514)  
who require more intense and sustained care.

**[00:38:21.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000515)  
This includes children, older persons, people with disabilities, and other individuals

**[00:38:25.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000516)  
whose circumstances require specialized intervention.

**[00:38:28.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000517)  
For fiscal year 2025, we served 9,620 clients, exceeding a target by 11.34%, while in fiscal

**[00:38:36.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000518)  
year 2026, 8,067 clients have already been served as of this reporting period.

**[00:38:43.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000519)  
For fiscal year 2027, we are proposing 3.3 billion to continue these specialized services

**[00:38:48.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000520)  
with a target of 8,640 clients.

**[00:38:51.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000521)  
Our challenge is to ensure that our facilities remain safe, appropriate, accessible, and

**[00:38:55.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000522)  
capable of delivering quality social welfare services.

**[00:39:00.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000523)  
While we continue to provide socialized care to clients, we also need to address the capacity

**[00:39:04.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000524)  
and infrastructure constraints of our residential care and center-based facilities.

**[00:39:09.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000525)  
At present, DSWD manages 77 facilities.

**[00:39:13.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000526)  
Of these, 11 facilities, or 14.3%, were established 50 years ago.

**[00:39:19.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000527)  
Or earlier.

**[00:39:21.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000529)  
Or earlier.

**[00:39:22.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000530)  
While another 35 facilities are already 30 to 49 years old.

**[00:39:26.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000531)  
This highlights the need to continuously invest in the facilities where our clients receive

**[00:39:30.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000532)  
care.

**[00:39:31.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000533)  
We are therefore pursuing facilities upgrading and service expansion.

**[00:39:36.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000534)  
At the same time, we are implementing a cost-of-care subsidy for registered licensed and accredited

**[00:39:41.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000535)  
private residential-based social welfare agencies.

**[00:39:46.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000536)  
This allows us to expand the capacity of the social welfare system by working with qualified

**[00:39:50.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000537)  
partners while continuing to support the regular upkeep and improvement of DSWD facilities.

**[00:39:57.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000538)  
The Department is also implementing innovative programs implemented under the President's

**[00:40:04.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000539)  
Administration designed to address specific dimensions of poverty and vulnerability.

**[00:40:10.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000540)  
These are the Walang Gutom 2027 Program, the Tarabasa Tutoring Program, and the Pag-Abot

**[00:40:14.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000541)  
Program.

**[00:40:15.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000542)  
While each addresses a different dimension of poverty and vulnerability, they share a

**[00:40:19.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000543)  
common objective.

**[00:40:20.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000544)  
Providing more responsive and innovative innovations to address the specific needs

**[00:40:25.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000545)  
of vulnerable Filipinos and helping them move forward more sustainable and dignified lives.

**[00:40:30.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000546)  
Each program addresses a different challenge, but are all consistent with our broader objective

**[00:40:34.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000547)  
of investing in human capacities, improving the circumstances of vulnerable Filipinos.

**[00:40:39.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000548)  
Let me now briefly discuss each flagship program stating with the Walang Gutom Program.

**[00:40:43.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000549)  
The Walang Gutom Program is the Department's flagship intervention to address food insecurity

**[00:40:48.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000550)  
by providing targeted food assistance to poor

**[00:40:50.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000551)  
households. For 2025,

**[00:40:52.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000552)  
the program

**[00:40:53.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000553)  
provided food transfers

**[00:40:54.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000554)  
to 300,000

**[00:40:55.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000555)  
poor households. In 2026,

**[00:40:58.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000556)  
implementation is being expanded to reach 750,000 food-insecure households by

**[00:41:04.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000557)  
the end of this year.

**[00:41:05.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000558)  
For fiscal year 2027, 1.89 billion of this locally funded project with a physical target

**[00:41:11.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000559)  
of 50,000 is allocated and 3.87 billion of foreign assisted project of refuel with a

**[00:41:17.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000560)  
physical target of 750,000

**[00:41:20.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000562)  
effectively expanding the program's coverage.

**[00:41:23.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000563)  
The Tarabasa Tutoring Program addresses learning gaps

**[00:41:26.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000564)  
among children while providing opportunities

**[00:41:28.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000565)  
for young people to participate as youth tutors

**[00:41:31.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000566)  
and youth development workers.

**[00:41:33.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000567)  
It is designed as a two-way intervention,

**[00:41:35.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000568)  
supporting learners while they also provide

**[00:41:38.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000569)  
meaningful engagement and assistance to the youth

**[00:41:41.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000570)  
and the parents or guardians of the learners.

**[00:41:43.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000571)  
As part of further clarifying

**[00:41:45.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000572)  
the program's core intervention,

**[00:41:47.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000573)  
we are also proposing to rename the program

**[00:41:49.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000574)  
as the Tarabasa Cash for Work Program

**[00:41:52.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000575)  
to more clearly reflect its primary mechanism

**[00:41:54.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000576)  
of providing cash for work opportunities

**[00:41:56.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000577)  
to the youth while delivering tutorial support

**[00:41:59.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000578)  
to learners and participation incentive

**[00:42:02.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000579)  
to parents or guardians.

**[00:42:04.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000580)  
The program has demonstrated

**[00:42:05.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000581)  
strong implementation performance in 2025

**[00:42:08.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000582)  
and is on its path to reaching its targets for 2026.

**[00:42:11.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000583)  
For 2027, we are proposing 900 million

**[00:42:14.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000584)  
to support 66,633 beneficiaries,

**[00:42:18.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000585)  
including 47,595

**[00:42:19.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000586)  
learners.

**[00:42:21.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000587)  
This investment will allow us to sustain the program

**[00:42:24.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000588)  
and continue addressing learning gaps

**[00:42:26.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000589)  
while creating opportunities

**[00:42:27.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000590)  
for youth participation and development.

**[00:42:30.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000591)  
The Pag-Abot Program aims to reach out

**[00:42:33.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000592)  
to vulnerable and disadvantaged children,

**[00:42:35.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000593)  
individuals, and families in street situations

**[00:42:37.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000594)  
and providing them with access to social protection services

**[00:42:40.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000595)  
and appropriate interventions based on their needs.

**[00:42:43.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000596)  
As part of the integrated response,

**[00:42:46.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000597)  
the program also implements the Walang Gutom Kitchen,

**[00:42:48.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000598)  
which gives immediate food assistance

**[00:42:50.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000599)  
while connecting them to other appropriate

**[00:42:52.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000600)  
social welfare services.

**[00:42:54.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000601)  
The program has demonstrated strong implementation performance,

**[00:42:58.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000602)  
reaching 99.48% fund

**[00:43:01.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000603)  
utilization in 2025,

**[00:43:04.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000604)  
with 6,846 clients served,

**[00:43:07.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000605)  
while in 2026, the program has served more than 10,000 clients

**[00:43:11.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000606)  
with a 63.71 utilization of its 807 million budget.

**[00:43:16.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000607)  
For fiscal year 2027, we are proposing 807 million to provide,

**[00:43:18.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000608)  
provide assistance to 5,000 clients. Through Pagabot, we are bringing social protection closer

**[00:43:24.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000609)  
to vulnerable and disadvantaged families, individuals, and families in street situation

**[00:43:29.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000610)  
who may otherwise remain outside the reach of regular government intervention. Beyond our major

**[00:43:34.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000611)  
programs, the SWD is also developing responses to prevent emerging social welfare needs.

**[00:43:40.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000612)  
These include initiatives such as Project Aruga, ProtecTEEN, Project SAFE for OSAEC victims,

**[00:43:46.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000613)  
which also addresses specific vulnerabilities and emerging concerns among children,

**[00:43:51.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000614)  
adolescents, and family. This initiative demonstrates our continuing effort to make

**[00:43:56.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000615)  
social protection more responsive to changing needs and emerging vulnerabilities.

**[00:44:02.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000616)  
The DSWD social protection and social welfare mandate is complemented by the work for

**[00:44:07.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000617)  
attached and supervised agencies. Should this body wish for us to proceed, I can present in

**[00:44:13.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000618)  
brief the financial performance of each agency,

**[00:44:16.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000619)  
or I can present the financial performance of each agency,

**[00:44:16.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000620)  
or I can call the heads of the ASAS to present and answer questions,

**[00:44:19.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000621)  
should you have any, on the performance of their agencies.

**[00:44:28.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000622)  
Mr. Chairman, I think I've read the whole NEP summary, so I will now have to close.

**[00:44:37.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000623)  
We thank the honorable members of the committee and your continued support for the department

**[00:44:40.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000624)  
and our shared commitment to empower people and communities and improve the lives of our

**[00:44:45.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000625)  
fellow Filipinos.

**[00:44:45.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000626)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:44:47.960] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000627)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[00:44:51.280] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000628)  
Before we move on to interpellations, may I remind our distinguished colleagues that each member is

**[00:44:58.080] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000629)  
allotted 5 minutes

**[00:45:00.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000630)  
for questions or interpellation excluding the responses of the resource persons.

**[00:45:07.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000631)  
When the bell sounds, the member has 30 seconds to conclude.

**[00:45:14.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000632)  
Assistent with the longstanding practice of the house,

**[00:45:18.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000633)  
the chair shall recognize minority, independent and majority members

**[00:45:24.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000634)  
in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:45:28.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000635)

**[00:45:34.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000636)  
before we move on, may I recognize first our members who just came in.

**[00:45:47.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000638)  
Assistant Majority Leader Johan Monic Bautista

**[00:45:53.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000639)  
Representative Ferdinand B1 Beltran,

**[00:45:57.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000640)  
Representative John Attorney JP Padernos,

**[00:46:02.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000641)  
Vice Chair Romel Rico Angara, Vice Chair Jerry Boy Espina

**[00:46:09.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000642)  
and Representative Yolio Rodriguez.

**[00:46:13.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000643)  
So for the first member who will be interpelating,

**[00:46:18.020] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-000644)  
may we call on Representative Perci Cendaña.

**[00:46:26.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000645)  
Magandang umaga,

**[00:46:27.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000646)  
Mr. Chair at magandang

**[00:46:28.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000647)  
umaga po sa DSWD family.

**[00:46:32.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000648)  
According to the NCDA, there are 2.8 million Filipinos registered as PWDs.

**[00:46:40.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000649)  
Kaya nakapagtataka po na nangalahati ang budget ng NCDA

**[00:46:45.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000650)  
from 187 million in the 2026 GAA to a mere 91 million in the 2027 NEP.

**[00:46:55.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000651)  
If we're going to do the math, that's

**[00:46:57.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000652)  
only Php32 per PWD. Inuulit ko

**[00:47:01.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000653)  
po, ang investment ng gobyerno sa darating na 2027, kung

**[00:47:06.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000654)  
ang basihan po ay NEP,

**[00:47:08.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000655)  
is only ₱32 per PWD.

**[00:47:12.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000656)  
Ang tanong po ng marami, bakit

**[00:47:14.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000657)  
parang discounted ang budget para sa PWD?

**[00:47:18.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000658)  
Ano po ba ang dahilan at ah nabawasan na ganito kalaki ang budget ng ating NCDA?

**[00:47:24.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000659)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:47:25.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000660)  
Mr. Chair, una po sa lahat,

**[00:47:28.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000661)  
syempre gusto ho namin na sana maibalik yung nawalang budget sa NCDA

**[00:47:33.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000662)  
which is an attach agency. Pero

**[00:47:35.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000663)  
gusto ko lang pong linawin na hindi naman ho ibig sabihin na nabawasan ang tulong at suporta ng

**[00:47:39.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000664)  
pamalaan sa ating mga may Kapansanan.

**[00:47:41.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000665)  
Sa katunayan, malaking bahagi ho ng budget ng mother department

**[00:47:45.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000666)  
itself goes through financial assistance through the program for people with disability.

**[00:47:50.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000667)  
Yung mga innovation programs namin, yung mga bagong programa katulad ng Project

**[00:47:54.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000668)  
Aruga are also geared towards people with disabilities and their families.

**[00:47:59.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000669)  
So we will provide the committee a disaggregated

**[00:48:02.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000670)  
data based on the mother department spending on people with disability.

**[00:48:05.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000671)  
So we would like to assure the good representation na hindi ho nababawasan.

**[00:48:09.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000672)  
Ngayon ho pag titignan natin yung mandate kasi ng

**[00:48:12.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000673)  
NCDA they were really tasked to

**[00:48:14.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000674)  
one do policies for people with disabilities

**[00:48:17.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000675)  
to to capacitate local government units kasi

**[00:48:20.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000676)  
devolved po. LGUs have PDAO offices

**[00:48:23.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000677)  
or people with the disability affairs offices.

**[00:48:26.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000678)  
Yun ho yung mga implementing arm.

**[00:48:28.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000679)  
So yung mga budget n nakabukod

**[00:48:30.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000680)  
now itong nawalang budget na to ay capital expenditure.

**[00:48:33.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000681)  
Nakalagay po iyan sa libro ng NCDA to provide IT equipment to the different PDAO

**[00:48:38.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000682)  
offices. Kasi alam natin may mga PDAO offices na maliliit ang LGU

**[00:48:42.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000683)  
at walang kapasidad na mag bumili ng kanilang mga gamit.

**[00:48:45.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000684)  
So yun ho yung na walang suporta kasi non-recurring

**[00:48:48.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000685)  
po siya. Pero pagdating ho naman sa direct grants and subsidies,

**[00:48:51.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000686)  
wala

**[00:48:52.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000687)  
ho niyan direct masyado sa NCDA.

**[00:48:54.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000688)  
Lahat ho yan nasa mother department.

**[00:48:56.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000689)  
What I would like to point out

**[00:48:58.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000690)  
also na sa NCDA na nanatili ho yung mga core programs niya

**[00:49:02.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000691)  
for instance it's new program on getting

**[00:49:05.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000692)  
working with development pediatricians to

**[00:49:09.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000693)  
go to the farthest communities to

**[00:49:11.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000694)  
provide free uh um free assessments so

**[00:49:15.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000695)  
that may assessment path yung mga

**[00:49:17.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000696)  
kababayan nating mahihirap na nahihirapan

**[00:49:19.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000697)  
kumuha ng assessment dahil napakahaba

**[00:49:21.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000698)  
ng mga pila sa mga hospital

**[00:49:23.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000699)  
na may dev peds.

**[00:49:24.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000700)  
so ang amin lang ho doon

**[00:49:26.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000701)  
bagamat't gusto naming maibalik

**[00:49:28.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000702)  
ah gusto lang ho rin namin linawin na

**[00:49:30.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000703)  
those are capital expenditure na nonreurring

**[00:49:34.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000704)  
Magkano po yung initial proposal

**[00:49:36.360] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000705)  
pre-NEP ng NCDA?

**[00:49:41.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000706)  
na >> the executive director was one who submitted

**[00:49:43.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-000707)  
ED Glenda, what was your initial?

**[00:49:47.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000708)  
>> with the indulgence of the good secretary

**[00:49:50.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000709)  
ang initial po ng NCDA for the tier one

**[00:49:54.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000710)  
is Um 287 million po tier 1 and tier 2 po.

**[00:49:59.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000711)  
So ang nabawas po sa amin is 85 ah 82,145,000 for

**[00:50:06.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000712)  
the capital outlay

**[00:50:07.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000713)  
as what was mentioned by the secretary

**[00:50:09.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000714)  
and then ₱15,781,000

**[00:50:13.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000715)  
para po ito doon sa MOOE

**[00:50:15.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000716)  
or yun pong mga hinier po namin na mga magde-develop

**[00:50:19.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000717)  
ng system at magme-maintain

**[00:50:21.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000718)

**[00:50:23.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000719)

**[00:50:25.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000720)  
Uh simula po ng dumating po ang ating mabuting secretary,

**[00:50:29.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000721)  
nag-develop na po ang NCDA ng

**[00:50:32.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000722)

**[00:50:36.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000723)  
at iba't ibang klase po

**[00:50:38.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000724)  
ng system katulad po ng disaster risk

**[00:50:41.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000725)  
management for persons with disability.

**[00:50:43.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000726)  
Meron din po tayong communitybased development

**[00:50:46.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000727)  
uh database system na isine-synchronize

**[00:50:49.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000728)  
na po natin at ang panibago po yung atin pong unified persons with disability ay the

**[00:50:54.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000729)  
system. So with this system

**[00:50:56.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000730)  
po kinakailangan po naming mabalik at least the MOOE para

**[00:51:00.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000731)  
po ma-maintain po namin 'tong systems

**[00:51:02.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000732)  
na 'to tsaka po para po ma-ensure po natin na yung mga bagong h po

**[00:51:06.770] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-000733)  
namin eh patuloy po silang makakatrabaho for the succeeding

at kinakailangan

**[01:00:00.850] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001201)  
Thank you. Thank you, Cong Perci. Ayusin mo nga. Kunin mo nga pabalik. All your observations were duly noted. We now recognize Representative Jam Baronda.

**[01:00:19.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001202)  
Thank you so much, Mr. Chair. Before I proceed, I would like to thank, of course,

**[01:00:25.650] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001203)  
Secretary Rex Gatchalian for his exemplary leadership in leading the department. Mr. Secretary,

**[01:00:35.570] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001204)  
I would just like to ask regarding the Uplift program of the government. We base our

**[01:00:44.290] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001205)  
beneficiaries on the CBMS program. May I ask for the total CBMS beneficiaries of the country?

**[01:00:53.010] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001206)  
Madam Chair,

**[01:00:55.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001207)  
hindi, we don't have access to it kasi ang binibigay lang sa amin yung poor and near poor. We can give you the poor and near poor.

**[01:01:05.490] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001208)  
May data privacy rules; mahigpit ang PSA doon.

**[01:01:10.060] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001209)  
So based po doon sa resulta nila, 2024 po ba na PSA?

**[01:01:20.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001210)  
2023, 2024.

**[01:01:25.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001211)  
Ang 2024 na poor data natin, 2,546,846 households ang classified as poor, plus 20% above the threshold. So yung near poor kasama po diyan.

**[01:01:42.540] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001212)  
So sa ngayon po, based on sa tala ninyo, ilan na po yung nabigyan ninyo ng cash assistance for Uplift?

**[01:01:49.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001213)  
Kasi ho, ma'am, kung matatandaan ninyo, Uplift has three different buckets. So we gave, in my opening remarks we said close to

**[01:01:55.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001214)  
six million.

**[01:01:57.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001215)  
Tatlong bucket 'yon. Unang bucket noon is a top-up sa 4Ps beneficiaries natin. That's around three

**[01:02:03.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001216)  
million household

**[01:02:04.720] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001216-split-2)  
yes

**[01:02:06.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001216-split-3)  
3.5 million household one time lang huyon they all got two thousand pesos

**[01:02:11.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001217)  
one time. The second bucket, ito ho yung mga wala sa 4Ps pero categorized as mahihirap rin sila.

**[01:02:19.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001218)  
Sa 2.5 million ang target diyan. Ang na-service na natin, 1.7 million,

**[01:02:27.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001219)  
ng tig-₱2,000 kada buwan mula July hanggang December.

**[01:02:32.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001220)  
Let me explain the gap po, if you'll allow me.

**[01:02:35.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001221)  
The gap here, Madam, Mr. Chair, kaya may gap to.

**[01:02:38.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001222)  
From our manual payout, if you can recall kasi the first round is manual for validation.

**[01:02:44.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001223)  
Despite the fact na hinanap namin sila, una tulad na nabanggit ko, wala na doon yung mga nakatira.

**[01:02:50.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001224)  
Pangalawa, iba na i-invalidate dahil after two years, gumanda na yung mga buhay,

**[01:02:54.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001225)  
hindi na sila nagkakwalify, so they're waiving the rights or the social workers are saying,

**[01:02:58.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001226)  
wag na kasi gumanda na ho yung buhay.

**[01:03:01.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001227)  
Now, there's a last category na hindi uma-appear sa poor and near poor because these are above minimum wage.

**[01:03:08.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001228)  
They're called low wage earners ng SSS.

**[01:03:11.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001229)  
Sa database po ito ng SSS kinuwa.

**[01:03:13.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001230)  
Hindi sila poor, hindi rin sila near poor.

**[01:03:16.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001231)  
So there's around 1.5 million sa kanila of which we've catered to 811,000.

**[01:03:22.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001232)  
Now, bakit ho may gap?

**[01:03:24.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001233)  
Yung mga basic deposit accounts nila based on SSS, kasi automated ho ito, non-existent na.

**[01:03:31.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001234)  
They're dormant.

**[01:03:31.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001235)  
Nasa listahan ng SSS, pero yung mga accounts either closed na or dormant na.

**[01:03:37.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001236)  
So total of what we've serviced so far is at around 5.5, 5.7 million of the 7.5 na target.

**[01:03:47.300] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001237)  
Mr. Secretary, ito kasi 'yon.

**[01:03:49.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001238)  
In my district, during my collaboration meeting with your department,

**[01:03:54.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001239)  
iba ang nasasabi ng figure ni PSA.

**[01:03:57.720] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001240)  
Iba din sa DSWD.

**[01:04:00.160] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001241)  
So, parang meron talagang disparity.

**[01:04:01.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001242)  
Ako na, ako na, ako na.

**[01:04:03.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001243)  
Mr. Chair, wala nang—

**[01:04:04.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001244)  
Mr. Chair, babatukan ko yung regional director ninyo eh.

**[01:04:09.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001245)  
Nandyan sa likod eh.

**[01:04:10.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001246)  
Wala nang data ang DSWD.

**[01:04:13.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001247)  
If you can recall, Listahanan is [unclear].

**[01:04:16.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001248)  
Hindi na hindi kami...

**[01:04:16.460] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001249)  
So, mali.

**[01:04:17.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001250)  
Please correct me.

**[01:04:19.040] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001251)  
Hindi po ganun.

**[01:04:20.120] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001252)  
Iba yung sinasabi ni PSA.

**[01:04:22.720] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001253)  
Tapos yung time na

**[01:04:24.220] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001254)  
mag-payout si DSWD, there are beneficiaries na nung na-check ng DSWD, hindi qualified.

**[01:04:33.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001255)  
So, tama naman si RD.

**[01:04:35.200] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001256)  
Sorry ah.

**[01:04:35.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001257)  
So, huwag mong batukan.

**[01:04:37.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001258)  
Kinagawa niya lang yung trabaho niya.

**[01:04:39.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001259)  
Ang point is, it's good that meron tayong check and balance.

**[01:04:45.760] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001260)  
But the thing is, parang duplication of work.

**[01:04:49.460] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001261)  
Sino ba talagang in charge of social services natin?

**[01:04:52.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001262)  
Si PSA ba?

**[01:04:53.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001263)  
Or si DSWD?

**[01:04:55.400] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001264)  
Okay, let's go back to listahanan.

**[01:04:57.620] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001265)  
I have very bad experience kasi with listahanan.

**[01:05:00.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001266)  
During my first term as Congresswoman in 2019, may lista po si Listahanan.

**[01:05:06.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001267)  
Unfortunately, in March 17, 2020, nagkaroon ng lockdown because of COVID pandemic.

**[01:05:12.660] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001268)  
Hindi na tapos.

**[01:05:13.100] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001269)  
So yung una naming reaksyon is we want Sustainable Livelihood Program of DSWD.

**[01:05:19.740] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001270)  
We list down 230 members.

**[01:05:23.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001271)  
Nagka problema na nawala na ng trabaho or yung mga negosyo nila, nagka problema na.

**[01:05:32.240] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001272)  
In short, we list down 230 beneficiaries.

**[01:05:35.900] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001273)  
Nung chineck po ng DSWD sa Listahanan, of the 223, yung qualified lang po are 157 that time.

**[01:05:47.020] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001274)  
So, nagkaroon ng more than 70 na mga beneficiary na hindi na qualified.

**[01:05:52.440] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001275)  
So, no?

**[01:05:53.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001276)  
Dahil ang basis nila 2019, nagka problema kami doon sa bigayan ng cash assistance for SLP.

**[01:06:02.040] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001277)  
So, there were like 70 na hindi qualified.

**[01:06:04.880] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001278)  
So, yun po yung problema natin, Mr. Secretary, kung meron kayong safety nets na i-base nyo on the situation,

**[01:06:12.340] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001279)  
like for example, sa nangyari ng 2019 and 2021 na listahanan.

**[01:06:17.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001280)  
Mr. Chair, una ho, biro lang 'yon. Love na love namin yung RD niya.

**[01:06:22.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001281)  
And we don't...

**[01:06:23.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001282)  
There's no violence in the department. We like RD Tolits.

**[01:06:27.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001283)  
Mr. Chair, isa-isahin ko. Loaded ho kasi yung question, but allow me to expound further.

**[01:06:32.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001284)  
Una ko, listahanan was the main database of the government.

**[01:06:36.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001285)  
But because of the law, the CBMS law that this house and the upper house passed, wala nang listahanan.

**[01:06:42.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001286)  
Unfortunately, listahanan na nire-refer nyo, just pabalik tanaw ko lang,

**[01:06:45.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001287)  
nung dumating rin ako, it was a chaotic listahanan, not because the department was at fault,

**[01:06:50.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001288)  
but because hindi natapos yung survey ng listahanan because COVID.

**[01:06:53.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001289)  
So they launched a listahanan because it was required from us to do so,

**[01:06:58.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001290)  
or else wala tayong database kahit na hindi siya fully natapos dahil sa COVID.

**[01:07:02.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001291)  
Unforeseen circumstance.

**[01:07:04.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001292)  
But that's behind us because wala nang listahanan. Bawal na ho yun.

**[01:07:08.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001293)  
No government agency can craft a new database.

**[01:07:11.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001294)  
Ayon sa batas, lahat kami ang gagamitin na namin CBMS.

**[01:07:15.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001295)  
So ma'am, the first question, kami ang in-charge sa implementation,

**[01:07:18.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001296)  
but the targeting in the list comes from PSA using CBMS.

**[01:07:23.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001297)  
DepDev at PSA, anak ho nila 'yon.

**[01:07:26.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001298)  
Number two, not that I am going to, not that I am lawyering for PSA,

**[01:07:32.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001299)  
but ma'am, alam rin po natin from our own experience, there is no perfect list.

**[01:07:36.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001300)  
Merong 20 to 30% na exclusion, inclusion error.

**[01:07:42.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001301)  
Pero unfortunately, from the ground, tama ho kayo, Mr. Chair, tama ang ating buting congresswoman,

**[01:07:48.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001302)  
etong CBMS na to, medyo napakalaki ng exclusion at inclusion.

**[01:07:53.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001303)  
Napakalaki.

**[01:07:54.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001304)  
Kaya nga ho, kung matatandaan nyo, lagi ho kami tinatanong, ba't ayaw namin gawing automated yung payout.

**[01:08:00.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001305)  
Dahil nga, we need to do the validation on the ground.

**[01:08:03.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001306)  
As the good Congresswoman pointed out, DSWD is tasked to safeguard the money that we have,

**[01:08:09.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001307)  
na makarating yon sa nangangailangan.

**[01:08:11.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001308)  
Hindi dun sa hindi nangangailangan.

**[01:08:13.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001309)  
So ang nangyayari, tama ho si Congresswoman, pagbigay ho sa amin ang CBMS list, we validate it.

**[01:08:19.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001310)  
That's why nagmamanual ho kami.

**[01:08:21.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001311)  
Dahil sa manual process namin nakikita,

**[01:08:23.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001312)  
totoong tao pa ba to?

**[01:08:25.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001313)  
Buhay pa ba siya?

**[01:08:26.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001314)  
Nandun pa ba siya?

**[01:08:27.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001315)  
Gumanda na ba yung buhay?

**[01:08:29.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001316)  
Kasi remember, CBMS was done 2024.

**[01:08:31.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001317)  
We're now doing 2026.

**[01:08:33.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001318)  
May iba diyan, gumanda na yung buhay.

**[01:08:35.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001319)  
Nakapag-abroad at nagkaroon ng negosyo.

**[01:08:39.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001320)  
So that's my answer.

**[01:08:41.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001321)  
The short and easy of it, unfortunately, we have taken this up with PSA and NEDA,

**[01:08:45.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001322)  
and they promised to tighten the next CBMS round next year.

**[01:08:49.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001323)  
Kausap lang namin sila kahapon.

**[01:08:51.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001324)  
Kasi sinasabi nga namin,

**[01:08:52.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001325)  
na nahihirapan kami dun sa listahan kasi kami ang sinasabugan.

**[01:08:56.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001326)  
Kami lagi ang pinuputukan.

**[01:08:58.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001327)  
Akala ng tao, nagdadamot kami,

**[01:09:00.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001328)  
or akala ng tao, mali yung targeting namin.

**[01:09:03.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001329)  
Kung listahanan yan, by all means, we would accept it.

**[01:09:06.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001330)  
Pero hindi kasi kami ang nagtarget neto.

**[01:09:08.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001331)  
But to the same degree, Mr. Chair,

**[01:09:10.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001332)  
I come from the ranks of the local government unit.

**[01:09:12.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001333)  
Our LGUs are also tasked to work closely with the PSA in rolling out the CBMS.

**[01:09:18.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001334)  
So next year, meron tayong bagong CBMS.

**[01:09:21.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001335)  
Hopefully, maggamot na tong mga issues na to.

**[01:09:24.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001336)  
Another case in point, Mr. Chairman,

**[01:09:26.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001337)  
we've also fixed the SLP issue that plagued that program.

**[01:09:30.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001338)  
Kaya nga, kung titignan nyo, ngayon, mas mabilis kami gumamit ng SLP.

**[01:09:34.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001339)  
Because nga, one, we are no longer saying na yun na lang, yung listahan na lang.

**[01:09:39.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001340)  
When we joined the department, we scrapped the hole na dapat sa listahanan kinukuha.

**[01:09:43.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001341)  
We do the field or manual validation on the ground.

**[01:09:46.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001342)  
Kaya nga, bottom line, lagi kami tinatarong bakit nagbamanual kami.

**[01:09:50.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001343)  
Hindi dahil trip lang hoon namin or dahil gusto namin pahirapan ng tao.

**[01:09:54.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001344)  
Kundi 'yon lang ang only means namin in trying to make sure na yung pera ng taong-bayan ay napupunta sa tamang benepisyaryo.

**[01:10:03.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001345)  
Thank you so much, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:10:05.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001346)  
It's different talaga pag nag-local, nag-congress ka din.

**[01:10:12.600] Rep. Julienne "Jam" L. Baronda** (asr-001347)  
So you're really the right man for this job.

**[01:11:17.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001361)  
Close to 40%.

**[01:11:18.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001362)  
Close to 40%.

**[01:11:20.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001363)

**[01:11:21.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001364)

**[01:11:26.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001365)

**[01:11:46.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001371)

I-insert ko lang

**[01:28:44.070] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001763)

**[01:29:09.790] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001776)

**[01:29:11.930] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001777)

**[01:29:13.950] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001778)  
let's say 10 years, 20

**[01:29:16.130] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001779)  
years, 30 years, pero hanggang ngayon

**[01:29:17.830] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001780)

**[01:29:19.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001781)  
Yes, hindi ho kami tumitigil. In fact, we're

**[01:29:21.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001782)

**[01:29:23.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001783)
