**[01:29:25.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001784)  
last year. The DBM has already granted us

**[01:29:27.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001785)  
5,000 new regular

**[01:29:29.810] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001786)  
positions. Hindi lang ho to

**[01:29:31.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001787)  
para sa mga social worker kasi meron rin tayong

**[01:29:33.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001788)  
mga development workers who's been with the department

**[01:29:35.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001789)  
for years. This is

**[01:29:37.670] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001790)  
for the four piece alone. So kung

**[01:29:39.450] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001791)  
15,000 sila, this is

**[01:29:41.810] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001792)  
the first 5,000 nila. Then may next

**[01:29:43.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001793)  
5,000 na commitment ulit until

**[01:29:45.750] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001794)  
lahat sila ma-regularize. Now,

**[01:29:47.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001795)  
Mr. Chair, when I say

**[01:29:49.850] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001796)  
4Ps, hindi naman kasi limited to 4Ps

**[01:29:51.750] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001797)  
lang to. Based on civil service

**[01:29:53.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001798)  
rules, pwede rin mag-apply the

**[01:29:55.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001799)  
eligible social workers sa labas

**[01:29:57.690] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001800)  
ng cluster ng four piece to become

**[01:29:59.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001801)  
regular.

**[01:30:00.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001802)  
positions as long as they meet the eligibility requirements. On top of that,

**[01:30:03.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001803)  
we also got a couple, konti lang naman, pero it's a step towards the

**[01:30:07.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001804)  
right direction, a couple of regular positions also for

**[01:30:10.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001805)  
plantilla positions for the disaster group, the innovations group, yung sa Social

**[01:30:14.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001806)  
Technology Bureau. And at the same time, hopefully, padating na rin,

**[01:30:18.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001807)  
sabi sa akin ni Sec Kim, yung sa AICS program natin ng mga manggagawa.

**[01:30:21.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001808)  
Ganda ng mga sagot ni Sec. Ibig sabihin po, Mr. Chair, it is a continuing regularization process po?

**[01:30:29.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001809)  
Correct. At may progress po. Hindi ho laway lamang, kundi

**[01:30:31.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001810)  
limang libo. Marami ho, and I think that's the first time na ganyang

**[01:30:36.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001811)  
karami yung nare-regularize. Hinahanda na rin yung second batch na 5,000.

**[01:30:41.950] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001812)  
Wow. Sige po. Dadako naman po ako sa ibang katanungan.

**[01:30:45.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001813)  
Psychosocial support po. Ang DSWD ay merong program na tinatawag

**[01:30:50.540] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001814)  
na Wireless Mental Health and Psychosocial Support, or WeSupport. This

**[01:30:56.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001815)  
DSWD

**[01:30:57.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001816)  
initiative aims to establish a systematic response in addressing mental

**[01:31:02.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001817)  
health and psychosocial concerns through wireless and online platforms.

**[01:31:07.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001818)  
Ilan po ang total users ng WeSupport platform sa nakaraang taon, at ano ang

**[01:31:13.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001819)  
breakdown per age group? Kung sakaling wala pong data right now,

**[01:31:18.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001820)  
puwede naman pong i-submit sa committee. Kasama po doon yung isa sa katanungan ko rin na

**[01:31:23.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001821)  
ano-anong mga coverage ng WeSupport program

**[01:31:27.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001822)  
para sa kabataan at estudyante?

**[01:31:29.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001823)  
Mr. Chair, the WeSupport program is a smaller program of the

**[01:31:34.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001824)  
department. 681 ang naging beneficiaries. Sounds really small, but we've already

**[01:31:39.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001825)  
pivoted under the Social Technology Bureau, a new program patterned after our

**[01:31:44.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001826)  
cash-for-work tutoring program, yung Tara Basa. Ngayon meron ho kaming Tara Usap.

**[01:31:49.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001827)  
It's a peer-to-peer support group in colleges and universities that we are

**[01:31:53.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001828)  
now piloting everywhere. Ang naging partner po namin doon is the

**[01:31:57.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001829)  
Ateneo School of Psychology, as well as the UP School of Psychology, kung saan

**[01:32:01.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001830)  
we're establishing safe spaces in schools. It's like Alcoholics Anonymous abroad.

**[01:32:06.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001831)  
At kina-capacitate natin yung mga peer counselors, pero obviously klaro rin sa kanila yung hangganan ng kanilang kayang gawin and the referral pathways. So that's something that we're proud

**[01:32:19.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001832)  
of, that we're already innovating. We veered towards peer-to-peer kasi we

**[01:32:24.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001833)  
always believe in community approaches kung saan we

**[01:32:27.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001834)  
empower the community to take care of the community.

**[01:32:30.340] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001835)  
May monitoring system po kayo para makita yung effectiveness sa pagbawas ng stress, anxiety, depression?

**[01:32:37.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001836)  
Dahil pilot pa lang ho ito, bahagi naman ho 'yon ng design. But we

**[01:32:40.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001837)  
will gladly report back to the committee after the pilot run is over.

**[01:32:44.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001838)  
Para po sa huling katanungan, given the recent school shootings na nagpapakita

**[01:32:49.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001839)  
ng vulnerability sa ating mga kabataan, ano po ang kongkretong plano ng DSWD

**[01:32:56.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001840)  
para palakasin ang long-term psychosocial support ng WeSupport para sa mga kabataan?

**[01:33:02.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001841)  
Madam, Mr. Chair, we're veering away not

**[01:33:04.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001842)  
just from WeSupport. Kami, we've been working with the Masayang Pamilyang

**[01:33:07.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001843)  
organisasyon, yung Masayang Pamilyang Pilipino organization

**[01:33:11.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001844)  
for the longest time. These are people from the academe,

**[01:33:15.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001845)  
most notably si Professor Alampay, who is one of the more senior

**[01:33:19.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001846)  
academe members of the Ateneo School of Psychology. Kami naniniwala, patatagin natin yung pamilya.

**[01:33:24.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001847)  
So we've revamped the 4Ps Family Development Sessions to strengthen

**[01:33:28.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001848)  
the role of our parents in making sure na alam nila ano yung mga red flags. Kasi it starts at home. Tama, malaki ang gampanin ng mga guro, pero pag kausap namin ang mga guro, nagsisimula yung formative years sa bahay.

**[01:33:44.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001849)  
So that's why we're looking at it from a paradigm of a parent

**[01:33:47.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001850)  
or a family intervention. So una po 'yon, binago na ho natin yung 18 years na

**[01:33:52.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001851)  
curriculum ng FDS, ng Family Development Sessions, to

**[01:33:55.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001852)  
incorporate newer newer topics like digital uh

**[01:34:00.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001853)  
digital literacy but in the same case uh digital awareness or digital parenting

**[01:34:04.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001854)  
These are new modules. Number two, yung batas ho na Parent Effectiveness

**[01:34:09.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001855)  
and simula namin roll out and these this one is being done

**[01:34:13.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001856)  
together with edu child another known family oriented

**[01:34:17.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001857)  
local group that does capacity building for our parents. At the end of the day,

**[01:34:22.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001858)  
yung long-term ho, we do the

**[01:34:24.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001859)  
sana huwag na maulit, but we do the post kung

**[01:34:27.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001860)  
may nangyayari. Working with the academe, we have trauma-informed care already na beyond

**[01:34:33.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001861)  
disasters, pati mga ganitong insidente.

**[01:34:35.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001862)  
Pero kami, ang approach pa rin namin, strengthening the Filipino families,

**[01:34:39.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001863)  
strengthening our parents' capabilities so that they can watch over their children at home.

**[01:34:44.190] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001864)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:34:45.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001865)  
Just a short manifestation dahil wala na po akong time.

**[01:34:49.920] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001866)  
Mr. Chair, nais kong i-highlight na lang ang mental health support ay may direct

**[01:34:55.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001867)  
impact sa safety, learning at overall well-being ng kabataan.

**[01:35:00.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001868)  
Kaya mahalaga na palakasin ang programang ito bilang preventive at

**[01:35:04.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001869)  
accessible support system para sa ating mga kabataan,

**[01:35:08.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001870)  
gaya ng WeSupport. Also, Mr. Chair, napansin ko po na bumaba po yung

**[01:35:13.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001871)  
pondo sa ating Supplemental Feeding Program na mula sa ₱9.56

**[01:35:19.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001872)  
bilyon nitong taon, naging ₱6.35 billion na lamang po sa NEP ng

**[01:35:25.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001873)  
2027. At dahil po doon, in the proper time, I will move to increase the budget of the DSWD for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:35:35.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001874)  
Hindi po dapat tinatapyas ang ahensya na inaasahan ng mga marginalized sectors at vulnerable constituents ng ating bansa.

**[01:35:45.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-001875)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair.

**[01:35:46.920] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001876)  
Thank you.

**[01:35:47.260] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001877)  
Thank you.

**[01:35:49.620] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001878)  
May we call on Representative Adrian Amatong.

**[01:35:57.180] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001879)  
Thank you very much Mr. Chair.

**[01:35:59.320] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001880)  
Magandang umaga po, Secretary Rex, sa buong DSWD family.

**[01:36:03.540] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001881)  
Mr. Chair, ang DSWD isa sa pinaka-active na agency sa distrito ko.

**[01:36:08.840] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001882)  
Kaya masaya kami.

**[01:36:11.140] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001883)  
Alam ko marami siyang programs, pero dalawa sa pinakagusto namin, yung AICS at saka yung SLP.

**[01:36:16.840] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001884)  
Yung AICS dahil marami kaming mga mahihirap na estudyante na hirap na hirap silang mag-aral,

**[01:36:24.920] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001885)  
eh dahil sa flexibility ng AICS,

**[01:36:27.120] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001889)  
natutulungan natin sila.

**[01:36:28.940] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001890)  
So mga thousands po yan.

**[01:36:30.360] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001891)  
Tapos yung sa SLP gustong gusto namin kasi iba siya doun sa mga doll out programs.

**[01:36:36.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001892)  
SLP kasi we give those who are willing to work really hard for themselves na mag-succeed

**[01:36:42.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001893)  
lalo na mga walang capital.

**[01:36:44.340] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001894)  
secretary gusto ko lang itanong. Narinig ko kasi medyo nabawasan yata yung budget niyo sa SLP.

**[01:36:51.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001895)  
Ah yes Mr. Chair,

**[01:36:52.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001896)  
it's one of these line item na binanggit namin at sumulat rin kami sa committee

**[01:36:57.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001897)  
na sana

**[01:36:57.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001898)  
maibalik at least man lang, ma-augment by a billion pesos. Mr. Chair, that is correct. Congressman

**[01:37:02.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001899)  
Amatong is correct. Yung SLP, we're very happy na maraming mga partners natin ang tinatanong

**[01:37:09.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001900)  
na SLP. That means nandoon na tayo sa mindset of teaching our people how to fish. We've actually

**[01:37:15.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001901)  
submitted a one-billion-peso fund augmentation sana for the supplemental, sorry, for

**[01:37:20.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001902)  
the Sustainable Livelihood Program. Kasi nga, that's part of our mandate, social development, hindi lang puro social welfare.

**[01:37:29.070] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001903)  
Mr. Chair, I'd like to support yung move na i-increase yung budget specifically

**[01:37:32.760] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001904)  
sa SLP kasi na-experience namin. Ako kasi minsan ngayon yung payout, ayokong pumunta sa mga

**[01:37:38.520] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001905)  
dole-outs kasi parang, although natutulungan naman yung local economy, pero

**[01:37:45.640] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001906)  
siyempre pagkatapos noon wala na eh. Pero yung SLP kasi ang laking promise, kasi nga sabi ni Secretary,

**[01:37:51.560] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001907)  
we teach our people how to fish. Hindi lang yung bibigyan ng isda.

**[01:37:55.320] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001908)  
So, Mr.

**[01:37:56.200] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001909)  
Chair, gusto ko lang i-support yung move na yun na i-increase talaga yung budget,

**[01:38:00.440] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001910)  
kung pwede mas malaki pa sa one billion. Kasi dapat nandoon na tayo sa mode of

**[01:38:04.840] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001911)  
enabling our people na makatayo sa sarili nila. Kasi kung lagi na lang nating

**[01:38:12.040] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001912)  
binibigyan ng dole-outs, walang mangyayari. Lalaki nang lalaki yung utang natin dahil sa dole-outs natin.

**[01:38:17.560] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001913)  
Whereas ma-stimulate natin yung economy tapos mabigyan natin ng opportunity tumayo sa sarili nila.

**[01:38:22.920] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001914)  
Malamang hindi na hihingi ng dole-outs, kasi yun ang goal namin. I really commend your team doon sa Zamboanga del Norte, sa third district, kasi kahit weekends, kahit holidays, nagtatrabaho sila.

**[01:38:35.840] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001915)  
Kaya nagrereklamo din ako doon sa tinapyasan yung budget niyo for infrastructure,

**[01:38:41.020] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001916)  
kasi nakaikot na ako sa maraming opisina ng DSWD dito sa distrito ko. Kawawang-kawawa yung sitwasyon nila. Minsan isang kuwarto, siksikan sila parang sardinas. Pag kumilos

**[01:38:52.980] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001917)  
yung isa, kikilos

**[01:38:53.980] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001918)  
din yung iba sa dulo kasi nga siksikan na sila. Pati ang dami-daming

**[01:38:57.480] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001919)  
mga files na nandoon. So, Secretary, nag-request din kayo para sa augmentation sa infra niyo?

**[01:39:06.010] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001920)  
Mr. Chair, for

**[01:39:09.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001921)  
doon sa GASS na pondo na nawala rin. But I assure the good congressman, after

**[01:39:14.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001922)  
he raised the concern at nakita ko yung mga opisina sa kanilang lugar, that we've

**[01:39:18.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001923)  
already downloaded funds from the central office to repair those.

**[01:39:21.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001924)  
Admittedly, the regional director who is behind me again should have raised

**[01:39:27.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001925)  
the red flag and told us na wala na siyang MOOE so that we could have downloaded. But

**[01:39:31.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001926)  
we've addressed that concern. Pero in the long run, part ho iyan ng hinihingi

**[01:39:34.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001927)  
naming augmentation sa general services na budget namin.

**[01:39:39.820] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001928)  
Mr. Chair, hindi lang ito sa distrito ko, kundi sa buong

**[01:39:43.420] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001929)  
Pilipinas. Why? Because hirap naman nating i-expect na nakangiti, kailangan

**[01:39:47.800] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001930)  
nakangiti yung mga DSWD frontliners natin kasi nga

**[01:39:51.760] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001932)  
hinaharap nila marami ring problema eh. Pero napakahirap namang i-expect na nakangiti sila

**[01:39:56.920] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001933)  
kung pag umuulan eh iiwas-iwas sila sa tubig, tapos

**[01:40:00.640] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001934)  
kung napakainit and all. So para po matulungan naman ang mga taga-DSWD, mas

**[01:40:05.520] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001935)  
maraming kababayan natin. Siguro kasi nagrereklamo din yung mga kliyente niyo

**[01:40:10.060] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001936)  
pag pumupunta doon, minsan wala silang masilungan nang maayos. So,

**[01:40:14.120] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001937)  
Mr. Chair, I'd like to reiterate the need to help DSWD sa infra nila.

**[01:40:21.390] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001938)  
Mr. Chairman, much appreciated. Pero let me just point out again.

**[01:40:26.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001939)  
Naka-embed sa mga budget, nakuwento ko nga ito kay Congressman, naka-embed sa mga

**[01:40:31.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001940)  
budget ng mga region namin yung repairs and maintenance ng kanilang mga tanggapan.

**[01:40:35.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001941)  
Hindi naman ho zero yan eh. That's why the regional director in Region 9 got a

**[01:40:41.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001942)  
tongue-lashing and will get more tongue-lashing after this, because hindi

**[01:40:45.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001943)  
niya ginawa. It's in the initiative also of the regional director. But we

**[01:40:51.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001944)  
do appreciate the additional fund augmentation, of course, pero hindi naman

**[01:40:55.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001945)  
ho dapat umabot sa ganoon. Admittedly, yung pinakita niyo sa aking mga

**[01:40:58.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001946)  
litrato ng mga satellite office sa inyong distrito, hindi ho dapat umabot sa ganoon.

**[01:41:03.720] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001947)  
Pero Secretary, hindi yun yung intensyon ko. Intensyon ko ho naman po, support. Yes.

**[01:41:09.600] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001948)  
Tapos yun lang, Mr. Chair, yung number one concern ko lang talaga is SLP because nakita ko yung promise talaga nung

**[01:41:16.960] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001949)  
program. In fact, si Governor Uy ng Zamboanga del Norte also recognizes

**[01:41:20.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001950)  
the promise of

**[01:41:21.540] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001951)  
SLP sa pagtulong lalo na sa mahihirap na mga probinsya. Alam ko yung sa mayayamang probinsya siguro hindi ganoon ka-significant yun.

**[01:41:28.820] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001952)  
But for us, yung mga constituents natin na gusto nilang makahanap din ng paraan na makatayo sa sarili nila,

**[01:41:34.680] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001953)  
na medyo may pride din sila, na gusto nilang umasenso sa sarili nilang pawis, malaking-malaking bagay yung SLP po.

**[01:41:41.100] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001954)  
Sana po yung pondo ng SLP ay madagdagan natin. Yun lang po.

**[01:41:44.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001955)  
Thank you again. I'd like to thank the good congressman for raising two very important things:

**[01:41:48.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001956)  
augmentation for SLP and augmentation for repairs and maintenance or MOOE namin.

**[01:41:52.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001957)  
Huwag ho kayong mag-alala, biro lang ho naman kanina yun.

**[01:41:56.760] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001958)  
Yun lang po. Hindi ko naubos yung time ko. Marami pang magtatanong. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:42:00.720] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001959)  
Thank you. The manifestation of Congressman Amatong is well noted.

**[01:42:05.720] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-001960)  
We move on with Representative Sara Elago.

**[01:42:11.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001961)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair, at magandang araw sa lahat.

**[01:42:14.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001962)  
Kasama sa presentation ng DSWD,

**[01:42:17.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001963)  
ang mga bagong programa tungkol sa Project SAFE,

**[01:42:21.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001964)  
Nandiyan din ang enhanced na ProtecTEEN, hindi bago, pero mas napaunlad.

**[01:42:26.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001965)  
Nandyan din ang programa natin para sa children with disabilities.

**[01:42:32.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001966)  
Magkano ang pondo nito para sa susunod na taon?

**[01:42:40.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001967)  
Mr. Chairman, right now, itong mga programa na ito are good programs na this year,

**[01:42:45.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001968)  
Nagkaroon ho ng tulong sa Kongreso at Senado na nakatanggap sila ng tig-25 million each.

**[01:42:53.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001969)  
Pero dahil sa NEP, inalis ho yun ulit, bumalik siya sa bare essentials na 50 million na paghahatian nilang tatlo.

**[01:43:01.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001970)  
Pero we also wrote the committee na baka puwede hong ibalik yung tig-25 million nila para mapalago natin itong mga programa na ito.

**[01:43:09.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001971)  
Mr. Chair, ano-ano yung maapektuhan ng mga activities kung hindi mapupondohan ito para sa susunod na taon?

**[01:43:17.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001972)  
Ang proposal ng DSWD nasa 25 million level pa rin.

**[01:43:21.390] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001973)  
Obviously, Mr. Chair, we wanted more.

**[01:43:23.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001974)  
Pero naunawaan na rin namin na may tight fiscal space.

**[01:43:28.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001975)  
So right now, hiningi na lang namin na sana mabalik lang sa 25 million kasi these are very good programs.

**[01:43:33.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001976)  
Ang mga target beneficiaries nito...

**[01:43:45.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001982)  
Yeah, the targets.

**[01:44:08.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001983)  
For the ProtecTEEN program that takes care of our young mothers, to make sure that they don't give up on their dreams,

**[01:44:15.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001984)  
and that their dignity is there,

**[01:44:17.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001985)  
and that they can pursue and become productive.

**[01:44:20.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001986)  
The total, the phase one of it is 415 young mothers for this year.

**[01:44:28.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001987)  
Ang maapektuhan siyempre kung hindi bumalik yan, yung 415 plus our projection sana na i-grow pa ito ng 841 nakaragdagang mga batang ina.

**[01:44:37.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001988)  
And then sana sa year three, another 1,128.

**[01:44:41.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001989)  
So all these numbers will not happen if we don't get back the funding that we had.

**[01:44:46.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001990)  
Now for Project Aruga, ito naman yung tulong natin sa mga pamilya na may mga anak na espesyal na pangangailangan,

**[01:44:52.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001991)  
ang tinitingnan natin na maaaring maapektuhan ay 3,390 households.

**[01:44:57.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001992)  
Now for Project SAFE,

**[01:45:00.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001993)  
ito ho kasi, it's an advocacy program that protects us from OSAEC, or Online Sexual Abuse and Exploitation of Children, and trafficking.

**[01:45:09.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-001994)  
Ang total na maapektuhan will be 3,417.

**[01:45:16.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001995)  
Mr. Chair, hindi dapat zero. Dapat ma-restore ang 25 million pesos.

**[01:45:20.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001996)  
Kung kaya pang dagdagan, dapat dagdagan para sa ProtecTEEN, Project SAFE at Project Aruga.

**[01:45:25.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001997)  
Ngayon naman, Mr. Chair, nabanggit kanina yung tungkol sa Haven for Women and Children.

**[01:45:31.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001998)  
Yung binanggit nyo po ba kaninang mga bilang para yun sa Haven for Women and Children na?

**[01:45:36.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001999)  
O magkahiwalay pa?

**[01:45:38.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002000)  
Women lang po ito. Women and children kasama.

**[01:45:42.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002001)  
Nasaan?

**[01:45:46.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002001-split-2)  
Mr. Chair, magkasabay po, women and children.

**[01:45:50.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002002)  
They're normally, yung mga binanggit po namin na numero 14 na DSWD Managed,

**[01:45:54.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002003)  
45 na LGU,

**[01:45:55.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002004)  
and then 54 na private that we also watch over.

**[01:46:02.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002005)  
May bilang po ba kayo nung bilang ng mga victim survivors clients?

**[01:46:07.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002006)  
Sa pampubliko at pampribado?

**[01:46:25.930] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002007)  
Mr. Chair, we'll furnish you a copy of the data being asked pag private at LGU ran.

**[01:46:31.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002008)  
What we have yung sa DSWD, 5,833.

**[01:46:36.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002009)  
5,833.

**[01:46:37.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002010)  
May na-identify ba kayo ng mga LGUs?

**[01:46:40.930] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002011)  
Na kinakailangan pang magtayo ng mga bagong social welfare facility?

**[01:46:47.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002012)  
Meron po, Mr. Chair, and we'll gladly furnish you.

**[01:46:49.290] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002013)  
Pero ang naging direction na ho namin is a PPP scheme.

**[01:46:52.930] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002014)  
If you can do PPP for healthcare, you can do PPP for social welfare and social protection.

**[01:46:58.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002015)  
Kasi una po, para magtayo, we need land.

**[01:47:00.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002016)  
And then pangalawa, may gestation period.

**[01:47:02.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002017)  
Ang construction time and all the permitting will take probably a year.

**[01:47:06.670] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002018)  
So mas mabilis po sana na i-augment na lang natin ulit yung nawala naming pondo

**[01:47:10.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002019)  
na in-allow na kami mag-cost of care na lang.

**[01:47:13.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002020)  
Yung mga ina-credit na namin, bigyan na lang natin sila ng mga kliyente

**[01:47:17.630] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002021)  
kasi they do it very well anyway at bayaran na lang po natin yung cost of care.

**[01:47:21.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002022)  
Mas mabilis po yun.

**[01:47:22.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002023)  
There are spare capacities for every segment, for every type of vulnerable community in the private swadas.

**[01:47:30.330] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002024)  
Mr. Chair, hintayin natin yung ulat tungkol doon sa na-ca-cater ng pampubliko at pampribado,

**[01:47:36.070] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002025)  
Kung magkano pa yung kinakailangan na pondo para ma-address.

**[01:47:40.290] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002026)  
Yung gap kung meron man sa mga lugar na nangangailangan ng mga shelters.

**[01:47:44.790] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002027)  
Kasi marami pa rin talagang mga victim-survivors ng abuse ang mga nangailangan ng mga temporary shelters

**[01:47:52.410] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002028)  
para meron silang ligtas na espasyo para makapagpagaling at matulungan tungo sa recovery.

**[01:47:59.470] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002029)  
Ngayon naman, nais kong magtanong sa Council for the Welfare of Children.

**[01:48:05.670] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002030)  
Kung mamarapatin ng ating Mr. Chair at ng Secretary.

**[01:48:09.450] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002031)  
Atungkol ho ito.

**[01:48:10.290] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002032)  
Ito sa implementation ng Juvenile Justice and Welfare Act.

**[01:48:15.190] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002033)  
Ano ko ang status ng implementation ito?

**[01:48:17.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002034)  
Mr. Chairman, again, let me be the one to answer if I can for our attached agencies.

**[01:48:23.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002035)  
I will not be answering for NAPC, PCUP, and NCSC for the record because they're merely supervised agencies.

**[01:48:30.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002036)  
Their executive directors will do so.

**[01:48:32.750] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002037)  
Mr. Chairman, itong usapin na ito naging napakainit dahil nga sa mga state ng mga pangyayari na hindi natin lahat ginugusto.

**[01:48:40.290] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002038)  
It's being implemented, pero there's room for improvement.

**[01:48:44.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002039)  
Hindi pa siya fully na i-implement.

**[01:48:46.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002040)  
Kaya nga pagdating sa panawagan para ibaba yung minimum age of criminal liability,

**[01:48:50.750] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002041)  
sinabi na namin for the record na hindi kami sang-ayon dahil hindi pa natin na fully implement.

**[01:48:55.290] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002042)  
Case in point, una po, kulang ho yung mga Bahay Pag-asa.

**[01:48:58.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002043)  
These bahay pag-asas that were tasked na itayo sa mga highly urbanized cities at mga provinces,

**[01:49:03.930] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002044)  
hindi pa ho natatayo.

**[01:49:05.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002045)  
Second, last year during the budget hearing, naluwabas na rin ho ito.

**[01:49:09.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002046)  
And that's why we innovated na kung hindi kaya ng mga lokal na pamalaan,

**[01:49:13.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002047)  
magtayo tayo ng mga regional bahay pag-asa.

**[01:49:16.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002048)  
You gave us a budget for five, a startup capital.

**[01:49:19.330] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002049)  
We are waiting for the SARO release.

**[01:49:21.930] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002050)  
Pero ready na ho kami to create those, to build those bahay pag-asa.

**[01:49:25.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002051)  
But again, baka sabihin nyo ang tagal.

**[01:49:28.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002052)  
The cost of care will also allow us to divert some of our clients to CICL private facilities.

**[01:49:37.430] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002053)  
Meron po niyan.

**[01:49:38.450] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002054)  
In fact, they do it.

**[01:49:39.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002055)  
So, unfortunately, the JJWA hasn't fully been implemented,

**[01:49:45.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002056)  
lalong-lalo na pagdating doon sa aspeto na giving them a second chance.

**[01:49:49.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002057)  
The restorative justice part of it.

**[01:49:52.250] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002058)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair, sa posisyon ng DSWD

**[01:49:56.070] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002059)  
na magkaroon ng buong-buo na implementation ng Juvenile Justice and Welfare Act.

**[01:50:03.290] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002060)  
Matugunan yung mga gaps, mabigyan ng mga pondo

**[01:50:06.150] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002061)  
ang mga kinakailangan na facilities at services.

**[01:50:09.310] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002062)  
At gayon din sa posisyon ng pagtutol sa pagpapababa ng minimum age of criminal responsibility.

**[01:50:18.590] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002063)  
Na posisyon din ng representasyon na ito.

**[01:50:21.270] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002064)  
Kaugnay niyan, Mr. Chair, mahingi po tayo sa DSWD:

**[01:50:25.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002065)  
Ano po yung mga priority doon sa mga gaps na dapat nating pondohan with urgency and necessity for the next year?

**[01:50:33.350] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002066)  
And then, next.

**[01:50:34.150] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002067)  
Gusto ko pong magtanong in particular dito sa AgriCamp facility

**[01:50:39.310] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002068)  
Na tinatayo sa Palawan.

**[01:50:41.530] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002069)  
Natapos na po ba ito?

**[01:50:44.190] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002070)  
At bakit po napili na lokasyon ang current location nito?

**[01:50:50.190] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002071)  
Una po, binigay po kasi yung lupa.

**[01:50:51.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002072)  
Nandun yung Bucor na property, yung sa Iwahig.

**[01:50:54.810] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002073)  
Napakalaki po ng kanilang lote.

**[01:50:56.970] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002074)  
And di ba, kung matatandan po natin, Mr. Chair,

**[01:50:58.990] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002075)  
ang magmamanage naman ng AgriCamp sa batas ay yung Bucor.

**[01:51:02.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002076)  
So, doon nilagay para malapit na sa kanilang existing operations

**[01:51:06.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002077)  
at napakalawak ng kanilang lupa.

**[01:51:09.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002078)  
So, binigay po nila yung parasela sa atin at ongoing po yung construction.

**[01:51:14.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002079)  
Admittedly, nagkaroon lang po kami ng konting hiccup na tigil ng konti.

**[01:51:17.950] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002080)  
Pero, we're now in the process of jump-starting again.

**[01:51:20.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002081)  
Ano po yung mga hiccup?

**[01:51:21.690] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002082)  
Number one, meron pong mga informal settlers na kailangan namin siguraduhin

**[01:51:26.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002083)  
na ang kanilang mga karapatan ay hindi namin na va-violate

**[01:51:30.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002084)  
kasi meron pong nakatira doon sa property ng Bucor.

**[01:51:33.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002085)  
And pangalawa, we made sure rin na sumusunod kami sa mga environmental standards

**[01:51:37.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002086)  
na naka-prescribe.

**[01:51:39.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002087)  
Dahil alam naman natin, napaka-sensitive pagdating sa Palawan.

**[01:51:45.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002088)  
Hindi lang, Mr. Chair, sa environmental standards.

**[01:51:48.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002089)  
Pero gusto ko hong matanong kung meron bang standards na naset

**[01:51:52.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002090)  
ang DSWD, ang CWC, ang JJWC, pagdating doon sa mismong location.

**[01:52:00.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002091)  
Ang concern po natin, sinabi nyo naman po, hindi ito pinili,

**[01:52:03.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002092)  
kundi ibinigay at Bucor ang nag-identify

**[01:52:08.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002093)  
at hindi po DSWD at CWC.

**[01:52:11.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002094)  
Ang concern lang po namin, nabangkit nyo na rin yung proximity

**[01:52:14.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002095)  
doon sa existing population sa iwahig prison and penal farm.

**[01:52:20.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002096)  
Nababahala po kami na yung mismong location

**[01:52:24.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002097)  
ay mas mataas na yung risk para sa stigma at discrimination

**[01:52:29.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002098)  
na maaaring maranasan ng mga convicted children

**[01:52:32.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002099)  
na mag-undertake ng rehabilitation doon sa area.

**[01:52:37.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002100)  
So yun po ang aming pangunahing concern.

**[01:52:39.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002101)  
Kaya namin tinatanong kung meron ba,

**[01:52:41.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002102)  
baka yung particular standards as regards choosing the location.

**[01:52:45.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002103)  
First, let me just, again, baka hindi ko nalinaw na mabuti,

**[01:52:48.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002104)  
ang desisyon na yun ay joint decision ng DSWD,

**[01:52:51.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002105)  
ng DOJ, BuCor, as well as nandoon ho yung JJWC.

**[01:52:55.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002106)  
Personally, ako nakapunta na ho ako doon sa property.

**[01:52:58.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002107)  
Una, maganda ho siya, kaaya-aya ho siya.

**[01:53:00.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002108)  
The setting kasi of an agri-camp has to be an open expanse.

**[01:53:04.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002109)  
Hindi yun man ho natin siya pwede ilagay sa limit.

**[01:53:06.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002110)  
Okay, for the record, 50 years later,

**[01:53:09.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002111)  
wala pa rin hong agri-camp ang bansa natin.

**[01:53:11.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002112)  
So this is the first time, admittedly, na magtatayo na tayo ng AgriCamp.

**[01:53:15.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002113)  
We've been violating that law over and over again.

**[01:53:18.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002114)  
Nakakatawa nga ho, nire-refer dapat ng mga judge sa mga agri-camp,

**[01:53:24.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002115)  
pero wala naman ang agri-camp ng bansa.

**[01:53:26.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002116)  
So in the end, mas nakakatakot yun ang nangyayari ngayon,

**[01:53:29.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002117)  
na nalalagay sila sa mga pasilidad na hindi naman dapat na natatagpuan yung mga kabataan natin.

**[01:53:34.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002118)  
So one, it was a joint decision.

**[01:53:36.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002119)  
Two, let me assure you na yung dati naming undersecretary at yung current namin na undersecretary,

**[01:53:41.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002120)  
na mga colleagues rin natin dito,

**[01:53:43.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002121)  
pinuntahan rin nila yun bago kami nag-sign off.

**[01:53:46.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002122)  
Si former undersecretary Villar and current undersecretary Dimaporo.

**[01:53:50.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002123)  
Lahat po yan, palahat kami pinuntahan namin.

**[01:53:52.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002124)  
Maganda ho yung lugar, kaaya-aya.

**[01:53:55.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002125)  
And ma'am, if I can give you the picture,

**[01:53:57.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002126)  
sobrang laki nung property ng Iwahig.

**[01:54:00.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002127)  
Napakalayo rin niya kung nasaan yung main Iwahig.

**[01:54:03.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002128)  
Nasa peripheral siya, it's a mountainous area,

**[01:54:05.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002129)  
perfect for an agriculture setting.

**[01:54:08.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002130)  
So we assure you na meron ho kaming pag-aaral dyan.

**[01:54:11.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002131)  
Standards naman, hindi ho yan lalagpas sa department,

**[01:54:14.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002132)  
lalo na sa standards department namin,

**[01:54:16.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002133)  
if the designs weren't meant to be restorative,

**[01:54:20.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002134)  
rehabilitative, with a dream later on for reintegration.

**[01:54:26.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002135)  
Maraming salamat ho.

**[01:54:27.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002136)  
Abukad sa nabanggit ko na na-concern,

**[01:54:29.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002137)  
yung tungkol din sa lapit niya sa maaring mga families, relatives,

**[01:54:34.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002138)  
na bibisita dun sa mga convicted children.

**[01:54:37.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002139)  
Dahil ito ay nasa malayong lugar,

**[01:54:39.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002140)  
patuloy natin hong babantayan,

**[01:54:41.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002141)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:54:42.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002142)  
at kung pwede rin mag-schedule ang ating sariling committee

**[01:54:46.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002143)  
for the welfare of children ng bagong ocular inspection

**[01:54:49.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002144)  
para tignan yung mga developments dun sa area.

**[01:54:52.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002145)  
At kung maaari, huwag na ho nating isama

**[01:54:54.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002146)  
kasi lagi ho, pag nakikita ko sa mga statements,

**[01:54:57.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002147)  
nakadikit ho yung construction ng agri-camp dun sa pangalan ng Iwahig.

**[01:55:02.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002148)  
Again, yung risk po ng stigma at discrimination dahil dito.

**[01:55:06.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002149)  
Well noted, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:55:07.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002150)  
Just as a point of information though,

**[01:55:09.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002151)  
isa to sa marami na ini-envision,

**[01:55:11.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002152)  
meaning hindi naman ito yung isa lang,

**[01:55:14.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002153)  
na una lang talaga.

**[01:55:16.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002154)  
And the dream is to build one in Luzon,

**[01:55:18.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002155)  
build one in Mindanao,

**[01:55:19.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002156)  
even if Palawan's in Luzon,

**[01:55:21.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002157)  
pero mas malapit rin siya sa proximity ng Visayas,

**[01:55:24.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002158)  
para nga ho sa accessibility ng mga pamilya.

**[01:55:26.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002159)  
Maraming salamat.

**[01:55:27.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002160)  
At tungkol naman, Mr. Chair,

**[01:55:29.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002161)  
sa implementation ng Expanded Solo Parents Welfare Act,

**[01:55:33.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002162)  
lahat ba ng LGUs ay nakapagbibigay ng monthly cash subsidy

**[01:55:37.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002163)  
para sa low-income solo parents?

**[01:55:40.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002164)  
May rekomendasyon bang,

**[01:55:41.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002165)  
ng DSWD

**[01:55:42.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002166)  
para yung mga hindi pa nako-cover

**[01:55:44.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002167)  
ay makabenefisyo mula sa batas na ito?

**[01:55:47.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002168)  
Mr. Chairman,

**[01:55:47.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002169)  
if my recollection serves me right,

**[01:55:49.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002170)  
it's the fund availability line is in that law.

**[01:55:52.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002171)  
So talagang alam rin natin,

**[01:55:54.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002172)  
may mga local government units tayo

**[01:55:55.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002173)  
na hirap to fund that mandate.

**[01:55:58.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002174)  
Nakakalungkot man isipin,

**[01:56:00.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002175)  
pero meron talaga mga LGU na hindi pa nagko-comply.

**[01:56:04.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002176)  
We are pushing them,

**[01:56:05.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002177)  
we are monitoring them,

**[01:56:06.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002178)  
pero kung mabibisita natin yun,

**[01:56:09.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002179)  
hindi funding allocation kasi,

**[01:56:11.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002180)  
depends on the fund availability sa LGUs.

**[01:56:14.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002181)  
So this is the number: of the total 1,658,

**[01:56:18.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002182)  
478 lamang ang mga nagko-comply.

**[01:56:21.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002183)  
Ito yung mga highly urbanized cities

**[01:56:23.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002184)  
at mga component cities na first class.

**[01:56:25.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002185)  
So we really want to ask Congress to look into it

**[01:56:29.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002186)  
na baka ho matulungan natin itong mga LGUs na ito

**[01:56:31.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002187)  
or to somehow make it a priority.

**[01:56:34.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002188)  
Sa tamang oras, Mr. Chair,

**[01:56:35.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002189)  
magpapropose ho tayo ng special provision

**[01:56:37.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002190)  
para matiyak na may pondo

**[01:56:39.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002191)  
kahit po mauna natin,

**[01:56:41.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002192)  
yung mga LGUs,

**[01:56:42.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002193)  
3rd, 4th, at 5th class municipalities

**[01:56:43.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002194)  
para sa monthly cash subsidy

**[01:56:45.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002195)  
para sa mga low-income solo parents.

**[01:56:48.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002196)  
Ngayon naman bilang panghuli,

**[01:56:49.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002197)  
magkano ho ang pondo

**[01:56:51.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002198)  
para sa child development centers,

**[01:56:53.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002199)  
child minding centers,

**[01:56:54.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002200)  
at supervised neighborhood play?

**[01:56:57.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002201)  
Mr. Chairman, I don't know

**[01:56:58.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002202)  
because hindi na po sa amin

**[01:57:00.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002203)  
ang ECCD child minding.

**[01:57:02.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002204)  
Nung dumating ako sa department,

**[01:57:04.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002205)  
inalis po sa amin,

**[01:57:05.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002206)  
nilagay sa DepEd,

**[01:57:06.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002207)  
and then later on sa DILG.

**[01:57:08.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002208)  
Unfortunately, I don't have access

**[01:57:09.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002209)  
to that information

**[01:57:09.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002210)  
or I don't know that information.

**[01:57:11.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002211)  
Okay, pero meron ho kayong

**[01:57:13.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002212)  
mga current set of standards

**[01:57:15.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002213)  
and policies na pinapatupad.

**[01:57:18.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002214)  
Pwedeng masumite sa committee na ito?

**[01:57:20.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002215)  
Mr. Chair, hindi po sa amin na

**[01:57:22.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002216)  
yung standards nila

**[01:57:23.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002217)  
na sa ECCD Council na po.

**[01:57:25.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002218)  
Ang natira na lang ho sa amin

**[01:57:26.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002219)  
yung universal feeding

**[01:57:27.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002220)  
ng mga daycare centers,

**[01:57:28.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002221)  
the nutritional side of it.

**[01:57:30.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002222)  
Okay, so niisa ho sa mga ito

**[01:57:31.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002223)  
ay wala hong pondo ang DSWD?

**[01:57:35.260] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002224)  
Because, Mr. Chair,

**[01:57:36.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002225)  
inalis na po sa amin

**[01:57:37.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-002226)  
yung mandate na yun.

**[01:57:38.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002227)  
Alright, maraming salamat ho.

**[01:57:40.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002228)  
Maraming salamat.

**[01:57:41.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002229)  
Mr. Chair, and I will register

**[01:57:42.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002230)  
for the second round.

**[01:57:43.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002231)  
Thank you.

**[01:57:43.700] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-002232)

My question now is about

Itong social pension

**[02:15:08.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003096)  
Thank you, Secretary. Mr. Chair, gusto ko rin pumalaman.

**[02:15:11.600] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003097)  
Ang social pension budget for 2027 is at around P51.6 billion.

**[02:15:16.880] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003098)  
Yet the target number of beneficiaries actually increases by about 151,775 to reach 4.24 million indigent seniors.

**[02:15:26.700] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003099)  
Given that this is a statutory entitlement, can you confirm that a flat budget is sufficient to cover more beneficiaries without reducing the per-person pension amount or creating new waitlists?

**[02:15:37.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003100)  
Mr. Chairman, it is not enough. Sa waitlist lang or dun sa listahan ng CBMS na pwede pa mag-qualify, that's around half a million already.

**[02:15:45.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003101)  
On top of that, sa mga LGU at katabi hunyo yung isa nating representative who's been championing universal social pension,

**[02:15:52.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003102)  
I think the right way of looking at it is to add more money for social pension.

**[02:15:56.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003103)  
Para ho, makover natin yung mga karapat dapat.

**[02:16:00.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003104)  
Ang hinihingi ho namin dyan, 6.8 billion to cover only those in the CBMS.

**[02:16:06.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003105)  
But if you are going to look at the waitlist sa ground, napakarami pa ho.

**[02:16:11.860] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003106)  
Before I conclude my interpolation, I want to note something positive.

**[02:16:16.980] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003107)  
Even if may mga budget cut po tayo sa DSWD, napansin ko po na tumaas ang ating personal services.

**[02:16:23.120] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003108)  
And its share of the budget grows from 6.3 to about 8.7.

**[02:16:26.700] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003109)  
And this reflects the DBM-approved enhanced structure.

**[02:16:31.240] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003110)  
And does this mean na yung roughly yung mga JO po natin na city and municipal link,

**[02:16:35.640] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003111)  
some of whom have served the department for over a decade,

**[02:16:38.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003112)  
will finally transition into a more contractual or permanent position?

**[02:16:42.440] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003113)  
And if so, I would like to put on record that this is a genuine win for the department

**[02:16:48.260] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003114)  
dahil kinikinala po natin ang sipag ng ating mga city and municipal link.

**[02:16:53.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003115)  
It is, Mr. Chairman, and we'd like to thank Congress and Senate

**[02:16:56.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003116)  
for championing it.

**[02:16:58.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003117)  
Dagdag na 5,000 plus na kulang-kulang 5,700 na bagong regular positions yun.

**[02:17:06.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003118)  
That will go to the four-piece cluster, whether you're a social worker or a development worker,

**[02:17:11.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003119)  
with a promise kaagad na may buntot pa siyang another 5,000.

**[02:17:17.060] Rep. JC M. Abalos** (asr-003120)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair. Maraming salamat po, Secretary Rex.

**[02:17:20.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003121)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:17:22.140] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003122)  
Thank you, Ko Abalos.

**[02:18:56.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003154)  
Mr. Chair,

**[02:18:57.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003155)  
sekretary, while shifting to e-wallets

**[02:19:00.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003156)

**[02:19:24.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003163)  
Atlat po ba?

**[02:19:25.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003164)  
Ang mga programa nito ay patungo na po

**[02:19:28.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003165)  
sa digitalization ng payout?

**[02:19:31.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003166)  
May text ng text sa akin po kasi, sekretary,

**[02:19:33.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003167)  
mga mahihirap nating questions sa Bulacan.

**[02:19:36.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003168)  
Paano daw natin masisiguro na ang mga binibisaryo natin

**[02:19:40.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003169)  
na walang bangko, smartphone, o internet

**[02:19:43.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003170)  
ay hindi mapag-iiwanan during payouts?

**[02:19:45.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003171)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:19:46.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003172)  
That's correct, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:19:47.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003173)  
For different programs, iba-ibang strokes po.

**[02:19:50.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003174)  
Sa walang gutong program, it's fully digital

**[02:19:52.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003175)  
kasi from day one, digital na yun.

**[02:19:54.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003176)  
For PoorPeace, ganun din ho.

**[02:19:56.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003177)  
Pero admittedly, doon sa social protection

**[02:19:59.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003178)  
like AICS, which bahagi ang Uplift,

**[02:20:01.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003179)  
we're still in the process of doing it.

**[02:20:04.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003180)  
Bakit po?

**[02:20:05.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003181)  
Kasi tulad dito sa Uplift, natuklasan namin,

**[02:20:07.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003182)  
katulad ng Pinoy in doubt nyo,

**[02:20:09.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003183)  
napakaraming mahihirap nating kababayan ng unbanked.

**[02:20:12.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003184)  
Wala silang bank account.

**[02:20:14.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003185)  
So dito sa Uplift, ang goal ho sana namin

**[02:20:17.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003186)  
that entire 2.5 million na Group 2, sana digital na.

**[02:20:22.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003187)  
Pero nung nag-payout ho kami for validation

**[02:20:25.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003188)  
para kunin yung kanila mga GCash account or ATM accounts,

**[02:20:28.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003189)  
only 20% of them ang may mga bank account.

**[02:20:32.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003190)  
So we've been working with the BSP, Banko Sentral,

**[02:20:37.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003191)  
si Deputy Governor Bernard Romulo Puyat

**[02:20:43.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003192)  
to make sure na on this payout sa Uplift,

**[02:20:46.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003193)  
nandun na yung mga banko at GCash.

**[02:20:48.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003194)  
We're enrolling them already.

**[02:20:50.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003195)  
Nahihirapan ho kami kasi gustuhin man namin

**[02:20:53.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003196)  
na mag-digital.

**[02:20:54.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003197)  
Tulad na na-point out nyo,

**[02:20:55.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003198)  
napakarami sa kanila ang walang banko

**[02:20:57.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003199)  
at nanganganib na mapag-iwanan

**[02:20:59.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003200)  
kapagka pipilitin namin yung digital oramismo.

**[02:21:02.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003201)  
Pero sa mga darating na buwan,

**[02:21:03.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003202)  
parami ng parami na ho,

**[02:21:04.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003203)  
20% na yung may bank accounts.

**[02:21:06.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003204)  
So hopefully we can get more than that 20%

**[02:21:08.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003205)  
para maglipat na tayo sa digital format.

**[02:21:11.610] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003206)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair Secretary,

**[02:21:16.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003207)  
for that response.

**[02:21:18.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003208)  
Pangalawang tanong ko po,

**[02:21:19.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003209)  
so last na lang po ito,

**[02:21:20.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003210)  
regarding po doon sa

**[02:21:21.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003211)  
launch of decentralized

**[02:21:24.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003212)  
PWID database

**[02:21:26.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003213)  
with NCDA.

**[02:21:28.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003214)  
How will the DSWD

**[02:21:30.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003215)  
audit existing

**[02:21:32.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003216)  
LGU-issued PWD

**[02:21:34.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003217)  
and senior citizens' cards

**[02:21:36.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003218)  
to purge fake IDs currently exploiting

**[02:21:39.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003219)  
municipal discount benefits?

**[02:21:41.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003220)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:21:42.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003221)  
Mr. Chairman,

**[02:21:43.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003222)  
right now this program is being

**[02:21:45.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003223)  
thought of carefully.

**[02:21:46.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003224)  
We're working with the

**[02:21:48.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003225)  
LGUs actually in the BIR

**[02:21:50.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003226)  
para masigurado natin seamless yung launch niya.

**[02:21:53.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003227)  
Meron tayong mga LGUs na nag-adapt na,

**[02:21:55.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003228)  
ready na sila.

**[02:21:56.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003229)  
Pero again, alam natin na masalimuot to

**[02:21:59.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003230)  
because even in the law on the PWD ID,

**[02:22:01.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003231)  
ang issuing agent talaga is the LGU,

**[02:22:04.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003232)  
not the national government.

**[02:22:06.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003233)  
So we don't want to infringe

**[02:22:08.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003234)  
in that devolved function.

**[02:22:10.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003235)  
Pero it doesn't mean na hindi tayo gagawa ng hakbang.

**[02:22:13.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003236)  
Patuloy tong programa na to,

**[02:22:15.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003237)  
pero tulad din sa inyo,

**[02:22:16.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003238)  
we are moving with utmost caution.

**[02:22:18.270] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003239)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair,

**[02:22:22.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003240)  
Secretary,

**[02:22:23.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003241)  
for that response.

**[02:22:25.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003242)  
Mr. Chairman,

**[02:22:27.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003243)  
the DSWD serves as the social conscience

**[02:22:31.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003244)

**[02:22:32.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003245)

**[02:22:34.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003246)  
to vulnerable Filipinos.

**[02:22:36.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003247)  
And within this framework,

**[02:22:38.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003248)  
the National Commission for Senior Citizens

**[02:22:41.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003249)  
stands as a cornerstone

**[02:22:43.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003250)  
in safeguarding the dignity,

**[02:22:45.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003251)  
welfare, and rights of our elderly population.

**[02:22:48.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003252)  
I therefore manifest

**[02:22:50.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003253)  
my full support

**[02:22:51.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003254)  
for the proposed budget

**[02:22:53.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003255)  
of the Department of Social Welfare and Development

**[02:22:56.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003256)  
and the National Commission for Senior Citizens.

**[02:22:59.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003257)  
Kung pwede sana, Mr. Chair,

**[02:23:00.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003258)  
sa mga kasama ko rito

**[02:23:02.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003259)

**[02:23:04.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003260)  
dagdagang po natin

**[02:23:05.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003261)  
kung pwede natin dagdagang pa sana ang kanilang budget.

**[02:23:08.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003262)  
Tulog po rin ni our good friend,

**[02:23:10.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003263)  
Congressman Rufus Rodriguez,

**[02:23:12.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003264)  
sigurado,

**[02:23:13.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003265)

**[02:23:15.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003266)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:23:17.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-003267)  
Salamat, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:23:19.220] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003268)  
Thank you.

**[02:23:20.220] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003269)

**[02:23:22.220] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003270)

**[02:23:25.220] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003271)  
Rep. Twitchy Niño.

**[02:23:28.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003272)  
Thank you po.

**[02:23:34.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003273)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[02:23:35.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003274)  
Meron po tayong presentation.

**[02:23:40.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003275)  
Ayan.

**[02:23:41.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003276)  
Okay.

**[02:23:42.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003277)  
So ang unang mga tanong po natin ay

**[02:23:45.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003278)  
mas may kinalaman sa national policy

**[02:23:48.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003279)  
as expressed in the DSWD budget.

**[02:23:52.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003280)  
Specifically on poverty alleviation.

**[02:23:55.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003281)  
So kinuha po natin yung datos

**[02:23:57.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003282)  
ng mga tawagin nating core ayuda programs

**[02:24:01.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003283)  
ng DSWD from 2016 to 2026.

**[02:24:05.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003284)  
Tapos nandyan din yung proposed ng 2027.

**[02:24:09.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003285)  
So kasama dyan, pinagsama-sama natin yung

**[02:24:13.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003286)  
sa pantawid, 4Ps, AICS, at saka SLP,

**[02:24:18.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003287)  
plus also some others,

**[02:24:20.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003288)  
yung ayuda ACAP, yung food stamp program,

**[02:24:25.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003289)  
at saka yung LITAO.

**[02:24:27.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003290)  
Pero parang tawagin nating mas minor yung mga ngayon.

**[02:24:30.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003291)  
Okay. Anong nakita natin dito?

**[02:24:33.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003292)  
Well, una, yung paglaki.

**[02:24:35.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003293)  
Over a decade, 2016 to 2026.

**[02:24:40.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003294)  
Noong 2016, last year of the Aquino administration,

**[02:24:45.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003295)  
73 billion yung total na ayuda sa ilalim ng DSWD.

**[02:24:49.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003296)  
2026 is na 184 billion.

**[02:24:53.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003297)  
Tapos makikita nyo rin dun sa color coding

**[02:24:56.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003298)  
yung mga share ng iba't ibang programa.

**[02:25:00.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003299)  
Yung blue, yun yung 4Ps.

**[02:25:03.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003300)  
Yung orange, yung AICS.

**[02:25:08.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003301)  
Okay. Lipat tayo.

**[02:25:11.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003302)  
The next tab is NEP versus GAA.

**[02:25:15.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003303)  
Okay. So it's the same.

**[02:25:17.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003304)  
Kaya lang kinumpere lang natin yung NEP as against GAA over the decade.

**[02:25:23.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003305)  
Anong masasabi lang natin dito?

**[02:25:25.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003306)  
Na actually from 2016 to 2020...

**[02:25:30.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003307)  
Sorry.

**[02:25:32.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003308)  
2016 to 2021,

**[02:25:37.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003309)  
ay more or less kung ano yung pinropose ng NEP na ayuda,

**[02:25:43.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003310)  
yun din ang ina-approve ng Kongreso.

**[02:25:47.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003311)  
Sa GAA.

**[02:25:49.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003312)  
Pero napansin natin from 2022,

**[02:25:52.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003313)  
pagpasok huling taon ng Duterte,

**[02:25:55.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003314)  
at lalo na sa ilalim ng Marcos Jr. administration,

**[02:25:58.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003315)  
ay naging significant yung tinatawag minsan insertions

**[02:26:06.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003316)  
ng Kongreso.

**[02:26:08.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003317)  
Pinapalaki significantly yung ayuda.

**[02:26:11.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003318)  
So nakikita natin yan.

**[02:26:12.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003319)  
Especially in 2024 and 2026.

**[02:26:16.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003320)  
Yung yellow, yung nasa taas na yun,

**[02:26:19.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003321)  
that's ACAP.

**[02:26:21.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003322)  
Na pinasok talaga yan ng Kongreso.

**[02:26:23.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003323)  
Okay.

**[02:26:24.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003324)  
Then the next tab,

**[02:26:27.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003325)  
dito yung ilang mga punto natin.

**[02:26:29.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003326)  
One,

**[02:26:30.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003327)  
yung total ayuda budget na covered dito

**[02:26:33.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003328)  
ay lumaki po, Mr. Chair,

**[02:26:35.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003329)  
by 152%.

**[02:26:39.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003330)  
So 152% ang inilaki ng ayuda budget

**[02:26:45.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003331)  
ng DSWD.

**[02:26:47.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003332)  
73 billion 2016,

**[02:26:49.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003333)  
184 billion 2026.

**[02:26:53.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003334)  
So unang tanong,

**[02:26:55.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003335)  
actually,

**[02:26:57.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003336)  
tanong sa Kongreso as much as

**[02:26:59.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003337)  
DSWD.

**[02:27:01.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003338)  
Wala tayo po tayong duda na

**[02:27:03.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003339)  
especially under Secretary Gatchal Yan,

**[02:27:05.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003340)  
ay generally very efficient ang DSWD

**[02:27:09.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003341)  
sa pag-implement ng mga programang ito.

**[02:27:12.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003342)  
Mas ang tanong po natin ay,

**[02:27:14.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003343)  
ano ang masasabi natin

**[02:27:17.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003344)  
sa ganitong mahigit pagdoble

**[02:27:21.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003345)  
ng ayuda budget?

**[02:27:23.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003346)  
Hindi ba pinapakita nito

**[02:27:25.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003347)  
yung mga problema at limitasyon

**[02:27:28.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003348)  
ng patakaran ng poverty alleviation?

**[02:27:31.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003349)  
Bakit?

**[02:27:33.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003350)  
Dahil yes, may natulungang mga mahirap.

**[02:27:37.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003351)  
May mga maaaring, sabi ng DEV-DEV,

**[02:27:40.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003352)  
nabawasan na raw ang bilang ng mahirap sa Pilipinas.

**[02:27:44.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003353)  
Maaaring totoo, may mga nagkokontest nun,

**[02:27:47.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003354)  
pero nabawasan dahil

**[02:27:50.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003355)  
nakadepende sa ayuda.

**[02:27:53.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003356)  
Hindi sa trabaho,

**[02:27:55.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003357)  
hindi sa kabuhayan.

**[02:27:58.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-003358)  
Di po ba ganun yun? Anong masasabi ng DSWD dyan?

**[02:28:03.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003359)  
Mr. Chairman,

**[02:28:05.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003360)  
the points are well taken.

**[02:28:07.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003361)  
Kami kasi in the department,

**[02:28:10.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003362)  
we want to look at certain things,

**[02:28:12.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003363)  
if we can go back to those slides.

**[02:28:14.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003364)  
For instance, yung conditional cash transfer yung 4Ps.

**[02:28:17.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003365)  
Other people will call it ayuda

**[02:28:19.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003366)  
while we call it investment in human capital.

**[02:28:22.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003367)  
Kasi may condition po yan eh.

**[02:44:01.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003814)

**[02:44:04.130] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003815)  
Number two, obviously,

**[02:44:05.670] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003816)

**[02:44:23.750] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003825)

information

**[02:44:31.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003829)

**[02:44:33.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003830)

**[02:44:35.670] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003831)

**[02:44:37.850] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003832)

**[02:44:39.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003833)  
I'm glad nga na unti-unti in this

**[02:44:41.970] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003834)

**[02:44:44.050] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003835)

**[02:44:46.010] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003836)

**[02:44:48.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003837)

**[02:44:50.570] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003838)  
So, is it safe to say, Mr.

**[02:44:52.110] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003839)  
Chair, that the good secretary

**[02:44:53.470] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003840)

**[02:44:55.170] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003841)

**[02:44:58.010] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003842)
