**[02:44:58.550] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003842-split-2)  
A hundred and one percent, Mr. Chair.

**[02:45:00.540] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003843)  
Versus AICS?

**[02:45:02.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003843-split-2)  
Both.

**[02:45:03.000] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003843-split-3)  
So, speaking about AICS, Mr. Chair, what will be the effect

**[02:45:10.720] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003844)  
Mr. Chair, if we forego or reduce the budget for the protective services for individuals and families, Mr. Chair?

**[02:45:18.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003845)  
mr chairman displacement we have a slide that i've been wanting to show since this morning

**[02:45:24.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003846)  
If we can pull it out.

**[02:45:26.400] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003846-split-2)  
Because, Mr. Chair, I'd like to add so that Secretary can also include it in

**[02:45:33.940] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003847)  
his explanation, because unlike AICS, there are several studies which were pointed out recently,

**[02:45:41.660] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003848)  
earlier. There is a World Bank study, there is PIDS, but can you also cite if there is also

**[02:45:47.800] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003849)  
a similar study as to the effect of AICS? Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:45:51.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003849-split-2)  
Number one, Mr. Chairman, the fear natin, this is a

**[02:45:54.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003851)  
tabular format. In 2025, the allotment was 44 billion, pero nakita niyo may other releases pa

**[02:46:00.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003852)  
from unprogrammed na 14 billion. We were augmented actually kasi kinapos tayo

**[02:46:06.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003853)  
because, tulad na sinabi ko, there are unforeseen events like volcano eruptions, RSSI, these type of things

**[02:46:14.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003854)  
na ginagamit namin ang 4Ps. Ah, sorry, ang AICS. So in that year alone, we were able to serve 9 million,

**[02:46:20.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003855)  
9.4 million beneficiaries

**[02:46:23.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003856)  
Mga may mukha, mga pangalan 'to. These are actual beneficiaries. In 2026, when Congress heeded our call last year,

**[02:46:30.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003857)  
you gave us 63.4 billion, of which 70% has already been obligated and we're in the process

**[02:46:37.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003858)  
of using that. Ang naging target natin is 8.7 million Filipino individuals who are coming to us day in and day out

**[02:46:46.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003859)  
out in our numerous offices. Now if we stick to the NEP level of 33 billion,

**[02:46:53.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003860)  
ang projected na service gap, meaning yung tatanggihan natin dahil wala na tayong pera,

**[02:46:57.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003861)  
is 4,085,631. And these are not mere numbers; these are real people

**[02:47:04.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003862)  
in your congressional districts, in your cities, na sasabihin namin, pasensya na po, lumiit po kasi yung pondo. So we are pleading

**[02:47:11.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003863)  
with Congress and later on in the Senate na i-restore ho to 63 billion yung pondo. Now I think there's

**[02:47:19.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003864)  
a next slide. May next slide to?

**[02:47:22.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003865)  
Now,

**[02:47:23.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003866)  
I want to point out that when the energy emergency broke out this year,

**[02:47:28.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003867)  
that nobody saw, this is also the funding allocation for the uplift assistance.

**[02:47:34.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003868)  
The close to 2 million drivers nationwide,

**[02:47:38.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003869)  
of the jeep, tricycle, TNVS, service delivery,

**[02:47:43.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003870)  
all of those were received by 5,000 because we used the AICS.

**[02:47:47.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003871)  
The AICS sort of serves as a standby fund for unforeseen events.

**[02:47:51.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003872)  
If that wasn't there, if you didn't raise it to 66 billion, 63 billion last year,

**[02:47:58.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003873)  
we would have been finished by the summer and we wouldn't have reached our allocation

**[02:48:02.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003874)  
for the crises that we are facing now.

**[02:48:07.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003875)  
So again, let me just put on record that we are asking Congress,

**[02:48:12.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003876)  
as well as the Senate later on,

**[02:48:14.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003877)  
to reinstate the budget of AICS to its current levels of 63.4 billion pesos.

**[02:48:20.260] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003878)  
Thank you.

**[02:48:24.100] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003879)  
Mr. Chair, so to end, thank you very much once again to DSWD.

**[02:48:29.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003880)  
For Congresswoman or more, 63 billion or more.

**[02:48:33.260] Deputy Speaker Mercedes K. Alvarez** (asr-003881)  
We fully support that, Mr. Chair. Thank you.

**[02:48:38.350] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003882)  
Thank you, Kong Chia-Ding. Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:48:47.460] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-003883)  
We now recognize Representative Bong Soon-Tai.

**[02:48:53.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003884)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[02:48:56.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003885)  
Secretary, magandang tanghali po.

**[02:49:00.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003886)  
Siyempre sa, to the DSWD family.

**[02:49:04.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003887)  
Well, una sa lahat.

**[02:49:07.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003888)  
Well, as we all know,

**[02:49:10.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003889)  
disaster has struck our country in numerous times.

**[02:49:13.320] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003890)  
Madaming, well, nabanggit ni Sec pag erap ng bulkan.

**[02:49:19.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003891)  
Merong mga sunog na nagganap, merong mga typhoon, which we were hit.

**[02:49:26.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003892)  
So kaya ako lang nabanggit to, una, nais kong magpasalamat sa DSWD, as we all know.

**[02:49:32.200] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003893)  
Government should be there when it's needed.

**[02:49:36.890] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003894)  
On time and available at yung pagtulong.

**[02:49:40.950] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003895)  
Pagdating dun sa disaster, masasabi ko na talagang on time.

**[02:49:45.350] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003896)  
Nasaksihan ko yan nung nagkabagyo sa aking distrito.

**[02:49:50.230] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003897)  
Ready naman yung food boxes ng DSWD.

**[02:49:54.710] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003898)  
It was readily available.

**[02:49:56.270] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003899)  
Andon yung mga sleeping kits, tsaka yung ready to eat.

**[02:50:03.510] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003900)  
Na pagkain, which was readily available.

**[02:50:08.450] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003901)  
I believe it was like that in the whole of Metro Manila

**[02:50:12.050] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003902)  
because andito yung warehouses ng DSWD.

**[02:50:17.110] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003903)  
Ang tanong ko lang, sa probinsya po kaya, I believe that would be a problem.

**[02:50:21.410] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003904)  
Yung logistics and warehouse, paano yung response ng DSWD sa mga ibang lugar naman?

**[02:50:28.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003905)  
Na hindi readily accessible itong mga warehouse at saka yung mga trucking requirements.

**[02:50:33.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003906)  
Mr. Chairman, that's a very good question.

**[02:50:36.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003907)  
Under this administration, binaguhu talaga yung paradigm on disaster response.

**[02:50:40.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003908)  
Hindi tayo naghahanda o nag-repack pag nandyan na yung sakuna.

**[02:50:44.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003909)  
Rather, for the first time in our country's history, we now operate close to 1,040 warehouses nationwide.

**[02:50:54.810] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003910)  
And these are different sizes warehouse.

**[02:50:56.490] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003911)  
Merong nasa syudad that we call hubs.

**[02:50:58.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003912)  
Merong kami mid-size hubs rin na nasa mga secondary cities.

**[02:51:01.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003913)  
And we have last mile warehouses.

**[02:51:03.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003914)  
As far as northern Palawan, in fact, alam niyo ni Congresswoman Salvame, or the GIDA areas.

**[02:51:10.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003915)  
And the second paradigm change is, tulad na sinabi ko, 365 days a year, we repack nationwide.

**[02:51:15.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003916)  
We have two repacking centers.

**[02:51:17.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003917)  
We're building the third one now in Mindanao because of Congress's approval of a multi-year allocation for it.

**[02:51:22.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003918)  
So meaning, the goods are being drawn from bigger packaging centers in the three island groups.

**[02:51:28.810] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003919)  
So meaning, naka-omnipresent, naka-palat na huyan.

**[02:51:31.330] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003920)  
Nung sumali kami sa departamento nung 2022, before my time, Secretary Erwin was already building on it.

**[02:51:39.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003921)  
After six months, we carried on his mission.

**[02:51:41.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003922)  
In fact, nung kaming dalawa, ang dinatan natin dyan na national stockpile ng food packs ay nasa mga 200,000 lang.

**[02:51:49.150] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003923)  
At the height, before this sunod-sunod na bagyo, we ran 5.2 million family food packs nationwide.

**[02:51:56.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003924)  
Not just in Metro Manila, but all over the country.

**[02:51:59.230] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003925)  
So ganyan karami yung national stockpile.

**[02:52:01.330] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003926)  
And for this month alone, we've already released 3.6 million food packs nationwide.

**[02:52:08.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003927)  
Kasi tulad nyo na sabi ko, may stockpile tayo.

**[02:52:11.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003928)  
Yet the remaining stockpile now still stands at 3.2.

**[02:52:14.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003929)  
Meaning, we're running our factories beyond its capacity na 22,000.

**[02:52:19.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003930)  
We're reproducing, responding, and stockpiling all at the same time.

**[02:52:23.170] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003931)  
Okay. Thank you very much, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:52:25.830] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003932)  
Well, kagaya ng sinabi ko, pagdating dyan talagang nararamdaman.

**[02:52:31.330] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003933)  
Kikita.

**[02:52:32.250] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003934)  
Now, the protective social welfare budget for the SWD was decreased from 131.6 billion in 2026.

**[02:52:41.590] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003935)  
Bumaba ito to 101.4 billion.

**[02:52:47.650] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003936)  
What component program was affected by this?

**[02:52:51.450] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003937)  
Mr. Chairman, the biggest is the assistance to individuals in crisis situation.

**[02:52:54.990] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003938)  
33 billion yung nawala ho doon.

**[02:52:57.950] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003939)  
30 billion.

**[02:53:01.330] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003940)  
Okay, 30 billion yung nawala.

**[02:53:03.350] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003941)  
Pero actually, nadagdag yan.

**[02:53:04.690] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003942)  
Kasi if you remember, I think it was noong 2025, we dinifund natin yung ACAP.

**[02:53:12.050] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003943)  
Pero inabsorbed ng AICS yung beneficiary ng ACAP.

**[02:53:17.150] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003944)  
But have we fixed the problems noong pag-absorb ng AICS sa ACAP program?

**[02:53:28.210] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003945)  
Dahil there were some co-affirming.

**[02:53:30.250] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003946)  
They identified.

**[02:53:31.330] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003947)  
They identified na yung aid distribution, nagkakaroon tayo ng duplication.

**[02:53:39.110] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003948)  
May mga beneficiaries, nakakuha na ng AICS, nakatanggap pa ulit ng ACAP.

**[02:53:44.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003949)  
Mr. Chairman, that has been addressed.

**[02:53:46.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003950)  
We've already explained that fully to COA.

**[02:53:49.190] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003951)  
And the fiscal targets naman, if you're going to talk about the fiscal targets,

**[02:53:54.150] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003952)  
we've met the fiscal targets noong kinonsolidate lahat sa AICS for last year.

**[02:53:57.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003953)  
As you pointed out, as the good congressman pointed out,

**[02:54:00.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003954)  
na inaugment yung AICS.

**[02:54:02.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003955)  
So yun yung pinakamalaki na nawala this year.

**[02:54:04.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003956)  
30 billion.

**[02:54:06.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003957)  
Yung inintroduce na amendment ng congress, yun yung nawala rin.

**[02:54:12.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003958)  
Okay, but what controls have we now placed?

**[02:54:15.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003959)  
Paano nagiging distribution ngayon natin ng AICS?

**[02:54:19.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003960)  
So per region, I think you posted it, but on a per district level, sino yung nagiging beneficiary?

**[02:54:25.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003961)  
Who gets the biggest chunk of this AICS?

**[02:54:28.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003962)  
How do we distribute?

**[02:54:29.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003963)  
Or allocate this fund?

**[02:54:31.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003964)  
Mr. Chairman, it's always followed the same principle.

**[02:54:34.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003965)  
It's on a need base and assessed by the social worker.

**[02:54:36.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003966)  
We get referrals every day from everywhere.

**[02:54:39.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003967)  
And it's really up to the frontline social services, the social workers,

**[02:54:43.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003968)  
to see if we were to say yes or no to a particular request.

**[02:54:47.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003969)  
So would it be safe to say that ngayon mas regulated na natin, hindi na po magagaya?

**[02:54:52.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003970)  
For example, as a matter of fact, doon po sa distrito ko, during the previous congress,

**[02:54:57.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003971)  
ang AICS pinang babayad po sa, pinang pe-payout yan sa mga nagpupunta doon sa mga pre-election rally, campaign.

**[02:55:06.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003972)  
In fact, naging leaflets pa nga po yan eh. Pre-filled up na yung form.

**[02:55:13.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003973)  
Nakalagay na lang yung pangalan ng beneficiary at yung date. Tapos binabahay-bahay.

**[02:55:18.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003974)  
So have we corrected that? Dahil yung AICS is a stopgap for individuals na talagang kailangan ng tulong.

**[02:55:25.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003975)  
And it should not be used that way. Dahil kawawa naman yung mga taong kailangan talagang matulungan na nangangailangan ng tulong.

**[02:55:35.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003976)  
Mr. Chairman, we totally agree. And we have tightened the implementing guidelines.

**[02:55:40.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003977)  
May mga anti-EPAL, may mga CLARO na you can't use it for your own interest or your political gains.

**[02:55:46.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003978)  
It's now enshrined both in the GAAA and at the same time our guidelines.

**[02:55:51.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003979)  
And we take these things seriously. Kakasuhan yung mga empleyado.

**[02:55:54.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-003980)  
Meron rin hong ganung provision sa loob ng aming administrative codes in the department.

**[02:55:59.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003981)  
Okay. Mr. Chairsec, as pinakita kanina ni Congressman Tino yung graph,

**[02:56:08.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003982)  
and I was listening to your answer and very happy to hear that ngayon ang ginagawa natin.

**[02:56:15.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003983)  
Because yung support for SLTs and developmental programs talagang napaka-importante.

**[02:56:22.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003984)  
Ang ayaw natin is masanay talaga yung mga tao sa dole-outs.

**[02:56:25.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003985)  
We want the aid coming from the government to really affect and help the families.

**[02:56:34.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003986)  
While I understand talagang hindi naman pwedeng tanggalin because there are some immediate and emergency needs and assistance na kailangan ibigay at makuha ng mga tao.

**[02:56:45.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003987)  
But slowly, we also have to integrate. Ngayon nawala na yung ACAP.

**[02:56:51.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003988)  
Sinama natin sa IX yung 4Ps. I'm talking about yung 4Ps. Sana mas mapalakas natin yung 4Ps.

**[02:56:57.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003989)  
Dahil yung 4Ps, ang beneficiaryo ng mga 4Ps, kailangan sumunod ka sa RA 11310.

**[02:57:06.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003990)  
There are strict guidelines, components to this, regulations that has to be followed.

**[02:57:12.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003991)  
And makikita mo may result because it's not really a dole-out for them.

**[02:57:17.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003992)  
It encourages and incentivizes families who are benefitting.

**[02:57:21.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003993)  
So sana ito mapalaki. As correctly pointed out, yung ating sustainable livelihood program, lumeit yung budget.

**[02:57:29.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003994)  
Pero gusto nga natin palakasin yung mga ganitong programa eh.

**[02:57:33.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003995)  
So maybe we should put some of the, kung maglalagay ang Congress ng budget, baka dapat dito ilagay instead of additional IX na naman.

**[02:57:45.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003996)  
Dahil ang gusto natin, slowly.

**[02:57:49.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003997)  
I think it was pointed out.

**[02:57:52.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003998)  
Nung nagsimula yung IX program nung panahon ng pandemia, because during the lockout, yung mga extreme lockdowns natin, talaga kahit yung mga gusto magtrabaho, hindi makapagtrabaho.

**[02:58:06.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003999)  
After a while, nung natapos yung pandemic, hindi nawala yung programa. In fact, mas lumaki pa ito.

**[02:58:13.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004000)  
But ang ayaw natin sana mangyari is mapag-iwanan yung mga...

**[02:58:17.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004001)  
...yung mga...

**[02:58:19.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004002)  
...developmental programs which would really lift the family from poverty or help them in a way.

**[02:58:27.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004003)  
Mr. Chairman, we agree.

**[02:58:29.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004004)  
The points are well taken.

**[02:58:32.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004005)  
Just as a quick rundown.

**[02:58:34.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004006)  
Tama ko kayo that we've been tightening the four-piece, strengthening it.

**[02:58:38.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004007)  
In fact, the World Bank last study said nga that it's contributed directly to 2.2 reduction in poverty.

**[02:58:43.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004008)  
That's a sign that our implementation has been tightened.

**[02:58:47.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004009)  
But I also want to point out na...

**[02:58:48.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004010)  
Ninabanggit ko ito kanina kay Congressman Tino.

**[02:58:51.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004011)  
Yesterday's PSA briefing was quite revealing.

**[02:58:54.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004012)  
Sabi nila na the contributors or the drivers to poverty reduction, one, reduce inflation pre-2026, but also the growth in household income.

**[02:59:05.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004013)  
There were five main reasons to it.

**[02:59:08.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004014)  
One was yung wages tumaas.

**[02:59:14.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004015)  
Pangalawa, entrepreneurship.

**[02:59:16.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004016)  
So nandun na huhulog yung mga SLP natin.

**[02:59:19.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004017)  
Ibig sabi nakakatulong sila.

**[02:59:20.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004018)  
And not just us.

**[02:59:21.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004019)  
From the small business corporation of DTI, the whole government of approach na DAR, DA, has countryside livelihood.

**[02:59:27.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004020)  
Lahat yun na pagsasama-sama to entrepreneurship output.

**[02:59:31.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004021)  
Third lamang, or isa sa pang lima, yung conditional cash transfer na local, which is four-piece.

**[02:59:37.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004022)  
So meaning, and other transfers, meaning the targeting is working.

**[02:59:41.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004023)  
Nakakontribute siya pero hindi natin nga gusto yun.

**[02:59:44.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004024)  
And the other two is foreign remittances and pension.

**[02:59:48.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004025)  
So kung lima yan, sabay ho, nandun yung entrepreneurship and nandun yung cash transfer.

**[02:59:54.090] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004026)  
And like I agree with you, na dapat we have to wean off the cash transfer gradually while strengthening entrepreneurship.

**[03:00:01.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004027)  
Okay. And lastly, yung tanong ko lang sa 4Peace. Sir, ilan yung current beneficiary natin ng 4Peace? Ilan yung bagong enrolled sa 4Peace programs? At ilan naman yung mga graduate na sa 4Peace programs?

**[03:00:16.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004028)  
For the record, nung nagsimula ho ang Marcos administration, 4.4 million ho yan. Laging 4.4. So nagtanong ho, since 2014. So since 2014 till 2022, ang napag-graduate lamang po ay 50,000.

**[03:00:32.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004029)  
We're not saying na walang umangatang buhay doon, but they weren't taking the exit mechanism seriously. But under the Marcos Jr. administration, simula ni Sen. Erwin, ngayon naman tinuloy namin, talagang pinag-igtingin natin yung case management.

**[03:00:46.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004030)  
Para yung mga dapat lumabas, lumalabas na. So from 2022 till now, 1.7 million families have already exited. Now, ang natinahong sa database natin, from minus that number, is at around 3 million.

**[03:01:00.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004031)  
Of which, if we are to peg it at the current poverty incidence, dapat 3.5 million na lang sila. Hindi nababalik sa 4.4. So that means by October, we're going to enroll an additional 500,000.

**[03:01:13.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004032)  
Okay. So, as I mentioned earlier,

**[03:01:16.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004033)

**[03:01:39.940] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004034)

**[03:01:46.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-004035)  
yung mga food boxes were readily available at walang nahalong politika. Napunta sa tao yung tulong na kinakailangan nila. Maraming salamat.

**[03:03:31.030] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004060)  
Mr. Secretary,

**[03:03:34.330] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004061)  
just for clarification po,

**[03:03:38.070] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004062)

**[03:03:40.410] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004063)

**[03:03:47.110] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004065)

**[03:03:48.590] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004066)

**[03:03:50.410] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004067)

**[03:03:58.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004068)  
Mr. Chairman, masalimuot ho yung sagot.

**[03:04:02.130] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004069)  
I'll try to make it as concise as possible.

**[03:04:04.190] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004070)  
We will submit to the good committee to be furnished to the good congresswoman

**[03:04:07.690] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004071)  
yung legal query ng department sa OP.

**[03:04:11.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004072)  
Kung saan sinabi nila, so to speak, na status quo muna po.

**[03:04:15.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004073)  
Meaning we supervise them, but we should not,

**[03:04:18.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004074)  
we should not,

**[03:04:20.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004075)  
we should not directly meddle at this point in time because of the legislation passed.

**[03:04:25.550] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004076)  
Pero naka-footnote rin doon that the law on optimization has taken into effect

**[03:04:30.490] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004077)  
and meron na rin hong IRR ng optimization.

**[03:04:34.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004078)  
So meaning nothing stops the department from submitting an optimization plan

**[03:04:38.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004079)  
up until na-approve yung optimization plan

**[03:04:40.450] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004080)  
tsaka lang namin makukuha yung full control and authority over NCSC.

**[03:04:44.490] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004081)  
So for now, it's status quo na independent ho sila sa amin.

**[03:04:47.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004082)  
They are merely coordinating with us.

**[03:04:50.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004083)  
For programs and for budget purposes.

**[03:04:53.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004084)  
For budget purposes also.

**[03:04:55.990] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004085)  
For budget purposes, nakasama ho sila sa araw pero ng talakay,

**[03:04:59.850] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004086)  
pero nakahiwalay ho naman silang line item.

**[03:05:02.490] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004087)  
Kasi sa batas po kasi, under ng Office of the President, ang NCSC.

**[03:05:11.030] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004088)  
So coordination lang po sa DSWD.

**[03:05:16.290] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004089)  
Coordination lamang po.

**[03:05:18.250] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004090)  
That's been very clear by the legal opinion.

**[03:05:20.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004091)  
The opinion of the Office of the President when we did the query like yourself.

**[03:05:24.450] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004092)  
Yes po. Kasi sa sulat po kasi ni Executive Secretary Ralph Recto,

**[03:05:29.570] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004093)  
lateral relationship, parang coordination lang po ng programs and parties.

**[03:05:34.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004094)  
Correct. But if you look at that same letter, meron ho yung caveat on optimization.

**[03:05:39.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004095)  
So lumabas na ho recently yung IRR ng optimization.

**[03:05:42.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004096)  
So we did submit our optimization plan that will include NCSC.

**[03:05:45.930] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004097)  
Okay. Thank you. Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:05:50.410] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004098)  
And at least na-klaro po kung ano po ang ano ng NCSC.

**[03:05:55.850] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004099)  
And number two po, marami po akong reklamong natanggap na sobrang bagal at tagal po

**[03:06:02.550] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004100)  
ng accreditation process ng mga home care at elderly facilities.

**[03:06:08.710] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004101)  
Ano po kaya ang nagiging problema?

**[03:06:11.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004102)  
I can tell you point blank that we've streamlined it already.

**[03:06:15.270] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004103)  
Kami na ho siguro may pinaka maikling timetable kapag ka kompleto at pumapasok.

**[03:06:20.410] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004104)  
Pumapasa. Minsan ho kasi nagre-reklamo pero I can show you if you can give us the names

**[03:06:24.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004105)  
na minsan hindi ho pumapasa and we have to be very careful.

**[03:06:28.150] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004106)  
Hindi ho pwedeng i-insist na gusto nila, gusto nila because kami naman ho ang madadali.

**[03:06:32.150] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004107)  
Kung may pinalusot kaming accreditation na hindi pumapasa sa standards prescribed by the department.

**[03:06:38.150] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004108)  
Alam niyo ho, Mr. Chairman, it used to take almost six months to one year

**[03:06:41.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004109)  
to get a SWADA accredited, registered license and accredited.

**[03:06:46.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004110)  
Kami, as fast as two months time.

**[03:06:49.290] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004111)  
Tapos?

**[03:06:49.930] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004112)  
Yes.

**[03:06:50.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004113)  
As long as you pass the standards.

**[03:06:52.430] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004114)  
Kaya lang minsan ho kasi they will insist na pasado sila but our recommendation is hindi sila pasado.

**[03:06:57.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004115)  
So doon ho talaga tumatagal but we do provide technical assistance.

**[03:07:01.550] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004116)  
If susundan naman nila yung mga recommendation namin, mapapabilis at makukuha nila yung kanilang accreditation.

**[03:07:07.350] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004117)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:07:09.350] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004118)  
And digital rin ho ang aming application.

**[03:07:12.390] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004119)  
Streamlined, meaning when you register, sabay na ho ang license to operate.

**[03:07:16.050] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004120)  
So two weeks, ay two months lang.

**[03:07:18.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004121)  
Kung pumapasa po.

**[03:07:20.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004122)  
And it's all done online.

**[03:07:21.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004123)  
Hindi na humano-mano na dinadaanan sa office yung mga requirements.

**[03:07:25.310] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004124)  
Thank you.

**[03:07:26.810] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004125)  
Number three, Secretary, under our existing laws po, mayroon pong 1% budget allocation for senior citizens at PWDs

**[03:07:37.530] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004126)

**[03:07:41.650] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004127)

**[03:07:48.050] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004128)  
Madam Chair,

**[03:07:50.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004129)

**[03:07:54.390] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004130)

**[03:08:00.590] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004131)  
So, nagpapakinabangan naman po ng mga senior citizens.

**[03:08:06.190] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004132)  
And slash PWD.

**[03:08:08.550] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004133)  
Thank you.

**[03:08:10.250] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004134)

**[03:08:15.610] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004135)

**[03:08:20.170] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004136)

**[03:08:25.290] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004138)

**[03:08:28.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004139)

**[03:08:34.070] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004140)

**[03:08:39.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004141)

**[03:08:41.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004142)  
Ibig sabihin, sa dami ho na kailangan i-accommodate, kalahati lang ho lagi taon-taon ang naibibigay sa amin.

**[03:08:48.010] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004143)  
Pangalawa, siyempre ho minsan lack of information,

**[03:08:50.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004144)

**[03:08:57.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004145)  
na hindi nila naunawaan na pag may ganun ka na kasi maliit lang naman, feeling nila entitled sila

**[03:09:02.430] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004146)  
and we personally agree, naaalis sila sa listahan.

**[03:09:05.490] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004147)

**[03:09:09.170] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004148)

**[03:09:13.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004149)

**[03:09:18.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004150)  
pero pag nasa birth registry ka at wala ka naman sa death registry at ikaw ay matanda na, kasama ka ho kagad.

**[03:09:26.150] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004151)  
Okay po, kasi yun nga po, yung mga nagbawas kayo, kasi dapat lang humagbawas kayo

**[03:09:31.450] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004152)  
kasi may mga nagpe-pension, may mga medyo mayaman na tumatanggap, no?

**[03:09:36.650] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004153)

**[03:09:41.430] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004154)

**[03:09:46.230] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004155)  
Again,

**[03:09:48.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004156)  
Again, we have to be, we have to look at the age kasi yung bagong guidelines namin, age muna.

**[03:09:54.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004157)  
I think you would agree na unahin natin yung pinakamatatanda.

**[03:09:57.990] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004158)  
So yung mga na-free up na slot, hinahanap namin sa CBMS yung 90 pababa

**[03:10:02.850] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004159)  
and then inuunti-unti namin malagay sila sa listahan.

**[03:10:09.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004160)  
So it's not geographic in short, it's age.

**[03:10:13.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004161)  
So titignan namin yung CBMS at yung mga waitlist, unahin natin yung mga pinakamatatanda

**[03:10:17.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004162)  
and I think that's the most equitable.

**[03:10:19.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004163)  
At this point in time, nakalahati lang yung funded nung mandate.

**[03:10:22.920] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004164)  
Siguro mas maganda na ho, siguro universal social pension.

**[03:10:26.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004165)  
And we totally agree 101%.

**[03:10:29.000] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004166)  
Ang mga seniors, lahat na po sila tatanggap na po.

**[03:10:33.280] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004167)  
So, paano po ba karami ang ma-existing at operational senior citizens

**[03:10:39.220] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004168)  
o elderly facilities natin nationwide?

**[03:10:42.600] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004169)

**[03:10:49.420] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004170)

**[03:10:57.120] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004171)

**[03:11:03.060] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004172)

**[03:11:21.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004174)

**[03:11:23.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004175)  
Pero tulad din ang paliwanag ko kanina, we are now taking, based on last year's budget,

**[03:11:27.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004176)  
we are now taking a PPP approach to it.

**[03:11:29.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004177)  
Meaning?

**[03:11:30.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004178)  
Meron na kaming cost of care provision.

**[03:11:32.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004179)  
Kung saan, kung meron namang private na pwede natin doon i-refer,

**[03:11:36.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004180)  
DSWD ho magbabayad ng cost of care.

**[03:11:38.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004181)  
Kasi mas mabilis, mas effective yung pagtatayo ng gano'n.

**[03:11:41.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004182)  
We're almost done with the guidelines.

**[03:11:43.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004183)  
We're just waiting for the final go signal from the DBM.

**[03:11:47.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004184)  
Yeah.

**[03:11:48.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004185)  
Four for the nationally-run residential care facility.

**[03:11:53.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004186)  
Three for the LG-run residential care facility.

**[03:11:57.260] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004187)  
Seven na po lahat.

**[03:11:58.580] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004188)  
So, sana po matagdagan natin.

**[03:12:00.820] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004189)  
Kasi ang dami po tayong abandoned and homeless senior citizens.

**[03:12:04.700] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004190)  
And we agree.

**[03:12:06.240] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004191)  
Thank you po.

**[03:12:07.800] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004192)  
At nito pong mga nakaraang mga linggo, sunod-sunod po ang mga pagulan at pagbaha

**[03:12:15.060] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004193)  
dahil sa habagat.

**[03:12:17.040] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004194)  
Nasusunod po ba sa distribution ng mga relief goods or family food packs

**[03:12:22.140] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004195)  
na priority ang mga senior citizens?

**[03:12:25.200] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004196)  
Tila hindi po ito nangyayari, Secretary.

**[03:12:27.700] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004197)  
Parang hindi po talagang mahal ng DSWD ang mga senior citizens.

**[03:12:30.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004198)  
Mahal na mahal namin ang mga senior citizens, lalo na po kayo.

**[03:12:36.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004199)  
Mr. Chairman, we have to remember.

**[03:12:40.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004200)  
Sa batas sa Local Government Code, ang first responder po talaga ang mga local government units.

**[03:12:46.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004201)  
Ang ginagawa lamang ng DSWD is to augment kasi kulang ang kapasidad ng mga LGU.

**[03:12:52.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004202)  
We're proud to say that this tong mga habagat, 3.6 million na family food packs ang lumabas in a span of four months.

**[03:13:00.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004203)  
Ibinabagsak po natin yun sa mga local government units for them to do the last mile distribution.

**[03:13:05.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004204)  
Dahil wala naman ho kaming sapat na tao to do that or know-how ng terrain.

**[03:13:09.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004205)  
Now, we constantly remind our local social welfare offices to prioritize vulnerable sectors.

**[03:13:17.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004206)  
Seniors, people with disability, indigenous people, pregnant and lactating women.

**[03:13:22.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004207)  
Pati sa camp management kung saan nagaganap ang pinakamarami na aktividad during an evacuation,

**[03:13:29.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004208)  
kine-remind din ho namin sila sa protocols.

**[03:13:30.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004209)  
Kasi, we set the standards, they execute it, we regulate them.

**[03:13:36.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004210)  
Pero sa 3.6 million na sana all na ho yun.

**[03:13:39.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004211)  
So, we would assume na dapat nabigyan ho ng mga local government units yung kanila mga senior.

**[03:13:44.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004212)  
And alam ko mahal na mahal ng mga local government units ang kanila mga senior citizens.

**[03:13:48.240] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004213)  
Yes, because seniors are the most vulnerable sectors when it comes to calamity.

**[03:13:52.520] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004214)  
So, for the record, Mr. Chair, I just want to put this in the record that I would like to...

**[03:14:00.820] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004215)  
to file a complaint against Region 1 Director Angela Gopela for her arrogance and her...

**[03:14:11.100] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004216)  
and doing injustice to the senior citizens.

**[03:14:14.740] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004217)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:14:16.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004218)  
Mr. Chairman, we will accept and we will commission the necessary fact-finding

**[03:14:19.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004219)  
and go through our administrative process and we will report back to the committee.

**[03:14:24.580] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004220)  
Thank you. She doesn't deserve that position.

**[03:14:28.380] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004221)  
Thank you. Thank you, Kuwang Mila.

**[03:14:30.820] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004222)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. That's all.

**[03:14:36.200] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004223)  
Maybe, can we take up already the budget of the NCOC or later?

**[03:14:41.060] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004224)  
Maybe later. I think it's already incorporated.

**[03:14:45.360] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-004225)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:15:07.860] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004226)  
We now recognize Cong. Elijah San Fernando. Is he around? No. So, Representative Edgar Erice...

**[03:15:27.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004227)  
Is he around? Representative Iris Montes.

**[03:15:35.990] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004227-split-2)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Um...

**[03:15:38.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004228)  
Magandang tanghali po, Secretary Rex, and to the whole DSWD family. Most of my questions

**[03:15:46.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004229)  
have been previously asked and have been adequately answered by the secretary so i just have

**[03:15:52.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004230)  
one simple question but before um i i asked that um i was just requested by our colleague

**[03:16:00.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004231)  
Congresswoman Johanne Monich Bautista to ask about her concern

**[03:16:05.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004232)  
Tungkol po ito, Secretary, sa QRF.

**[03:16:09.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004233)  
Sa presentation po kasi mapapansin natin,

**[03:16:12.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004234)  
like for 2025,

**[03:16:14.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004235)  
yung regular niyo po

**[03:16:16.100] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004236)  
is 1.25 billion. Tapos nagkaroon po ng replenishment ng 7.8. Sa 2026, 3 billion po, nagkaroon ng replenishment na 9.6. Ang concern lang po ng ating colleague ay for 2027, 1.25 lang daw po. Hindi po ba daw kukulangin? Assured po daw tayo ba na kahit maliit ito ay magre-replenish ulit?

**[03:16:42.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004237)  
Mr. Chairman, it's a very good question. Nagagalak ho kami sa tanong na 'yan. Actually, may assurance naman ho na may pondo kasi replenishable. Pero siyempre sa panahon ng sakuna dapat mabilis ho kami, maagap. So nakikiusap ho kami, sumulat rin kami sa committee na sana ma-restore back to its 3 billion level. At dati napagbigyan naman ho kami para mas mabilis kami na nakaka-responde, lalo na sabay-sabay ho yung sakuna. It's no longer contained in one area; it's multi-region.

**[03:17:10.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004238)  
So sana ho mabalik ho talaga kami.

**[03:17:11.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004239)  
We are earnestly asking the committee to consider because ho

**[03:17:16.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004240)  
maaapektuhan yung turnaround time namin sa pag-responde, pag-procure, paglatag ng emergency cash transfer.

**[03:17:24.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004241)  
And I want to point out, para lang sa kaalaman ng lahat, wala namang dagdag na cost sa gobyerno dahil nandun lang siya nakalagak sa QRF ng national.

**[03:17:32.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004242)  
So, sana lang lakihan yung seed capital ng QRF namin para mas mabilis kaming makaresponde.

**[03:17:37.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004243)  
Thank you, Secretary. This representation shares your sentiments and I do support that we restore your 2026 QRF levels.

**[03:17:49.420] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004244)  
Moving on to my question, a simple question, Mr. Chair.

**[03:17:57.580] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004245)  
The DSWD has repeatedly faced challenges in ensuring timely and targeted delivery of social protection programs.

**[03:18:07.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004246)  
Yan po ay nasabi nyo na, nabanggit nyo na during the lead committee hearings at kanina po, parang nire-iterate po ninyo uli.

**[03:18:17.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004247)  
Dahil po, meron tayong fragmented and disconnected data system.

**[03:18:24.180] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004248)  
So, wala po tayong real-time na accurate na data.

**[03:18:30.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004249)  
So, medyo nahihirapan po tayo to roll out sa part po ninyo yung mga social protection programs.

**[03:18:37.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004250)  
Ano po?

**[03:18:37.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004251)  
That is why this representation, ang kababaihan kabalikatan para sa kapakanan at kaunlaran ng bayan, actively participated during the lead committee hearings.

**[03:18:50.680] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004252)  
Out of that, the 4K has filed at least six or seven interventions, one of which is House Bill 9084.

**[03:19:00.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004253)  
It calls for the establishment of a national and integrated registry system.

**[03:19:07.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004254)  
That is interoperable to address this gap.

**[03:19:11.340] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004255)  
So, the system establishes the foundational mechanism for efficient, transparent, and responsive governance.

**[03:19:17.640] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004256)  
It also proposes a system that builds on existing national initiatives, such as the Philippine identification system and social registries, transforming them into a unified data ecosystem that supports inclusive growth and digital transformation.

**[03:19:37.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004257)  
So, it also addresses the long-standing fragmentation of government data systems, which limit efficiency and effectiveness of public service delivery.

**[03:19:48.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004258)  
But beyond Mr. Secretary, a single registry, what the country truly needs is an integrated yet interoperable system that connects administrative registries like LGU household records, barangay level data,

**[03:20:05.700] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004259)  
data, sa civil registration and identification systems naman po, nakapaloob sana doon yung

**[03:20:12.560] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004260)  
PhilSys, birth and death records, and national ID.

**[03:20:17.380] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004261)  
Dapat meron din po sigurong component na sectoral and program-based registries.

**[03:20:23.260] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004262)  
Nandoon po yung 4Ps, yung senior citizen, yung PWDs, meron pong disaster response list,

**[03:20:30.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004263)  
education, and labor database.

**[03:20:32.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004264)  
Sa social registry naman po, dapat meron mga poverty targeting mechanism,

**[03:20:38.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004265)  
household vulnerability mapping, meron din pong PhilHealth, GSIS, SSS, DepEd, DOLE, and LGU databases.

**[03:20:48.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004266)  
So, only by linking the systems can we ensure real-time, accurate, and inclusive targeting of beneficiaries,

**[03:20:56.660] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004267)  
while also, kapaloob din po doon sa bill, pag-safeguard ng data privacy and accountability.

**[03:21:02.120] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004268)  
So, ang simpleng tanong ko lamang po, Mr. Secretary, does the department view this integration as a step towards the right direction?

**[03:21:13.780] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004269)  
And will the Secretary support this reform?

**[03:21:17.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004270)  
Mr. Chairman, in fact, we are doing a small pilot inside the department, a social registry that integrates our own databases

**[03:21:25.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004271)  
from 4Ps, Walang Gutong, the program databases.

**[03:21:28.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004272)  
But it would be nice if it's scaled up to a national level.

**[03:21:31.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004273)  
And we're fully supportive.

**[03:21:33.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004274)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair.

**[03:21:37.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004275)  
I will furnish you po a copy of my bill.

**[03:21:40.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004276)  
And I will appreciate if you can take a good look at it.

**[03:21:45.460] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004277)  
And siguro po, you can share, you can review, send some comments and inputs to improve the bill.

**[03:21:53.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004278)  
And if you find it helpful and necessary, perhaps I will just request the Secretary to bring it up, perhaps, to the Executive.

**[03:22:01.160] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004279)  
Department for its passage or for it to be a priority of this Congress.

**[03:22:10.060] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004280)  
Thank you, Secretary Rex.

**[03:22:12.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004281)  
Mr. Chair, we'll gladly accept that we already registered our full support to the bill in writing early on.

**[03:22:18.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004282)  
So, we'll gladly accept and see if there are new angles that you want us to comment on.

**[03:22:23.940] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-004283)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, that would be all.

**[03:22:27.840] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004284)

**[03:23:32.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004299)

**[03:23:34.710] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004300)

**[03:23:42.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004301)

**[03:23:45.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004302)  
Nabanggit na po kanina, kagaya po nung AICS, hindi ko na po babanggitin kasi I believe

**[03:23:49.710] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004303)

**[03:24:01.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004305)

**[03:24:09.010] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004306)

**[03:24:11.510] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004307)

**[03:24:17.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004308)

**[03:24:25.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004309)

**[03:24:29.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004310)

**[03:24:36.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004311)  
On behalf lang po ng ating mga workers po.

**[03:24:40.410] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004312)  
Mr. Chair, kasi po siguro the good secretary can explain.

**[03:24:48.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004313)

**[03:24:56.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004314)

**[03:25:02.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004315)

**[03:25:08.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004316)

**[03:25:09.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004317)

**[03:25:17.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004318)

**[03:25:25.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004319)

**[03:25:34.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004320)  
mga nagtatrabaho nating mga kababayan.

**[03:25:37.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004321)  
That they are also covered.

**[03:25:39.310] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004322)  
And entitled to the programs of the DSWD.

**[03:25:42.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004323)  
Mr. Chairman, I just want to again point out na ang AICS ho is for everybody.

**[03:25:46.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004324)  
It looks at the crisis situation more than the income level.

**[03:25:50.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004325)  
So lagi namin sinasabi magtungo lang ho sa aming mga tanggapan para ma-assess ng social

**[03:25:55.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004326)  
worker natin.

**[03:25:56.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004327)  
And in fact, I just don't have the data with me.

**[03:25:58.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004328)  
We will gladly furnish the committee.

**[03:26:00.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004329)  
A percentage, a real significant percentage of AIICS really goes to those above the middle

**[03:26:05.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004330)  
class.

**[03:26:06.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004331)  
So those above the poverty threshold, the near poor.

**[03:26:08.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004332)  
All the way to 50%.

**[03:26:09.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004333)  
I just don't have the percentages right now but we'll send it to you.

**[03:26:13.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004334)  
It goes to show na yung crisis in crisis situation yung tinitignan.

**[03:26:19.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004335)  
Now number two, the president also instructed us to widen the lalo na ngayong energy emergency.

**[03:26:25.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004336)  
That's why the uplift assistance has three components to it of which yung pangatlo is

**[03:26:31.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004337)  
targeting low wage earners.

**[03:26:33.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004338)  
These are above minimum wage earners ng SSS.

**[03:26:36.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004339)  
That's around 1.5 million.

**[03:26:38.210] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004340)  
Ang na-service na namin around 800,000 kasi tulad na nabanggit ko kanina dormant yung beneficiary

**[03:26:46.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004341)  
accounts ng marami sa kanila.

**[03:26:48.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004342)  
But we're working on that with SSS to try to look for more of them para ma-exhaust natin

**[03:26:53.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004343)  
yung 1.5 the monthly allocation na tigta 2,000 para sa ating mga SSS low wage earners.

**[03:27:00.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004344)  
Thank you po.

**[03:27:01.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004345)  
And thank you for that explanation po from our Secretary Rex Gatchalian.

**[03:27:06.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004346)  
I think yun lang po.

**[03:27:07.210] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004347)  
I think yun lang po ang aking ipo-point out.

**[03:27:09.110] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004348)  
Nasagot naman po.

**[03:27:11.110] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004349)  
And again, I just wanted to manifest our sincere appreciation to the good Secretary and the

**[03:27:18.110] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004350)

**[03:27:22.110] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004351)
