additional budget of the DSWD.

**[03:27:33.110] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-004354)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:27:35.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004355)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:27:37.210] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004356)  
Thank you.

**[03:27:41.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004357)  
We now recognize Representative Krisel Lagman.

**[03:27:49.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004358)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:27:50.980] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004359)  
Hindi pa kumakain si Secretary.

**[03:27:52.840] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004360)  
Iba talaga pag rooted sa local government ang head ng department.

**[03:28:01.840] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004361)  
Lalong-lalo na pagdating sa social services.

**[03:28:04.840] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004362)  
Damang-dama po namin ang hands-on approach

**[03:28:10.840] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004363)  
and holistic approach ng inyong paggogobyerno, Secretary Rex.

**[03:28:14.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004364)  
Ang Tabaco, Albay, laging nasasalanta.

**[03:28:22.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004365)  
Maging bagyo man yan o mga volcanic eruptions.

**[03:28:25.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004366)  
Itong nakaraan ang pinakamahaba na evacuation time sa loob ng evacuation centers.

**[03:28:35.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004367)  
Inabot po sa 7 buwan.

**[03:28:38.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004368)  
7 buwan.

**[03:28:39.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004369)  
Nagsimula ng January 6 at nag-decamp ng August 6.

**[03:28:45.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004370)  
So, 7 months po.

**[03:28:47.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004371)  
And hindi po talaga nagpabaya ang DSWD.

**[03:28:51.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004372)  
Naandito ang aming regional director, si RD Norman Laurio.

**[03:28:55.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004373)  
And nagpapasalamat po ako sa agaran at maliksi na pag-responde niya sa mga pangailangan ng aming mga evacuees.

**[03:29:05.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004374)  
Suficiente rin po yung pinamimigay.

**[03:29:09.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004375)  
In fact, every 15 days, may binibigay na good for 3 days na food packs.

**[03:29:20.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004376)  
So, sa isang buwan, nakaka-receive ng mga around 9 to 10 boxes ang aming mga families.

**[03:29:29.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004377)  
Ito lang po ang aking comment.

**[03:29:32.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004378)  
First is, wala pong pinipili.

**[03:29:35.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004379)  
Ang ibig kong sabihin, maging isang miyembro lang ng pamilya,

**[03:29:39.540] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004380)  
o sampung miyembro ng pamilya,

**[03:29:41.440] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004381)  
eh ang ibinabahagi po ay isang kahon.

**[03:29:45.440] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004382)  
And every 3 days po sila nakaka-receive nito.

**[03:29:49.440] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004383)  
Baka maaari po na maging mas maayos ang pagbabahagi nito.

**[03:29:55.440] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004384)  
Kasi ang isang pamilya na may isang miyembro,

**[03:30:00.000] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004385)  
At makakareceive ng 6 kilos of rice every 3 days ay napakalaki po na appropriation na sana ay napunta doon sa mas malalaking pamilya.

**[03:30:10.920] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004386)  
So, iyon po ang isang comment ko.

**[03:30:13.180] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004387)  
Pangalawa rin naman po yung variety.

**[03:30:15.680] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004388)  
Alam ko kakaiba ang sitwasyon namin sa Albay.

**[03:30:18.900] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004389)  
Hindi all the time ay ganito kahaba ang evacuation.

**[03:30:25.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004390)  
Ang iba ilang araw lang, baka linggo.

**[03:30:28.920] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004391)  
Pero nangyayari po at nangyayari pa lang.

**[03:30:32.420] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004392)  
Kaya ang pakiusap ko po sana may variety rin ang laman ng mga boxes natin.

**[03:30:40.420] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004393)  
And sana po naandito ang chairman ng Ways and Means Committee.

**[03:30:45.380] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004394)  
Katabi ko po si chairman.

**[03:30:48.940] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004395)  
And napansin po namin ay yung madali pong ma-distribute at matagal ang shelf life ang nilalagay natin.

**[03:30:56.820] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004396)  
Siyempre.

**[03:30:57.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004397)  
Kasi mahabaan.

**[03:30:58.920] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004398)  
Ang storage nito.

**[03:31:00.740] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004399)  
Subalit sana po instead of 3-in-1 coffee na loaded po talaga sa asukal at flavorings,

**[03:31:10.540] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004400)  
baka po po pwedeng stick na lang ng coffee.

**[03:31:14.240] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004401)  
Baka mas marami pang mabibili kesa sa limang pakete ng 3-in-1.

**[03:31:19.700] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004402)  
Baka po pwedeng isang dosena na ng stick ng coffee.

**[03:31:23.380] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004403)  
Kasi at least yun totoong kape at hindi loaded sa asukal.

**[03:31:27.040] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004404)  
Nakalagay rin po doon ay Ovaltine.

**[03:31:31.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004405)  
And maganda po yun para sa mga bata.

**[03:31:34.680] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004406)  
Pero sana magkaroon ng mas nutritious na substitute.

**[03:31:38.680] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004407)  
Kasi ang tinitingnan lang po natin ay hindi lang yung may pantawid gutom,

**[03:31:43.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004408)  
kundi sana nutritious din po ang laman ng ating pinamimigay.

**[03:31:49.420] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004409)  
So wala po akong masyadong reklamo, sir.

**[03:31:53.320] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004410)  
Mabilis ang aksyon.

**[03:31:55.920] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004411)  
Sustained.

**[03:31:57.040] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004412)  
Masyado nga pong na-pamper yung mga LGUs.

**[03:32:00.420] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004413)  
Kasi under your watch, ngayon lang namin naramdaman

**[03:32:03.840] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004414)  
na hindi na iisipin ng LGUs kung ano ang ipamimigay.

**[03:32:08.260] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004415)  
Kasi sustained siya.

**[03:32:10.720] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004416)  
Hindi lang siya quick response, pero sustained ang response.

**[03:32:15.340] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004417)  
Tuloy, yung probinsya ng Albay, for instance,

**[03:32:19.240] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004418)  
once lang namihagi ng bigas.

**[03:32:21.680] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004419)  
At nung pag-decamp, doon nagbigay pa ng fresh food.

**[03:32:27.040] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004420)  
Yung mga LGUs naman po ay hindi na gumagamit ng kanilang quick response funds.

**[03:32:32.600] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004421)  
Kasi umasa na lahat sa DSWD.

**[03:32:36.280] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004422)  
So kanina sinabi ninyo na kayo ang pang-augment.

**[03:32:42.480] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004423)  
Hindi po ganun ang nangyayari.

**[03:32:44.480] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004424)  
So palagay ko may mali rin po doon sa senaryo, sir.

**[03:32:49.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004425)  
Kailangan ata mapag-aralan din mabuti.

**[03:32:52.480] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004426)  
O mag-guide yung LGU.

**[03:32:54.440] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004427)  
Kapag hindi ka nagbigay,

**[03:32:57.040] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004428)  
hindi ba po pwedeng i-ask sa lahat sa DSWD?

**[03:33:01.060] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004429)  
Lahat ako, salita, hindi ko malang narinig yung response ninyo.

**[03:33:05.220] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004430)  
So again, maraming salamat po.

**[03:33:07.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004431)  
Mr. Chairman, totally agree.

**[03:33:10.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004432)  
Una ho yung efficiency.

**[03:33:12.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004433)  
Now is the time that we study our options on individuals versus family packs.

**[03:33:18.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004434)  
Mahirap sa logistics sa storekeeping, pero we're up to the challenge.

**[03:33:22.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004435)  
Number two, variety.

**[03:33:24.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004436)  
Hindi na lang ngayon kasi sa Albay nangyayari.

**[03:33:27.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004437)  
Ito yung protracted disasters, like what we're seeing now in Central Luzon na one month and a half na.

**[03:33:32.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004438)  
When those boxes na, we just kept what it was nung na nakuha namin yung control ng department.

**[03:33:38.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004439)  
Tama ko kayo, we have to start thinking of the new type of disasters that's hitting us.

**[03:33:44.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004440)  
And variety is something that's important.

**[03:33:46.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004441)  
The food fatigue is something that's real.

**[03:33:48.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004442)  
At kaya kami nakakarinig rin.

**[03:33:50.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004443)  
So we will take that into account.

**[03:33:52.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004444)  
But the first two items, well noted, we will study.

**[03:33:56.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004445)  
Mahirap kasi ho.

**[03:33:58.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004446)  
That means us storekeeping multiple types of packs.

**[03:34:02.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004447)  
But we're up to the challenge.

**[03:34:04.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004448)  
Number three, yung 3-1 coffee.

**[03:34:08.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004449)  
Ako, I myself drink black and I agree.

**[03:34:10.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004450)  
Pero gusto ko lang ko i-point out, lahat ho yun, bago nalagay sa kahon, dumadaan sa FNRI.

**[03:34:15.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004451)  
It passed their minimum standards, but we will review.

**[03:34:19.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004452)  
Well noted as well, Madam Chair.

**[03:34:21.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004453)  
Mr. Chair, and last but not the least, yun sa QRF.

**[03:34:24.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004454)  
Ma'am, you read my mind.

**[03:34:25.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004455)  
And you said what I can't say publicly because we're in the middle of a disaster.

**[03:34:30.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004456)  
But rest assured that we're already talking to DBM because we're seeing slow or low utilization ng mga quick response sa LGU level.

**[03:34:38.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004457)  
And I would also point out nga na unlike the national QRF na na-exhaust sa lokal, you know this very well,

**[03:34:46.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004458)  
nare-revert yan after two years or three years into the general fund.

**[03:34:50.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004459)  
Five years pala, sorry, five years.

**[03:34:52.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004460)  
I haven't practiced local government in more than four years now.

**[03:34:55.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004461)  
So we have to really think about what you said.

**[03:35:00.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004462)  
Kasi ang sinasabi nyo, lagi ko rin yan sinasabi na sa isip ko.

**[03:35:03.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004463)  
Pero we're going to find a way to make the LGUs counterpart man lang.

**[03:35:08.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004464)  
Kasi nga, like I said, mabigat na ng pabigat.

**[03:35:11.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004465)  
And I hope we see lang kasi the only reason why we're doing this massive is because in a hungry family, walang national government, walang local government.

**[03:35:22.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004466)  
Pag galit siya sa gobyerno, galit siya sa gobyerno.

**[03:35:24.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004467)  
Kung hindi siya nakakuha ng pagkain, galit siya.

**[03:35:26.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004468)  
Pero it's no reason for the lopsided utilization of the QRF.

**[03:35:34.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004469)  
While the national government is using its QRF massively to a point na nade-deplete natin,

**[03:35:39.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004470)  
the local government will look at how they utilize and how to prod them with the help of DILG and DBM to spend their QRFs also.

**[03:35:47.860] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004471)  
I would just like to add na maganda yung aksyon na ginawa ng DSWD

**[03:35:54.860] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004472)  
na nagbigay ng Emergency Cash Assistance para mabibili nila yung mga [unclear] items na kailangan rin nila.

**[03:36:04.860] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004473)  
Pero sa 7 buwan at isang Emergency Cash Assistance, baka sa susunod pa pwedeng planuhin na mas madalas.

**[03:36:12.860] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004474)  
May cash for work. One cash for work, one emergency cash assistance.

**[03:36:17.860] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004475)  
So baka mas tingnan natin kung pa paano mabalansihin.

**[03:36:22.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004476)  
And since it's an ongoing disaster, si Mayon hasn't really de-escalated.

**[03:36:29.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004477)  
The Undersecretary for Disaster will sit down with a good Congresswoman on packaging more.

**[03:36:34.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004478)  
But well noted not just for Mayon but for every other protracted disaster.

**[03:36:39.960] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-004479)  
Maraming maraming salamat, Secretary. And congratulations, job well done.

**[03:36:46.200] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004480)  
Thank you, Kong Lagman.

**[03:36:48.840] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004481)  
As if we're running out of interpellators, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:36:51.880] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004482)  
The good thing is we have with us the Committee Chair of the Ways and Means.

**[03:36:58.400] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-004483)  
We recognize Congressman Miro Quimbo.

**[03:37:05.100] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004484)  
Thank you very much.

**[03:37:06.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004485)  
Actually, I wasn't supposed to ask you a question, but it seems that for the first time we're running out of warm bodies.

**[03:37:13.280] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004486)  
So just to keep the conversation going, well, I mean honestly, I have nothing to ask.

**[03:37:21.980] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004487)  
Because how do you fix something that's already working?

**[03:37:28.800] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004488)  
I think we have very hardworking employees.

**[03:37:34.060] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004489)  
The response time has become so much quicker.

**[03:37:38.220] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004490)  
Even in terms of professionalizing the distribution and pickup of our pag-asa boxes.

**[03:37:48.100] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004491)  
How do we call our boxes again?

**[03:37:49.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004492)  
Family food box.

**[03:37:50.700] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004493)  
Family food box.

**[03:37:51.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004494)  
I said that earlier, in previous years, parang kahon ng pag-asa.

**[03:38:01.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004495)  
Because it does. I mean, that's what it does.

**[03:38:04.020] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004496)  
Meaning when people see it, it's already a symbol of something that addresses deep wants at a very dire moment.

**[03:38:13.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004497)  
So tingin ko, baka pag-isipan natin, it's too businesslike.

**[03:38:18.020] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004498)  
It's too Rex Gatchal yan.

**[03:38:21.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004499)  
Parang it's part of a key performance indicator.

**[03:38:28.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004500)  
Parang ganun ang dating eh, di ba?

**[03:38:29.960] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004501)  
What's it called? Family?

**[03:38:31.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004502)  
Family food pack.

**[03:38:33.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004503)  
FFP.

**[03:38:34.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004504)  
Palitan mo na yun.

**[03:38:35.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004505)  
We will, Mr. Chair.

**[03:38:37.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004506)  
I think with something more, yeah, something more spiritual, di ba?

**[03:38:41.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004507)  
Hopeful, yeah.

**[03:38:42.220] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004508)  
Yeah, yung aspirational.

**[03:38:44.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004509)  
But anyway, so even the way that it's managed, the way they are packed, these are very simple things.

**[03:38:51.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004510)  
But even on the part of the end user, you see them very much satisfied.

**[03:38:57.640] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004511)  
And even the way it is packed today, I have rarely heard of anyone actually na, ang tawag dyan, binuburiki eh.

**[03:39:06.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004512)  
Yung dinidismantle and then nilalagay in their own political packaging.

**[03:39:12.120] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004513)  
Hindi ko na rin nababalitaan yan eh.

**[03:39:13.900] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004514)  
Kasi sobrang hirap yata i-unbundle.

**[03:39:16.760] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004515)  
Tanggal pala yung scotch tape dyan, di ba?

**[03:39:19.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004516)  
So, but like I said,

**[03:39:21.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004517)  
yung tanong ko na lang is really on senior citizen pension.

**[03:39:26.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004518)  
So, I've seen that while the budget for this, for next year, is actually lower, slightly lower from this year.

**[03:39:34.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004519)  
Despite the fact that the target has increased by, I think, more than 50,000, ang additional beneficiaries.

**[03:39:43.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004520)  
Pero bakit mas mababa yung budget?

**[03:39:47.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004521)  
Mr. Chairman, ano kasi yun?

**[03:39:50.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004522)  
Congressional amendment?

**[03:39:51.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004523)  
Yung nadagdag?

**[03:39:53.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004524)  
And even that, kasi kapos na kapos eh.

**[03:39:55.540] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004525)  
We are fully supportive of the provision that you've been pushing together with former Congresswoman Stella

**[03:40:01.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004526)  
on a universal senior pension, social pension for senior citizens.

**[03:40:06.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004527)  
It will address the public perception na pinipili.

**[03:40:10.740] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004528)  
And we totally agree.

**[03:40:12.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004529)  
Sana hawa, huwag lang ma-restore, but we add more to cater to all of them.

**[03:40:17.440] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004530)  
Yeah, I think, Chairman, the point here relative to senior citizens,

**[03:40:21.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004531)  
and pension is, nothing can be worse when you're doing a great program,

**[03:40:27.060] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004532)  
but you receive more criticisms than praises.

**[03:40:30.240] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004533)  
Because yung tumatanggap, kadalasan di naman nagpapasalamat na eh, diba?

**[03:40:35.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004534)  
Pero yung hindi nakakatanggap na karamihan, ay yun ang pinuputakti tayo.

**[03:40:40.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004535)  
Actually, Mr. Chairman, tama ho yun. Plus minus eh.

**[03:40:42.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004536)  
Kasi mas malaki yung minus than yung plus.

**[03:40:44.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004537)  
Kasi at the running rate of 11 million senior citizens, and we're only catering to 4.5 to 5,

**[03:40:49.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004538)  
that means yung 6 na milyon na hindi nakakatanggap,

**[03:40:51.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004539)  
minus dun sa lamang pahuyong hindi nakatanggap na galit.

**[03:40:55.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004540)  
At saka, ayoko naman lahat eh, pero marami sa ating mga kababayan,

**[03:40:59.500] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004541)  
katatanggap pa lang ng benepisyo,

**[03:41:01.760] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004542)  
ang susunod ay imbis sa magsabi ng maraming salamat sa Pangulo, sa DSWD,

**[03:41:07.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004543)  
ang kadalasan nadidinig ko eh, ano pa kaya susunod?

**[03:41:11.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004544)  
Diba? So, I really think that the program needs to be expanded.

**[03:41:16.400] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004545)  
So, there's an existing proposal here to have a reduced,

**[03:41:21.880] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004546)  
ah, amount for the new ones, meaning instead of 1,000 a month,

**[03:41:26.740] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004547)  
500 pesos a month lang so that the outlay becomes half.

**[03:41:30.400] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004548)  
And I think it's going to be more palatable and obviously it's going to be more affordable.

**[03:41:35.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004549)  
Sabi ko nga, Sek, imbis na dinadala sa kung ano-ano flood control, dito lang natin dalhin.

**[03:41:41.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004550)  
Kasi dito mas napapakinabangan pa ng tao.

**[03:41:43.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004551)  
Mr. Chairman, we agree 101% on both counts.

**[03:41:49.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004552)  
Half, but everybody.

**[03:41:50.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004553)  
And at the same time, yun nga, we put our money where we know na talagang direkto sa tao.

**[03:41:56.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004554)  
Huwag lang magalit ko si Sekvin sa akin.

**[03:41:59.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004555)  
Kasi that means a minus to his side.

**[03:42:02.100] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004556)  
Yeah, at the appropriate time, Mr. Chairman, we will move that more funding be given dito.

**[03:42:09.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004557)  
Kasi, you know, it's so hard to explain to our SSS and GSIS pensioners,

**[03:42:15.760] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004558)  
especially our SS pensioners who only get a pension of, on the average,

**[03:42:20.460] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004559)  
between

**[03:42:20.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004560)  
three to five thousand a month.

**[03:42:22.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004561)  
So, ang tingin talaga nila is bakit kami paparasahan na nagsipag kami.

**[03:42:27.900] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004562)  
For lack of a better explanation, is ang tingin nila is kami nagtrabaho,

**[03:42:32.340] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004563)  
o yung asawa namin nagtrabaho, kaya may survivor pension kami,

**[03:42:35.780] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004564)  
but we are automatically disqualified.

**[03:42:38.340] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004565)  
While itong kapitbahay namin na buong buhay niya ay walang kinontribute sa economy in terms of livelihood,

**[03:42:45.540] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004566)  
siya ay nari-rewardan when the source of the funds really come from,

**[03:42:50.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004567)  
taxpayers, including themselves,

**[03:42:53.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004568)  
especially since they had been paying compensation taxes during their entire working life.

**[03:42:58.620] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004569)  
So, ang hirap i-justify, eh.

**[03:43:00.780] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004570)  
And I think nadidiminish yung ganda ng programa.

**[03:43:05.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004571)  
Mr. Chair, we totally agree, and we're 101% behind that concept na huwag ipinalize yung mga may pension.

**[03:43:13.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004572)  
And sang-ayon ho kami.

**[03:43:15.360] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004573)  
At sana, Secretary, is we really move towards,

**[03:43:20.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004574)  
a more contactless electronic transfer.

**[03:43:25.800] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004575)  
Kasi, kawawa rin yung ating mga beneficiaries, kailangan pa i-biyahe,

**[03:43:30.340] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004576)  
especially mga senior citizens.

**[03:43:32.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004577)  
At wala naman akong pinapatunguhan o pinaparinggan,

**[03:43:37.280] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004578)  
pero gusto-gusto na ibang mga politiko na iniipon, eh.

**[03:43:41.260] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004579)  
Di ba, iniipon, magtatagal yung mga ating mga lolo't lola,

**[03:43:45.840] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004580)  
at nakikita mo, talagang they will have to hire tricycles to get to the venue,

**[03:43:50.120] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004581)  
They have to pay 150 pesos

**[03:43:50.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004582)  
one way, so 300 piso ka agad yun, di ba?

**[03:43:54.400] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004583)  
So, ang laking na ibabawas.

**[03:43:57.680] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004584)  
Sabi ko nga is, we need to keep pushing towards an electronic, more seamless distribution system.

**[03:44:05.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004585)  
Mr. Chairman, in fact, that move is already being done in the uplift assistance bucket 2.

**[03:44:12.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004586)  
Kanina na talakay ho namin na we're working with the BSP on banking the unbanked,

**[03:44:16.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004587)  
lalo na sa mahirap.

**[03:44:17.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004588)  
We saw in UPLIFT bucket number two, yung poor at near poor.

**[03:44:20.920] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004589)  
Yung poor, almost 80% are unbanked.

**[03:44:24.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004590)  
That's why what we did with the BSP is to get the banks and the e-wallets on site already.

**[03:44:28.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004591)  
So, pag-payout, nire-register na rin namin with the hopes of banking that 2.5 million na mga Pilipino na mahihirap at near poor na walang banko.

**[03:44:39.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004592)  
But we totally agree, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:44:42.080] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004593)  
I just hope that it will not also lead to delay, no?

**[03:44:45.540] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004594)  
Yung while there will be birth pains, meaning registration,

**[03:44:50.920] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004595)  
that will take time, but after that, baka pwedeng seamless na talaga.

**[03:44:56.040] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004596)  
They get it from Cebuana or they get it from their own.

**[03:45:00.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004597)  
electronic wallets.

**[03:45:01.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004597-split-2)  
Mr. Chair, we're gradually growing the database ng poor na walang banko, na walang bank. Yun nga yung sa UPLIFT bucket number two

**[03:45:11.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004598)  
with the hopes na on the third tranche, kasi napakaliit lang ho yung may bank account sa first tranche eh.

**[03:45:16.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004599)  
So hopefully lahat ng mga napapasok namin digital na sila, pero we need to grow it talaga.

**[03:45:22.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-004600)  
Malayo pa ho tayo doon sa may mga bank accounts.

**[03:45:24.630] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004600-split-2)  
Okay, now that there are other warm bodies, I think it's time for me to wrap up.

**[03:45:31.310] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004601)  
Bawal ang dead air dito eh. This is the first time, guys, that there's dead air, that no one is actually lined

**[03:52:29.000] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004680)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Actually, Mr. Chair, I have no question on the budget of the DSWD. But I have to do this because I believe the DSWD deserves my full support and, if our fiscal condition permits, I strongly support an increase in its budget.

**[03:52:53.010] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004681)  
The responsibility of the DSWD is enormous.

**[03:52:56.430] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004682)  
During typhoons, flood, fire, earthquakes and other disaster, DSWD is among the first agency.

**[03:53:06.790] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004683)  
Our people turn to.

**[03:53:09.630] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004684)  
But its responsibility goes far beyond disaster response.

**[03:53:14.350] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004685)  
Every day, it's aid for families, senior citizens, PWDs, children and other vulnerable sectors of our society.

**[03:53:24.570] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004686)  
I particularly wish to emphasize the importance of the LAWA and BINHI program.

**[03:53:31.850] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004687)  
What I appreciate about LAWA and BINHI is that it goes beyond.

**[03:53:36.790] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004688)  
It gives our people an opportunity to work, become productive and strengthen their ability to provide to their families.

**[03:53:47.790] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004689)  
Programs like LAWA and BINHI represent the direction we should pursue in social protection.

**[03:53:55.790] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004690)  
From ayuda toward empowerment, from temporal resistance toward sustainable livelihood and greater self-reliance.

**[03:54:05.850] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004691)  
from temporary assistance toward sustainable livelihood and greater self-reliance.

**[03:54:07.250] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004692)  
I would like to thank Director Isabel Laniada for providing LAWA and BINHI to my constituent in the fifth district of Cavite.

**[03:54:21.590] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004693)  
Mr. Secretary, it's really very successful.

**[03:54:24.270] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004694)  
It's not only develop camaraderie but also cooperation among the members of this LAWA and BINHI program.

**[03:54:33.870] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004695)  
I have personally seen how programs like this

**[03:54:37.890] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004700)  
can make a difference in our communities.

**[03:54:42.010] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004701)

**[03:54:48.610] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004702)  
vulnerable Filipino families.

**[03:54:52.130] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004703)  
I also wish to commend our dear Secretary Rex Gatchalian for his leadership of the department.

**[03:55:01.010] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004704)  
While the cabinet members of President Ferdinand Bombo Marcos, Jr. are performing their respective

**[03:55:08.130] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004705)  
responsibilities, Secretary Rex has consistently received a very high public performance rating

**[03:55:17.010] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004706)  
and has been ranked among the administration's top performing cabinet.

**[03:55:21.610] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004707)  
We are very lucky, napakaswerte ng ating mamamayan sa pagkakaroon ng isang Secretary

**[03:55:30.970] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004709)  
na may karanasan, may alam at natutugunan

**[03:55:39.010] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004710)

**[03:55:44.810] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004712)  
But this recognition also belongs to the thousands

**[03:55:48.370] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004713)  
of DSWD personnel and social workers who bring

**[03:55:52.970] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004714)  
government assistance directly to our people.

**[03:55:56.330] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004715)  
Mr. Chair, we cannot give DSWD enormous

**[03:56:00.730] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004716)  
responsibilities without giving it adequate resources. If we want programs such

**[03:56:09.610] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004717)

**[03:56:17.130] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004718)

**[03:56:31.290] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004720)

**[03:56:45.770] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004722)

**[03:56:53.530] Rep. Roy M. Loyola** (asr-004723)

**[04:59:50.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006430)

**[05:01:00.620] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-006450)  
We

**[05:01:02.360] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-006458)  
proceed. We recognize Kong Howard.

**[05:01:13.790] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006458-split-2)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Good afternoon to the DSWD family,

**[05:01:16.520] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006459)  
Secretary Rex. First and foremost, I would like to thank our good secretary. Last year,

**[05:01:23.060] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006460)  
October and November, we suffered a, I couldn't explain what it was. It's the hardest time

**[05:01:32.200] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006461)  
in our province. And the secretary, together with his team, was there immediately. I think

**[05:01:52.480] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006462)  
it was only overnight or in the two nights with, excuse me, Diane, was there. And not

**[05:01:59.520] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006463)  
just once, but twice in a span.

**[05:02:02.200] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006464)  
So, Mr. Secretary, thank you for your prompt response. With that, I would like to ask about

**[05:02:13.400] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006465)  
the Kalahe program. As we mentioned, during that calamity, marami po sa aming mga small

**[05:02:19.740] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006466)  
bridges, kasi marami po kami mga island barangay, talaga pong na-wipe out. And in this small

**[05:02:27.080] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006467)  
barangay, meron pong mga eskwelahan. And I just don't have the pictures right now.

**[05:02:32.200] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006468)  
May mga estudyante po kaming tumatawid po papunta sa isang eskwelahan na tumatawid ng tubig

**[05:02:38.980] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006469)  
na hanggang bewang po yung ano, because na-wipe out po yung kanilang mga foot bridges. So,

**[05:02:43.440] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006470)  
we wanted to ask how, what are the parameters or requirements in availing of this program?

**[05:02:54.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006471)  
Mr. Chairman, yung Kalahe kasi is a big line item. Marami ang laman. There's zero in.

**[05:03:03.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006472)  
So, we have one GOP na local funded, which is to the amount of 300 million.

**[05:03:12.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006473)  
The locally funded is around 300 million. Now, the criteria is very simple. We look at the poverty incidents. We look at the capacity of the LGU.

**[05:03:33.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006474)  
na nakaraan ba ay maganda

**[05:03:35.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006475)  
yung implementasyon. Kasi kung

**[05:03:38.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006476)  
matatandaan natin, may counterparting ito.

**[05:03:40.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006477)  
The whole concept is empowering the community, ownership of the community, pero mag-front kayo ng 20%, not in cash but in sweat equity.

**[05:03:49.990] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006478)  
Now, yung KALAHI, the bigger program itself, that was running for 20, 30 years, naubos na ho yung pondo niya

**[05:03:55.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006479)  
because it's a World Bank loan

**[05:03:59.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006480)  
that was extinguished last year.

**[05:04:02.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006481)  
Ang successor program niya is called the Panahon ng Pagkilos.

**[05:04:06.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006482)  
It's a line item again.

**[05:04:08.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006483)  
Pretty much the same concept, except that it looks for disaster resilience program as an added dimension.

**[05:04:14.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006484)  
So poverty, stunting, presence of vulnerable communities, including IPs,

**[05:04:19.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006485)  
and the prevalence of disasters so that we can come up with disaster-resilient plans or sub-projects.

**[05:04:25.320] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006486)  
And do we have any budget allocated for this for the following fiscal year?

**[05:04:31.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006487)  
Meron po, because it's an ODA.

**[05:04:33.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006488)  
But like most ODAs, naka-prescribed na when we submitted the list of target LGUs.

**[05:04:39.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006489)  
What we can do right now is to submit to the committee and your office the different LGUs that made the cut for CAPIS,

**[05:04:46.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006490)  
both for locally funded and at the same time for the ODA that I mentioned for community-driven development.

**[05:04:54.100] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006491)  
Thank you for that.

**[05:04:55.320] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006492)  
Secretary, and right now, could you say that the requested budget is sufficient?

**[05:05:03.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006493)  
It never is, Mr. Chairman.

**[05:05:05.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006494)  
And we hope that you can also look into this.

**[05:05:07.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006495)  
Pero pahabaan na ng pahabaan listahan namin.

**[05:05:09.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006496)  
Baka hindi na talaga kami ma-entertain.

**[05:05:11.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006497)  
Kasi dagdag kami ng dagdag sa listahan.

**[05:05:14.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006498)  
But it's a good program because it empowers communities and it brings development into the far-flung areas.

**[05:05:20.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006499)  
Maliit man yung proyekto, but meaningful.

**[05:05:22.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006500)  
Kasi katulad ng sinabi nyo, tulay na araw-araw ginagamit.

**[05:05:25.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006501)  
Water system na araw-araw ginagamit.

**[05:05:28.540] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006502)  
Tama po, Mr. Chair.

**[05:05:29.960] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006503)  
We'll see.

**[05:05:30.780] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006504)  
Maybe our appropriations may allocate additional budget for that.

**[05:05:36.200] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006505)  
My next question, although actually it's been raised already several times by our fellow congressmen,

**[05:05:41.400] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006506)  
so I think I would just manifest.

**[05:05:43.700] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006507)  
The SLP program that you have established is really helping a lot of associations,

**[05:05:51.740] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006508)  
not just in my district, but nationwide.

**[05:05:55.320] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006509)  
And I hope our Committee on Appropriations may add, I heard that kulang po ng P1 billion, tama po, based on our request.

**[05:06:09.540] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006510)  
Our Vice Chairman of Appropriations is here.

**[05:06:13.940] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006511)  
Maybe we can prioritize ito pong SLP.

**[05:06:18.200] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006512)  
Karamihan po ng mga congressmen, naninig po natin, ito, much favored po nila talaga yung SLP program.

**[05:06:24.540] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006513)  
Because it not just empowers the communities, it really changes the lives of our constituents.

**[05:06:31.840] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006514)  
So with that, I would like to end my manifestation.

**[05:06:37.460] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006515)  
Mr. Chair, yun lang po.

**[05:06:38.500] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006516)  
Baka pwede po natin tugunan yung pangailangan ng DSWD with regards to the SLP program.

**[05:06:44.420] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-006517)  
Maraming salamat po.

Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[05:06:53.560] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-006519)  
Thank you, Cong Howard.

**[05:06:59.380] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-006521)  
Dino Yulo.

**[05:07:01.840] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006522)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:07:03.840] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006523)  
Good afternoon, my colleagues, most especially good afternoon to the whole DSWD family.

**[05:07:07.800] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006524)  
Secretary, good afternoon.

**[05:07:09.800] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006525)  
This is quite short, just a manifestation.

**[05:07:12.800] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006526)  
Our district is heavily ravaged by Typhoon Tino.

**[05:07:16.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006527)  
I think many of you were there.

**[05:07:18.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006528)

**[05:07:24.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006529)

**[05:07:28.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006530)  
So maybe you can take a look at those hanging bridges.

**[05:07:32.260] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006531)

**[05:07:38.580] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006532)

**[05:07:53.220] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006533)  
In all of this, I think our able partner has been the DSWD.

**[05:07:56.880] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006534)  
So thank you.

**[05:07:58.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006535)  
Secretary, the whole DSWD family, and a special commendation to our, I don't know if it's here, our regional director, Director Arwin.

**[05:08:07.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006536)  
Thank you, regional director Arwin.

**[05:08:09.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006537)  
Thank you, sir.

**[05:08:10.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006538)

**[05:08:12.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006539)

**[05:08:17.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006541)  
Okay, thank you.

**[05:08:18.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006542)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[05:08:20.380] Rep. Emilio Bernardino L. Yulo** (asr-006543)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:08:22.380] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-006544)  
Thank you, Mr. Dino.

**[05:08:26.940] Rep. JC Rahman A. Nava, M.D.** (asr-006545)  
We now recognize Cong. Garbin.

**[05:08:29.940] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006546)  
Thank you.

**[05:08:32.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006547)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[05:08:33.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006548)  
Magandang hapon po to the SEC.

**[05:08:35.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006549)  
Magandang hapon to the DSWD family.

**[05:08:38.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006550)  
My question pertains to the implementation of Republic Act 9344.

**[05:08:44.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006551)  
Since the DSWD is at the center of implementing the diversion program through the Juvenile Justice Council,

**[05:09:00.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006552)  
the recent event, one that happened in Tacloban, the other one in Zamboanga,

**[05:09:11.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006553)  
discloses some of the loopholes and weaknesses of the existing law.

**[05:09:20.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006554)  
Some of the findings were the failure because of the,

**[05:09:27.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006555)  
the functional, lack of functional well-budgeted establishment,

**[05:09:39.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006556)  
especially referring to the Bahay Pag-asa that will manage the intervention program of these children in conflict with the law.

**[05:09:55.080] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006557)  
May I inquire, Mr. Secretary, whether the proposed budget,

**[05:10:01.110] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006559)  
for 2027 includes a budget for the establishment of Bahay Pag-asa in different localities,

**[05:10:08.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006560)  
especially in big cities and urban areas?

**[05:10:12.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006561)  
That's a very relevant and timely question.

**[05:10:15.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006562)  
In the JJWA, the LGUs are the ones tasked to build and fund and operate the Bahay Pag-asas.

**[05:10:22.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006563)  
The DSWD's main role is to set the standards,

**[05:10:25.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006564)  
to monitor the progress of the intervention programs.

**[05:10:29.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006565)  
Pero years later, we have given the data na highly urbanized cities don't have Bahay Pag-asas,

**[05:10:34.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006566)  
a lot of the provinces do not.

**[05:10:36.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006567)  
Kaya nga kanina nung tinanong ako,

**[05:10:38.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006568)  
is it time to revisit the JJWA?

**[05:10:42.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006569)  
Yes, in the sense na dapat natin lagyan ng provision for the national government to step in already,

**[05:10:48.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006570)  
because hindi natin makita yung full fruition ng restorative justice kung wala hong mga Bahay Pag-asa na ito.

**[05:10:53.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006571)  
Pangalawa,

**[05:10:55.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006572)  
last year, because of the lack of Bahay Pag-asa,

**[05:10:58.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006573)  
the Congress and the Senate gave us a new creature called the Regional Bahay Pag-asa.

**[05:11:04.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006574)  
Five.

**[05:11:05.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006575)  
We're not fully funded, initially funded.

**[05:11:08.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006576)  
We're still waiting for the SARO to be released,

**[05:11:11.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006577)  
pero we're constantly following up because naka-for-later-release siya.

**[05:11:14.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006578)  
But the authorities in DBM understand the magnitude na kailangan masimulan na ito.

**[05:11:19.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006579)  
But Mr. Chairman, let me point out na bagama't wala yang mga Bahay Pag-asa,

**[05:11:25.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006580)  
meron pang isang provision na last year nilagay sa libro ng DSWD, or sa budget ng DSWD.

**[05:11:30.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006581)  
Yun yung ability namin na mag-farm out ng services for private social welfare and development agencies,

**[05:11:38.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006582)  
kasi may mga private naman eh,

**[05:11:40.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006583)  
na pwede kami magbayad ng cost of care.

**[05:11:43.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006584)  
So inaantay na lang namin yung final go signal because the guidelines are being drafted already,

**[05:11:48.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006585)  
together with DBM and COA.

**[05:11:50.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006586)  
Once we do that, we can now refer clients to

**[05:11:53.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006587)  
existing private facilities that can rehabilitate

**[05:11:58.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006588)  
and hopefully reintegrate with the right intervention plan,

**[05:12:02.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006589)  
our CICLs.

**[05:12:04.470] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006590)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[05:12:07.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006591)  
I fully understand the situation of the SWD.

**[05:12:13.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006592)  
But as you know, those referrals will only be very limited,

**[05:12:18.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006593)  
especially if cases such as Samboanga Tacloban

**[05:12:24.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006594)  
and cases that will happen in far-flung areas.

**[05:12:28.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006595)  
And so before we speak of intervention and their version program

**[05:12:33.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006596)  
that could be implemented by the council to be managed by DSWD,

**[05:12:37.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006597)  
I hope that the national government will also have an intervention

**[05:12:42.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006598)  
in giving funds to those localities that can,

**[05:12:46.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006599)  
because not everyone can afford this Bahay Pag-asa.

**[05:12:51.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006600)  
And I hope that this,

**[05:12:54.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006601)  
intervention by the national government in coming up with those funds

**[05:12:59.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006602)  
to be allocated for those local government units

**[05:13:02.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006603)  
and that can maintain this Bahay Pag-asa.

**[05:13:05.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006604)  
My second question is,

20 years ago when we adopt and approve this law,

**[05:13:36.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006610)

**[05:13:38.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006611)

**[05:13:45.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006612)

**[05:13:52.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006615)

**[05:13:58.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006617)

**[05:14:03.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006618)

**[05:14:07.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006619)

**[05:14:10.100] Rep. Alfredo A. "Pido" Garbin Jr.** (asr-006620)

**[05:14:15.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006621)

**[05:14:19.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006622)  
that we are against lowering down

**[05:14:21.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006623)

**[05:14:24.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006624)

**[05:14:27.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006625)

**[05:14:31.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006626)

**[05:14:33.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006627)

**[05:14:36.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006628)

**[05:14:38.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006629)  
before we can say whether the law is successful

**[05:14:41.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006630)

**[05:14:44.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006631)

**[05:14:47.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006632)  
medyo mahirapan rin tayo i-justify sa world stage,

**[05:14:50.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006633)  
that from the age of 15,

**[05:14:54.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006634)  
14, no, 15, 15,

**[05:14:57.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006635)  
biglang ibababa natin,

**[05:14:59.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006636)  
para tayong

**[05:15:00.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006637)

**[05:15:05.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006638)  
and in one of the hearings in the senate uh the lowering of the minimum age the science doesn't

**[05:15:12.080] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006639)

**[05:15:19.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006640)

ng jjwa to discuss in in there are other risk factors not just the age we have to address the

**[05:15:31.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006642)

**[05:15:36.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006643)

**[05:15:44.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006644)

**[05:15:52.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006645)  
i think we can address the

**[05:15:54.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006646)

rin yung

**[05:18:33.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006680)

**[05:24:23.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006798)  
So, papaano po ang suportang ibinibigay ng DSWD,

**[05:24:28.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006799)  
particularly ng inyong inyong pong ahensya,

**[05:24:30.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006800)  
sa mga LGU na walang kapasidad na suportahan o tumugon

**[05:24:34.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006801)  
dito sa mga auxiliary services neto,

**[05:24:36.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006802)  
particularly on FSL interpreters?

**[05:24:39.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006803)  
At meron po ba tayong datos?

**[05:24:42.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006804)  
Ilan sa mga syudad at munisipalidad

**[05:24:45.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006805)  
yung may access at walang access sa mga FSL interpreters?

**[05:24:50.420] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006806)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[05:24:52.420] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006807)  
Currently po, wala po kaming nare-receive na request

**[05:24:55.300] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006808)  
from the LGU in terms of the assistance for interpreters.

**[05:24:59.700] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006809)  
What we receive are requests from the national government agencies

**[05:25:03.940] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006810)  
which we provided.

**[05:25:05.340] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006811)  
However, kailan lang po nag-meeting po kami

**[05:25:08.660] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006812)  
sa ilang organizations of deaf within the country

**[05:25:13.420] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006813)  
at napag-usapan po namin which we proposed also to the DSWD Academy

**[05:25:19.060] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006814)  
to provide po.

**[05:25:20.260] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006815)  
So, we have a curriculum for each LGU na magkaroon po tayo ng

**[05:25:23.940] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006816)  
Filipino Sign Language interpreter po.

**[05:25:26.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006817)  
So, wala pong datos sa mga syudad o munisipalidad na

**[05:25:31.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006818)  
sino ba yung mga syudad o munisipyo na meron o wala?

**[05:25:34.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006819)  
May ganun po bang datos ang ahensya?

**[05:25:37.260] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006820)  
Sa ngayon po, Mr. Chair, wala po kaming datos.

**[05:25:40.580] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006821)  
Opo. Pero yun nga po, given the fact na wala nga po,

**[05:25:44.860] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006822)  
but we anticipated kasi kahit po sa amin, yun pong kakulangan po ng Filipino Sign Language interpreter.

**[05:25:52.140] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006825)  
din po yung nakakaharap po ng NCDA.

**[05:25:55.140] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006826)  
That's why we deemed it proper to offer nga po itong curriculum na ito para po sa ating mga LGU

**[05:26:02.140] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-006827)  
so that they can have at least two Filipino sign language interpreter po.

**[05:26:07.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006828)  
Maraming salamat po sa paglilinaw na yan, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair.

**[05:26:10.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006829)  
Indication lang po yan na medyo mahina po ang ating implementation particular dito sa batas for FSL.

**[05:26:19.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006832)  
Kasi kung wala po tayong datos ng mga syudad at munisipalidad na meron bang access sila sa mga FSL interpreters, ibig sabihin hindi natin alam.

**[05:26:26.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006833)  
nasaan ba yung mga gaps patungkol diyan.

**[05:26:29.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006834)  
Kasi ang report coming from the ground ay marami po, nga kababayan at kapatid natin mula sa Deaf community,

**[05:26:36.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006835)  
papasok halimbawa ng munisipyo o papasok ng Hall of Justice, papasok ng iba't-ibang mga opisina,

**[05:26:42.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006836)  
hindi sila magkaintindihan kasi walang mag-i-interpret for them.

**[05:26:48.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006837)  
Yung accessibility po kasi hindi lang naman usapin niya na may rampa o yung pagiging PWD-friendly

**[05:26:53.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006838)  
and nandoon lang sa built na infrastructure.

**[05:26:57.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006839)  
Ito rin po ay nangangahulugan na available yung mga servisyo na ganyan,

**[05:27:01.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006840)  
lalong-lalo na sa mga kapatid natin na deaf.

**[05:27:03.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006841)  
So, yun po ang isa sana na po pwedeng tignan ng ating ahensya.

**[05:27:10.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006842)  
Next po na tanong natin, Mr. Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[05:27:13.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006843)  
alam niyo po, isa sa mga talagang invisibilized na workforce itong mga FSL interpreters natin,

**[05:27:20.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006844)  
wala kasing standard eh.

**[05:27:22.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006845)  
So, walang standard kung magkano yung rate nila,

**[05:27:25.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006846)  
wala actually sila ng mga benepisyon na natatanggap.

**[05:27:28.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006847)  
In fact, sila-sila, from among themselves, sila-sila lang ang nag-uusap,

**[05:27:32.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006848)  
o magka-isa tayo, ganito ang rate natin.

**[05:27:34.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006849)  
O magka-isa tayo, ganito yung terms natin pagkukuha tayo ng project.

**[05:27:39.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006850)  
Ano po yung mga ginagawang hakbangin ng DSWD?

**[05:27:43.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006851)  
Para kilalanin yung ating mga FSL interpreters bilang isang mahalagang bahagi ng ating workforce.

**[05:27:52.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006852)  
At ano po yung mga specific measures na pinopropose po ng kagawaran

**[05:27:56.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006853)  
para makilala natin itong mga FSL interpreters natin bilang bahagi ng formal na workforce natin, Mr. Chair.

**[05:28:03.680] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006854)  
If I can jump in.

**[05:28:05.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006855)  
Yes.

**[05:28:06.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006856)  
Bahagi, pinasok po namin yan, if you can recall,

**[05:28:09.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006857)  
social protection sa SGLG is in the realm of the DSWD.

**[05:28:13.880] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006858)  
Kami ho nagbibigay ng mga indicators to compel our local government units to put in FSL interpreters.

**[05:28:20.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006859)  
Nilagay ho namin yan as one of the indicators that they will be graded upon.

**[05:28:24.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006860)  
Kasi like we said, responsibilidad ay agree ng mga national government agency, pati ng mga LGU ho yan.

**[05:28:30.720] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006861)  
That's a way of us compelling and formalizing.

**[05:28:33.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006862)  
It's in the SGLG rating card, yung availability or yung presence of an FSL interpreter in the locality.

**[05:28:45.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006863)  
Maraming salamat po sa paglilinaw, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair.

**[05:28:49.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006864)  
Kaya ako po binabanggit ito dahil just recently po nag-deliver tayo ng privileged speech about this matter

**[05:28:55.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006865)  
na karagdagang proteksyon at pagkilala sa mga FSL interpreters natin.

**[05:28:59.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006866)  
In fact po, Mr. Secretary, nakakalungkot, nakakaroon tayo ng budget deliberation ngayon about your budget.

**[05:29:05.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006867)  
Pero wala man lang available na nag-i-interpret ng pinag-uusapan natin.

**[05:29:10.740] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006868)  
Kung gustong maintindihan ng mga kapatid natin mula sa deaf community,

**[05:29:15.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006869)  
kung anong pinag-uusapan natin ngayon, sila yung kinakailangang maghanap ng paraan para magkaintindihan.

**[05:29:20.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006870)  
Maintindihan nila itong pinag-uusapan natin ngayon.

**[05:29:23.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006871)  
And that shows, Mr. Chair, yung gaps talaga pagdating dito sa may existing na nabatas eh.

**[05:29:29.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006872)  
So kaya kailangan nating palakasin ito.

**[05:29:31.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006873)  
At kung may commitment naman po ang DSWD na ipatupad ito at palakasin ang programang ito,

**[05:29:37.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006874)

**[05:29:45.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-006875)  
We will do better.

**[05:29:46.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006876)  
Maraming salamat po. Malaking bagay po yun, Mr. Sekretary, yung binanggit nyo po.

**[05:29:50.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006877)  
At ikatutuwa po yan ng ating mga stakeholders mula po sa deaf community.

**[05:29:54.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006878)  
With that, Mr. Chair, buo po ang suporta ng kinatawang ito at ng Kamanggagawa Party-list sa DSWD.

**[05:30:00.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006880)

**[05:30:10.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006885)  
At sa iba pa po na mga sektor

**[05:30:13.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-006887)

**[05:50:06.990] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007399)  
Mr. Chair, Honorable Secretary Gazzalian, thank you.

**[05:50:14.470] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007400)  
I'd like to reiterate my support sa budget ng ASWD.

**[05:50:19.970] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007401)  
I'd like to reiterate your good performance.

**[05:50:23.210] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007402)  
Thank you.

**[05:50:25.470] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007403)  
I'd like to reiterate your quick response to the earthquake, to the flooding, with our very active Regional Director Cabaya.

**[05:50:35.530] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007404)  
And marami na rin kami natulungan through the ASWD.

**[05:50:39.770] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007405)  
May isang tanong lang po ako, Secretary, yung sinasabi mo na beyond what is 10,000, yung GL, sa hospital.

**[05:50:48.370] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007406)  
How can we do that?

**[05:50:49.550] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007407)  
For example, mayroong pangangailangan na hindi na

**[05:50:55.470] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007408)  
makaya ng MAIP namin and very kailangan talagang tulungan.

**[05:51:01.050] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007409)  
Tapos, how can we refer that to the DSWD?

**[05:51:04.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007410)  
Mr. Chairman, the good congressman is correct.

**[05:51:07.430] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007411)  
It's normally used together with the MAIP kasi hindi naman nasasagot lahat ng MAIP.

**[05:51:11.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007412)  
Okay.

**[05:51:12.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007413)  
So, it's as simple as you referring the client to any of our office or satellite office in North Cotabato.

**[05:51:19.390] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007414)  
We will never turn down anybody na pumunta sa pintuan ng DSWD.

**[05:51:23.250] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007415)  
Our social workers will assess.

**[05:51:25.470] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007416)  
And then, issue out the guarantee letter doon sa service provider that they need.

**[05:51:31.190] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007417)  
We have hundreds and hundreds or thousands of service provider ranging from drug stores

**[05:51:36.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007418)  
all the way to orthopedic supplies and burial services also.

**[05:51:41.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007419)  
So, sa regional office namin yan pinaprocess or any of our satellite office.

**[05:51:46.390] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007420)  
Okay.

**[05:51:46.890] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007421)  
Mr. Secretary, for example, if the patient is doon sila napunta sa region 11, SPMC.

**[05:51:55.010] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007422)  
PMC, pero resident sila ng aming distrito, where will we refer?

**[05:52:02.450] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007423)  
Sa region po where they are confined, kasi napakahirap naman ho na babalik pa siya sa

**[05:52:07.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007424)  
home region niya.

**[05:52:08.350] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007425)  
This happens all the time, lalo-lalo na sa Davao region, sa Cebu region.

**[05:52:12.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007426)  
Our officers will never look or discriminate kung saan ka galing.

**[05:52:17.010] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007427)  
Ang importante sa amin yung crisis at yung pangangailangan.

**[05:52:19.730] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007428)  
Thank you, Sir Secretary, and again, congratulations to the SWD family.

**[05:52:27.110] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007429)

more, then we will also support that.

**[05:52:39.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007432)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:52:40.830] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-007433)  
Thank you, Chairman.

**[06:39:45.910] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007444)  
Mr. Chair, parehong bumaba ang pondo ng Council for the Welfare of Children at ng Juvenile Justice and Welfare Council.

**[06:39:54.330] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007445)

**[06:39:56.670] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007446)

**[06:40:09.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007447)  
Mr. Chair, I'll let the two Executive Directors who are with us right now, para hindi naman husayang yung gasolina nila sa pagpunta dito, sila na ho magpapaliwanag para sa kanilang mga ahensya.

**[06:40:21.550] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007448)

**[06:40:26.670] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007450)  
Mr. Chair, Madam Congressman, the bulk of the budget cut of the Council for the Welfare of Children, we are looking at around 60 plus million po.

**[06:40:39.870] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007451)  
More than half of that accounts for or pertains to ICT po, budget for ICT po, digitalization for our management information support unit.

**[06:40:49.790] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007452)  
Tapos yung mga natira po doon, Madam Chair, Mr. Chair, are funds for the operation.

**[06:40:56.670] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007453)  
We also have funds of the divisions and units of the Council for the Welfare of Children po, which includes our support for our regional staff, capacity building and technical assistance for LGUs, budget for the Child-Friendly Local Governance Audit, and the Presidential Award for Child-Friendly Municipalities and Cities po, among others, Mr. Chair.

**[06:41:17.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007454)

**[06:41:26.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007455)  
At saka kung ano ba yung tunay na sitwasyon ng mga batang Pilipino.

**[06:41:31.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007456)

**[06:41:44.260] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007457)  
Mr. Chair, Madam Congresswoman, ang proposal po namin for 2027 ay 318,211,000.

**[06:41:52.940] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007458)

**[06:41:56.560] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007459)  
... ay 148,999. Kasama na po dyan yung RLIP po, hindi lang po yung PS, MOOE, and Capital Outlay. Yan na po yung lahat.

**[06:42:08.820] CWC Executive Director Angelo M. Tapales** (asr-007460)

**[06:42:19.980] JJWC Executive Director Tricia Claire Oco** (asr-007461)  
Mr. Chair, for the JJWC, we also have our budget lowered.

**[06:42:31.840] JJWC Executive Director Tricia Claire Oco** (asr-007462)

**[06:42:46.080] JJWC Executive Director Tricia Claire Oco** (asr-007463)

**[06:42:59.260] JJWC Executive Director Tricia Claire Oco** (asr-007464)

**[06:43:01.840] JJWC Executive Director Tricia Claire Oco** (asr-007465)

**[06:43:16.220] JJWC Executive Director Tricia Claire Oco** (asr-007466)

**[06:43:27.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007467)

**[06:43:31.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-007468)

**[06:58:26.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007868)

**[06:58:28.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007869)

**[06:58:30.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007870)

**[06:58:32.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007871)

**[06:58:34.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007872)

**[06:58:36.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007873)

**[06:58:40.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007875)

**[06:58:42.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007876)

**[06:58:44.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007877)

**[06:58:46.200] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007878)

**[06:58:48.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007879)  
Kami kasi, naniniwala ko kami,

**[06:58:50.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007880)

**[06:58:52.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007881)

**[06:58:54.780] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007882)

**[06:58:56.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007883)  
dependency, yung buong pamilya

**[06:58:58.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007884)

**[06:59:00.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007885)

**[06:59:02.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007886)

**[06:59:04.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007887)

**[06:59:06.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007888)

**[06:59:08.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007889)  
to help the family cope.

**[06:59:10.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007890)

**[06:59:18.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007894)  
for the pilot. Maliit lamang yung pilot.

**[06:59:20.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007895)  
But for 2027,

**[06:59:22.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007896)  
it's now at 8.6

**[06:59:25.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007897)

**[07:00:21.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007921)

**[07:00:26.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007922)

**[07:00:29.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007923)

**[07:00:34.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007924)

**[07:00:40.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007925)

**[07:00:45.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007926)

**[07:00:46.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007927)

**[07:00:48.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007928)

**[07:00:52.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007929)

**[07:00:55.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007930)

**[07:00:57.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007931)

**[07:01:00.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007932)  
So tulad ng sinabi namin,

**[07:01:01.800] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007933)

**[07:01:05.060] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007934)

**[07:01:08.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007935)

**[07:01:10.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007936)

**[07:01:12.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007937)

**[07:01:14.400] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007938)

**[07:01:17.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-007939)
