**[07:38:05.030] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008437)  
Malikit pa lang po yung binigay sa amin, 584.

**[07:38:07.890] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008438)  
But as Secretary Kim said, it's going to be gradual, tuloy-tuloy naman po.

**[07:38:11.670] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008439)  
Okay po.

**[07:38:12.330] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008440)  
I just want to advocate for them, Secrex.

**[07:38:14.430] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008441)  
I know you advocate for your people as well.

**[07:38:16.990] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008442)  
But we'll just put a little bit more pressure on DBM na sabi...

**[07:38:20.410] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008443)  
Sorry po, ata tawagin.

**[07:38:21.730] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008444)  
On the spot po na.

**[07:38:22.550] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008445)  
Si Director Bench ba yan?

**[07:38:23.950] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008446)  
Nandyan, yes.

**[07:38:24.530] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008447)  
Okay, Director Bench, are you on the spot?

**[07:38:26.690] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008448)  
It's been a year.

**[07:38:31.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008449)  
Yan, Director.

**[07:38:32.810] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008450)  
Sorry, Director Bench.

**[07:38:33.910] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008451)  
Sorry, you're on the spot.

**[07:38:35.030] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008452)  
Ako na po kasi sila talaga po yung frontliners natin, eh.

**[07:38:45.370] DBM Director IV Benjieleth M. Zuñiga** (asr-008453)  
We'll have to turn on po, Madam Chair.

**[07:38:47.550] DBM Director IV Benjieleth M. Zuñiga** (asr-008454)  
Actually, tama naman po, Secrex.

**[07:38:49.210] DBM Director IV Benjieleth M. Zuñiga** (asr-008455)  
As discussed po with Secretary Kim po nung Friday, for approval na po siya.

**[07:38:56.190] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008456)  
Actually, ang exact words is, Sec. Kim, di ba?

**[07:38:58.930] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008457)  
Nandyan na, na-approve na, hanapin lang yung papel.

**[07:39:02.870] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008458)  
Pero padating na, di ba?

**[07:39:04.310] DBM Director IV Benjieleth M. Zuñiga** (asr-008459)  
For approval na po niya, may question lang po siya doon sa bureau na nag...

**[07:39:08.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008460)  
Ano?

**[07:39:10.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008461)  
Madam Chair, I will see to it that I call Sec. Kim to remind him so that we get those positions in

**[07:39:16.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008462)  
with a priority na yung mga matatagal na humauna, then follow up the next succeeding position.

**[07:39:22.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008463)  
I just want to point out, to be fair with DBM, wala man sila dito.

**[07:39:26.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008464)  
Ang total na nilabas na nila for 2026 na plantili positions namin is 8,463,

**[07:39:33.350] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008465)  
of which 5,000 plus went to 4Ps, at the rest nakakalat.

**[07:39:36.810] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008466)  
Itong sa AICS lang may clarification, pero I don't think we have...

**[07:39:40.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008467)  
any disagreement whatsoever.

**[07:39:43.390] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008468)  
Thank you po, Sec. Director Bench.

**[07:39:46.610] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008469)  
Mahungulit lang po talaga kami.

**[07:39:48.410] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008470)  
Because it's been a year since I spoke about it.

**[07:39:54.030] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008471)  
Budgeteering po yun ng DSWD.

**[07:39:56.670] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008472)  
So I'd like to follow up lang po, just to advocate for our frontliners,

**[07:40:02.450] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008473)  
our field personnel who are there every day,

**[07:40:06.110] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008474)  
disbursing, distributing all of the services that DSWD provides.

**[07:40:10.370] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008475)  
So alam naman yun ni Secrex talaga how much I've been making kulit about this over the past year.

**[07:40:17.370] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008476)  
Kasi po, nakita ko po talaga, iba mag-reddit tayo na lang, hindi pa sila na-regularize.

**[07:40:23.410] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008477)  
So hopefully po, 400 plus positions po, Secrex, right?

**[07:40:27.330] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008478)  
That's what we're expecting for 584 positions.

**[07:40:32.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008479)  
Yeah, for AICS in particular.

**[07:40:33.370] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008480)  
Okay. 4Ps done na po. End disaster.

**[07:40:36.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008481)  
First batch po, 5,000 na po yun. May buntot pa ulit na another 5,000.

**[07:40:40.450] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008482)  
Apo. Sige po, Secrex. We'll work together po.

**[07:40:43.810] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008483)  
We'll, mag-ulit po tayo sa DBM to advocate for our frontliners.

**[07:40:50.970] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008484)  
Thank you, Secrex.

**[07:40:52.630] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008485)  
I'll move lang po to, there's a, one of our initiatives in the Committee on Appropriations

**[07:41:01.370] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008486)  
is to put forth some of the questions of the civil society organizations.

**[07:41:06.750] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008487)  
And this morning po, Senior Vice Chair,

**[07:41:10.450] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008488)  
Abbot Garcia, and several of our vice chairpersons in the Committee on Appropriations

**[07:41:14.810] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008489)  
conducted the People's Budget Review.

**[07:41:16.830] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008490)  
So mayroon na pong engagement sa civil society organizations as to what insights they have

**[07:41:22.730] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008491)  
with regard to particular agencies, particular budget items, and the like.

**[07:41:27.230] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008492)  
And similarly po, we also committed to them that we will propound questions on their behalf to the agency.

**[07:41:37.330] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008493)  
So I have here a few.

**[07:41:39.650] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008494)  
A few of the questions.

**[07:41:41.830] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008495)  
And if you would allow me po, Secrex, I'd just like to ask some of their questions po.

**[07:41:47.610] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008496)  
The first would be from Enet with regard to early childhood care and development.

**[07:41:52.850] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008497)  
I believe this was tackled earlier today as well.

**[07:41:56.470] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008498)  
Particularly with regard to the supplementary feeding program of the SWD.

**[07:42:01.270] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008499)  
The question is, DSWD's supplementary feeding declined from 9.564 billion

**[07:42:09.650] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008500)  
in fiscal year 2026 to 6.359 billion in 2027 in the NEP,

**[07:42:16.970] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008501)  
while the target remains 1,899,415 children in CDCs and SNPs.

**[07:42:25.430] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008502)  
Question po, how will the SWD maintain the same beneficiary target

**[07:42:29.910] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008503)  
with roughly 3.2 billion less in funding?

**[07:42:33.830] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008504)  
If you can recall, Madam Chair,

**[07:42:36.350] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008505)  
dinagdagan yung number of days kaya lumaki ho yan eh.

**[07:42:38.770] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008506)  
To synchronize with the target.

**[07:42:39.630] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008507)  
To synchronize with the DepEd from 120 to 180 days.

**[07:42:42.750] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008508)  
So binalik lang ho sa 120.

**[07:42:44.890] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008509)  
So meaning, same number of beneficiaries, lesser number of days.

**[07:42:48.130] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008510)  
But we put it in our...

**[07:42:50.610] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008511)  
Nilagay natin, no?

**[07:42:52.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008512)  
But again, we will respect the wisdom of Congress

**[07:42:56.970] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008513)  
if you wish to synchronize it back to 180

**[07:43:00.510] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008514)  
para tugmang-tugma kami with DepEd.

**[07:43:03.610] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008515)  
Thank you, Secrex.

**[07:43:04.950] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008516)  
Actually, that was one of the concerns that was raised by SecSun

**[07:43:09.630] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008517)  
yesterday.

**[07:43:10.570] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008518)  
It's really the cut from...

**[07:43:13.450] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008519)  
The difference essentially from the 2026 GA-A level

**[07:43:16.610] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008520)  
of roughly 26 billion for the school-based feeding program

**[07:43:20.610] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008521)  
down back to the 2026 NEP level of 11 billion.

**[07:43:26.090] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008522)  
So that was one of the discussions yesterday po.

**[07:43:29.750] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008523)  
It will take that into consideration as we look through the budget.

**[07:43:34.490] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008524)  
Pero given the limited fiscal space po,

**[07:43:36.470] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008525)  
we'll do the best that we can.

**[07:43:39.630] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008526)  
They were just asking po, Secrex, as a follow-up question,

**[07:43:43.270] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008527)  
what meal cost, number of feeding days, and nutritional standards

**[07:43:46.650] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008528)  
are assumed under the 6.359 billion allocation?

**[07:43:50.390] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008529)  
We're still at the 25 pesos per meal cap,

**[07:43:53.310] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008530)  
which is an increase when we joined this administration.

**[07:43:56.690] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008531)  
It was only at maliit lang ngayon, 18 pesos.

**[07:43:59.910] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008532)  
So we're now at 25 pesos.

**[07:44:01.230] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008533)  
And then milk for the undernourished is at 22 pesos,

**[07:44:05.790] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008534)  
which we get from the Carabao Center.

**[07:44:09.630] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008535)  
And then 120 days, as mentioned.

**[07:44:12.730] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008536)  
And then the caloric intake is based on the FNRI-approved Pinggang Pinoy.

**[07:44:20.230] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008537)  
Thank you, Secrex.

**[07:44:21.670] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008538)  
And finally po, will any part of the cost be shifted to LGUs, parents, or other sources?

**[07:44:26.750] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008539)  
And how will DSWD prevent inequities between richer and poorer LGUs?

**[07:44:31.710] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008540)  
Again, it's based on enrollment.

**[07:44:34.230] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008541)  
Once they enter the number of children enrolled in their IS system,

**[07:44:38.670] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008542)  
dun lang naman namin hinipin.

**[07:44:39.630] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008543)  
So the more enrollment, the more you get.

**[07:44:42.570] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008544)  
Obviously, it's an efficient way of doing it.

**[07:44:44.650] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008545)  
Kesa kung mag-a-allocate kami ng fix,

**[07:44:46.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008546)  
pero hindi naman nila natatamaan yung bilang ng studyante na nag-e-enroll.

**[07:44:51.370] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008547)  
But on a separate note,

**[07:44:53.530] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008548)  
if they mean kung ipapass on namin yung cost sa iba, hindi po.

**[07:44:57.590] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008549)  
Pero ang nangyayari ngayon,

**[07:44:59.230] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008550)  
because of the

**[07:45:00.240] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008551)  
difficulty in central procurement

**[07:45:01.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008552)  
and logistics of handling fresh goods,

**[07:45:04.520] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008553)  
65%, 55%

**[07:45:06.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008554)  
to 60% of this is downloaded

**[07:45:07.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008555)  
directly to the LGUs that

**[07:45:09.900] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008556)  
we mandated or encouraged

**[07:45:12.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008557)  
them to use the Sagip Saka Law

**[07:45:14.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008558)  
para diretso ho in procurement sa ating

**[07:45:16.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008559)  
mga magsasaka. So, as much

**[07:45:18.180] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008560)  
as we can, we empower the local government units

**[07:45:20.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008561)  
kasi they're the ones providing the

**[07:45:22.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008562)  
teachers anyway. And from our

**[07:45:24.140] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008563)  
experience, LGUs

**[07:45:26.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008564)  
also augment.

**[07:45:27.860] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008565)  
Kasi hindi naman ho sapat

**[07:45:29.700] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008566)  
minsan sa maraming LGUs, nagdadagdag pa

**[07:45:32.120] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008567)  
ng bilang

**[07:45:33.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008568)  
or ng number para

**[07:45:35.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008569)  
tumaas yung unit cost.

**[07:45:37.620] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008570)  
Thank you, Secrex. Those are the questions

**[07:45:39.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008571)  
from ENET pertaining to early

**[07:45:41.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008572)  
childhood care and development.

**[07:45:43.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008573)  
Another

**[07:45:44.320] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008574)  
CSO po who would like to ask

**[07:45:47.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008575)  
questions would be FSL National

**[07:45:49.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008576)  
Network pertaining to

**[07:45:51.780] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008577)  
the National Council for Disability

**[07:45:54.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008578)  
Affairs.

**[07:45:54.500] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008579)  
A few questions

**[07:45:56.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008580)  
po. There are two questions.

**[07:45:57.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008581)  
If we may direct the question

**[07:46:00.280] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008582)  
po, Secrex.

**[07:46:00.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008583)  
She will be in a better position to answer.

**[07:46:03.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008584)  
Thank you po. The first question would be

**[07:46:06.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008585)  
please enumerate all prior

**[07:46:08.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008586)  
policies as evidenced

**[07:46:10.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008587)  
by appropriations in 2026

**[07:46:12.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008588)  
towards compliance to RA

**[07:46:14.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008589)  
11106 for its

**[07:46:16.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008590)  
six language domains, prospectively

**[07:46:18.660] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008591)  
in the 2027

**[07:46:20.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008592)  
appropriations.

**[07:46:22.600] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008593)  
With the indulgence

**[07:46:23.920] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008594)  
of Mr. Chair,

**[07:46:25.780] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008595)  
on Friday po meron po kaming meeting

**[07:46:27.700] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008596)  
regarding po the Memorandum

**[07:46:29.460] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008597)  
circular under the UP Law

**[07:46:31.760] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008598)  
Center po regarding the use of the

**[07:46:33.480] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008599)  
FSL on our courts.

**[07:46:35.820] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008600)  
And we also

**[07:46:37.920] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008601)  
are giving

**[07:46:41.020] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008602)  
assistance to any agency

**[07:46:43.100] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008603)  
po na nagre-request po ng

**[07:46:45.340] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008604)  
Filipino Sign Language

**[07:46:47.140] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008605)

**[07:46:49.540] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008606)  
there's a work

**[07:46:51.880] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008607)

**[07:46:53.780] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008608)

**[07:46:56.040] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008609)  
the DSWD Academy

**[07:46:57.700] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008610)  
regarding provision of FSL training to all LGUs.

**[07:47:04.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008611)  
Thank you.

**[07:47:05.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008612)  
And last question, how has the council complied with,

**[07:47:09.260] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008613)  
as evidenced in PS appropriations previously and prospectively for 2027,

**[07:47:15.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008614)  
for the mandate of RA 10524 on quotas for hiring of persons with disabilities,

**[07:47:22.300] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008615)  
specifically deaf persons?

**[07:47:24.460] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008616)  
What kinds of positions are these deaf hires?

**[07:47:27.700] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008617)  
Currently, our positions in our local government units,

**[07:47:35.260] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008618)  
we have 97.4% engagement or persons with disability compliance.

**[07:47:43.940] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008619)  
But only 400 are those who have regular positions.

**[07:47:50.060] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008620)

**[07:48:00.660] NCDA Executive Director Glenda D. Relova** (asr-008623)

**[07:49:33.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008648)

**[07:49:43.980] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008650)

**[07:49:48.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008651)

**[07:50:00.300] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008655)

**[07:50:04.380] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008656)  
NCSC,

**[07:50:06.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008660)

**[07:50:10.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008662)

**[07:50:13.420] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008663)

**[07:50:20.660] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008664)  
Uulitin ko po.

**[07:50:22.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008665)

**[07:50:28.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008666)  
We need to help DBM.

**[07:50:29.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008667)

**[07:50:33.340] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008668)

**[07:50:35.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008669)

**[07:50:43.020] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008670)

**[07:50:44.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008671)  
Para ma-identify na natin ahead of time.

**[07:50:47.820] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008672)

**[07:50:51.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008673)

**[07:50:56.460] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008674)

**[07:51:00.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008675)  
Hindi ho kailangan maging komplikado ho ito.

**[07:51:02.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008676)

**[07:51:05.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008677)

**[07:51:09.320] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008678)

**[07:51:12.480] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008679)

**[07:51:16.000] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008680)

**[07:51:18.600] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008681)

**[07:51:21.220] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008682)

**[07:51:25.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008683)

I think they really have to talk to PSA kasi yung PSA nagre-react

**[07:51:35.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008685)

**[07:51:37.100] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008686)  
na tama yung notion ho namin.

**[07:51:38.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008687)

**[07:51:42.580] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008688)

**[07:51:44.160] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008689)

**[07:51:48.940] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008690)

It may not be perfect.

**[07:51:57.960] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008693)

**[07:52:01.620] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008694)

**[07:52:04.280] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008695)  
Tapos yun lang ang susuriin natin.

**[07:52:05.500] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008696)

**[07:52:08.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008697)  
Thank you, Secrex.

**[07:52:26.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008701)

**[07:52:30.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008702)  
Hindi po makapag-align at the moment yung CHED, yung PASUC at saka yung DBM

**[07:52:37.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008703)

**[07:52:41.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008704)

**[07:52:45.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008705)

**[07:52:52.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008706)  
Doon naman po sa FHE, free higher education, ang nangyayari nag-a-accrue lang, nag-a-accumulate lang yung deficiency between the gap nung estimated number of enrollees

**[07:53:07.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008707)  
versus the actual number of enrollees.

**[07:53:09.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008708)

**[07:53:18.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008709)  
So it's really, I think there has to be a system po. I agree with Sec Rex.

**[07:53:24.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008710)  
And I think before the next budget season, sana maayos na.

**[07:53:28.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008711)  
Kasi yung sa FHE po naayos nila between last year and this year's budget season.

**[07:53:34.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008712)  
Pero yun po kasi nangyayari.

**[07:53:35.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008713)  
I recognize the problem.

**[07:53:37.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008714)

**[07:53:41.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008715)

**[07:53:48.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008716)

**[07:53:58.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008718)

**[07:54:01.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008719)

**[07:54:05.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008720)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Chairman.

**[07:54:07.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008721)

**[07:54:09.040] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008722)  
Mag-a-accrue ito nang mag-a-accrue.

**[07:54:10.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008723)

**[07:54:14.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008724)

**[07:54:15.840] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008725)  
Tapos magba-backlog ulit.

**[07:54:21.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008727)

**[07:54:25.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008728)

**[07:54:29.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008729)

**[07:54:31.640] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008730)

**[07:54:36.440] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008732)

**[07:55:16.440] NCSC Acting Executive Director Ana Marie C. Calapit** (asr-008741)

**[07:55:42.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008747)

**[07:55:59.760] NCSC Acting Executive Director Ana Marie C. Calapit** (asr-008749)

**[07:56:02.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008751)  
So should be, Sec Rex?

**[07:56:04.110] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008751-split-2)  
Ayoko na ho.

**[07:56:04.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008752)  
Ayoko na ho makipag-back and forth kasi mahaba na ho.

**[07:56:07.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008753)  
A couple of things.

**[07:56:09.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008754)  
The PhilSys is coded.

**[07:56:11.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008755)  
So hindi mo talaga makukuha yung PhilSys ng lahat ng mamamayan.

**[07:56:14.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008756)

**[07:56:16.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008757)

**[07:56:22.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008758)

**[07:56:26.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008759)

**[07:56:30.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008760)

**[07:56:33.760] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008761)

**[07:56:35.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008762)  
Pero parang hihingin mo yung buong PhilSys database.

**[07:56:40.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008763)

**[07:56:46.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008764)  
Again, we can have a discussion separately, pagpatawag ho kami ng Kongreso,

**[07:56:51.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008765)  
but the budgeting kasi is getting affected by this.

**[07:56:54.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008766)  
Parang it's turning into a habol nang habol every year

**[07:56:58.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008767)

**[07:57:00.560] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008768)  
Mag-a-apply sila online,

**[07:57:02.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008769)

**[07:57:06.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008770)

**[07:57:09.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008771)

**[07:57:11.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008772)

**[07:57:13.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008773)  
Pero if we just project na nandun sila,

**[07:57:16.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008774)

**[07:57:21.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008775)

**[07:57:24.360] DSWD Secretary Rex Gatchalian** (asr-008776)

**[07:57:30.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008777)

**[07:57:32.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008778)  
Kong Mila, being the chairperson of the Committee on Senior Citizens,

**[07:57:38.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008779)

**[07:57:49.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008781)

**[07:58:00.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008783)  
mag-accrue lahat po ng

**[07:58:04.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008785)

**[07:58:30.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008791)

**[07:58:34.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-008792)  
the reversion ng QRF to its fiscal year 2026 level of Php3 billion.
